## st-0177 — Sleeping Beauty (Brier Rose)
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
A man lives in a big house. A woman lives there too. They have a little child. They make a big party. Many old women come to the house. They give good things to her.

One old woman is not there. She is very bad. She says the child die by a point. Another old woman change the rule. The child only rest now.

The man put away all bad points. The girl grow up every day. She walks in the house. She finds a small room. An old person work there. The girl look at the point.

She fall into a long rest. Everyone in the house rest too. Many things stop moving. Plants grow around the house. They are strong and dark.

Many years pass by. Men try to visit the house. The plants are too strong. A young man come to the area.

The plants open up. He walks into the rooms. He finds the girl on her bed. He gives her a love sign. She opens her eyes.

Everyone else open their eyes too. They feel very glad now. The young man and the girl live together.

### A2
Once, a mother and father lived in a big house. They had a little baby girl. They made a great party for the child. Several people came to give things to the family.

However, one old woman was not welcome. She felt very bad about this. She came into the room. She said that the young girl would die from a bad point.

Another person changed this rule. She said the child would only sleep instead of die. The father tried to keep his daughter out of trouble. He broke all bad items in the country.

When the girl grew up, she walked around the house. She found a small room up high. An old person worked there with a bad machine. The girl touched it immediately.

She fell down into a deep sleep upon a bed. Everyone else in the house fell down too. Even the animals stopped moving. A large wall of green plants grew around the building.

Many years passed by. No one entered the house for a long time. Different men tried to visit the area. The green plants were too strong for them.

Finally, a young man came to the place. He heard the story about the sleeping girl. He reached the place, and the plants opened up. He walked inside the quiet rooms.

He found the beautiful girl resting on her bed. He gave her a touch of love. She got up from her long sleep. Everyone else in the house got up too.

They were very happy. The young man and the young girl lived a nice life together.

### B1
Once upon a time, a king and his family lived in a very big house. They had a baby girl and invited many people to a great party. Several good women came to give wonderful gifts to the child. They brought sweet things and nice items for the young baby. The king wanted to make everyone feel very welcome and glad. The big rooms were full of music, food, and smiling talk. Everyone talked about how lovely the little girl was. The party continued for many hours into the evening. People danced and sang songs to celebrate the happy birth. The mother and father were proud of their child. They gave food and drink to every single guest. The event went on until the night was dark. Every person in the house had a wonderful time. They wanted the good life to continue without end.

However, one old woman was not invited. This made her very angry. She came into the room and told everyone a bad fact. She said that the young girl would stop living upon a point. One good woman changed this rule so that the child would rest. The child would rest instead of passing away during those years. The king tried to keep his daughter safe by burning items. He burned every sharp tool in the country. When the girl grew, she walked around the house. She found a small room up top. An old person was working there with a sharp tool. The girl touched the sharp point immediately. As soon as she touched it, she fell down. She fell into a deep rest upon a bed.

Everyone else in the house also fell down during that same moment. Even the horses stopped moving. The birds stopped moving too. A large wall of green plants grew around the building. Many years passed by. A long time went without anyone entering the house. Different men tried to visit the place. The green plants were too strong for them. Finally, a young man came to the area. He came after hearing the story about the resting beauty. When he reached the place, the plants opened up. The plants opened to let him through. He walked inside the quiet rooms. He found the beautiful girl resting on her bed. He gave her a kiss. The kiss woke her up from her long rest.

Everyone else in the house woke up too. They were very happy. The man and the girl lived a wonderful life together. They lived together from that day on. The sun was bright upon the big house. The sun was bright upon the green land around it. People came from far and wide to see the happy family. They brought flowers and gifts to show their love. They brought support for the king and his wife. The king and his wife thanked everyone for kind words. Peace filled the whole country for many years. Every person in the land remembered the story. They remembered the story of the resting beauty.

### B2
Once upon a time, a king and a mother lived in a large castle. They had a baby girl and invited many people to a great party. Several wise women came to give wonderful gifts to the child. However, one old woman was not invited, which made her very angry. She came into the room and said that the young girl would stop breathing when she touched a sharp point. Another wise woman changed this rule so that the child would only rest instead of meeting death. The king tried to keep his daughter safe by burning all such items in the country. When the girl grew up, she walked around the castle and found a small room high up. An old person was working there with a sharp tool, which the young lady touched immediately. As soon as she touched it, she fell down into a deep sleep upon a bed. Everyone else in the house also fell asleep during that same moment. Even the horses and birds stopped moving while a large wall of green plants grew around the building. Many years passed by, and a long time went without anyone entering the castle. Different men tried to visit, but the green plants were too strong for them. Finally, a young man came to the area after hearing the story about the resting beauty. When he reached the place, the plants opened up to let him through. He walked inside the quiet rooms and found the beautiful girl resting on her bed. He gave her a kiss, which woke her up from her long sleep. Everyone else in the castle woke up too, and they were very happy. The young man and the young lady lived a wonderful life together from that day on. The castle was full of light and sound again. People came from all over the land to see the happy family. Every room was cleaned and painted to celebrate the moment. The king looked at his daughter with a big smile. She felt wonderful and walked through the garden every single afternoon. Birds sang songs high above the green grass and the beautiful flowers. No one ever spoke about the dangerous old woman or the sharp point again. Life went on in a very peaceful way for everyone involved. Years passed, and the country remained strong and safe under the king. The young lady grew even more kind and wise as she aged. Many children were born in the area during the following years. Everyone remembered the story of the long sleep and the wonderful awakening. It became a favorite tale told by parents to their little ones. The castle doors remained open to all visitors who wished to enter. Music and laughter filled the air throughout the winter and summer months. Nothing could ever trouble the peace of that wonderful home. The young man and his wife shared a deep love that lasted forever. They walked together along the river and through the forest every morning. The sun shone brightly upon them day after day. Such a happy ending was discussed by people across the whole world. Even today, the tale is remembered with a smile by everyone who hears it. The castle stands tall and proud against the blue sky. Visitors can still see the beautiful rooms where the long sleep happened. Guides tell the story to groups of curious people every single week. The memory of the event lives on in books and songs. Everything about the place feels magical and full of history. Children look at the walls and imagine the green plants growing around them. They ask questions about the brave man who opened the path. The answers are given with joy by the local people. No one can forget how the young lady was saved by a simple kiss. The magic of that day remains a part of the country forever. Life in the castle continued without any further trouble or fear. The king and his family enjoyed every single moment of their time together. Friends from distant towns visited often to share meals and stories. The food was always good and the wine was very sweet. People danced in the large hall until late into the night. Happiness was found in every corner of the big building. The story of the sleeping beauty became a symbol of hope for all. Even when bad things happened in the world, this tale brought comfort. People believed that true love could overcome any difficult problem. The young lady and her husband set a fine example for everyone. Their relationship was built on trust and deep respect. They helped each other through every challenge that came their way. The country grew rich and peaceful under their careful eyes. No war or major disaster ever disturbed their quiet land. Schools were built so that children could learn and grow smart. Markets were busy with people buying and selling fresh food. The streets were clean and safe for all citizens to walk upon. Everyone had enough to eat and a good place to live. The king retired from his heavy duties and rested in the garden. He was proud of the young couple and the work they had done. The mother also spent her days reading books and listening to music. The entire nation lived in harmony and peace for many decades. Such a wonderful outcome was more than anyone could have expected. The magic that had caused so much trouble finally brought great joy. Every person in the land knew the tale by heart. It was passed down from generation to generation without change. The castle remained a famous landmark for hundreds of years. People traveled from far away just to look at its tall towers. They took pictures and walked through the beautiful gardens. The spirit of the place touched the hearts of all visitors. Nobody ever felt sad when they heard the story of the castle. It always brought a warm feeling to the listener's mind. And so the great adventure came to its proper and happy end.

### C1
Once upon a time, within the stone walls of a grand and sprawling castle, a king and a mother celebrated the birth of a long awaited daughter. Seeking to honor the child, they arranged a magnificent feast and invited the realm's most notable figures, including twelve wise women endowed with extraordinary gifts. Each wise woman bestowed upon the infant a singular virtue, weaving a tapestry of beauty, grace, and intellect that promised a charmed existence. The atmosphere was thick with joyous anticipation until the heavy oak doors groaned open to reveal an uninvited guest. She was an elderly woman whose omission had kindled a bitter resentment, and she stepped into the hall with a dark cloud of malice hanging over her.

Without offering a single pleasantry, the uninvited woman pronounced a terrible curse that overshadowed all the preceding blessings. She declared that before the princess reached her sixteenth year, she would brush against a sharp point and immediately cease breathing. The assembled guests gasped in horror, their bright laughter instantly silenced by the heavy shadow of impending doom. Fortunately, one wise woman had not yet bestowed her gift, and she stepped forward to soften the harsh decree. Though she lacked the power to completely undo the malicious spell, she altered its ultimate consequence so that the princess would merely fall into a profound slumber rather than perish.

Determined to thwart the prophecy, the frantic king issued an immediate decree ordering the destruction of every sharp instrument throughout the entire realm. Every spindle, needle, and pointed tool was gathered from the cottages and palaces alike, reduced to ashes in roaring courtyard fires. Meanwhile, the young princess blossomed into a remarkably kind and intelligent maiden, entirely unaware of the dark fate that shadowed her steps. On the very day she turned sixteen, the king and mother happened to be away, leaving the curious girl to explore the castle corridors alone. She wandered through unfamiliar wings, her fingers trailing along ancient stone walls until she discovered a narrow, winding staircase leading up into a forgotten tower.

Reaching the top, she found a small wooden door with a rusty key turning sluggishly in the lock. Pushing it open, she stepped into a dusty chamber where an elderly person sat beside a spinning wheel, working diligently with a sharp metal tool. Fascinated by the strange device, the young lady stepped closer and asked what the spinning tool was meant to do. Before she could fully comprehend the situation, her hand brushed against the sharp tip. At that exact instant, the heavy weight of exhaustion claimed her, and she collapsed onto a bed of roses, falling into a deep and immovable sleep.

That very second, a profound slumber swept through the entire castle, binding every living creature in its invisible spell. The king and mother, having just returned, fell fast asleep in the great hall, while the courtiers drifted off mid-sentence. In the stables, the horses slumped against their stalls, and the birds suspended in flight froze against the grey sky. A great wall of thick green plants began to sprout around the outer ramparts, rising rapidly until it completely obscured the towers from view. Years drifted by in absolute silence, and the once bustling fortress became nothing more than a whispered legend among the local villagers.

Decades passed, and many adventurous travelers attempted to breach the formidable barrier of green branches, but the thorny wall proved impassable. Many young men lost their way or were trapped by the thick vines, unable to unlock the secret of the hidden castle. Finally, a courageous young man arrived in the region after hearing distant tales of a sleeping beauty resting behind the fortress walls. As he approached the perimeter, the century mark of the sleeping curse reached its completion. To his astonishment, the thick green plants parted willingly before him, creating a clear pathway into the quiet courtyard.

He stepped across the threshold and walked through the silent halls, observing the motionless guards and the quiet animals frozen in time. Reaching the upper tower at last, he pushed open the door to the small room and beheld the princess lying in eternal repose. Her beauty took his breath away, and he knelt beside the bed, overcome by a strange and sudden affection. He gently pressed his lips to her forehead, breaking the ancient spell with a simple act of devotion. The moment his lips touched hers, the princess fluttered her eyelashes, opened her bright eyes, and gazed up at him with a warm smile.

Immediately, the entire castle surged back to life as the lingering enchantment dissolved into the morning air. The horses shook their manes in the stables, the courtiers resumed their interrupted conversations, and the birds soared once more across the sky. Joy flooded every corridor as the king and mother rushed into the room, embracing their daughter with tears of pure relief. Laughter and music echoed through the great hall, banishing the shadows of the past hundred years. From that day forward, the young man and the princess shared a deep and enduring love that guided them through every season.

The realm entered a golden era of peace and prosperity under the careful attention of the young couple. Schools were established, bustling markets filled the streets with fresh food, and every citizen lived in comfort and safety. The king retired to the sunlit gardens, spending his remaining years in quiet contentment surrounded by blooming flowers and singing birds. People traveled from distant towns just to witness the legendary castle and hear the story of the great awakening. No one ever spoke of the dark curse or the old woman again, for the memory of fear had been entirely replaced by enduring hope.

Generations passed, yet the tale of the sleeping beauty remained a favorite bedtime story whispered by parents to their children. The castle stood tall and proud against the blue sky, serving as a timeless symbol that true devotion could overcome any hardship. Visitors walked through the very rooms where the long sleep had occurred, marveling at the preserved history and the magic of the place. The young man and his wife walked hand in hand along the forest paths every morning, their bond growing stronger with each passing year. Nothing could ever disturb the profound harmony of their home, and their story lived on in the hearts of everyone who heard it.

## st-0178 — Beauty and the Beast
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
A man lives in a town. He has children. Bad business comes. They lose money. They move to a small house. The family members talk much. Beauty works hard every day. She helps her father a lot. She is a good child. She has a big love inside. Life is hard for them now.

A large company comes to the town. The father asks for presents. Other girls want good things. Beauty wants a small color plant. She likes the little color plant. The father goes away from home. He looks for business. The goods are gone. The father is in bad air. He sees a big house. Food is on a table. He eats and stays there.

He sees a color plant. He takes the small color plant. A big beast appears. The beast is very bad. The father explains the problem. The beast wants a child. Beauty goes to the big house. The beast is kind to her. Beauty feels true love. She sees her bad father. She wants to visit him. The beast lets her go home.

She stays long at home. She remembers the big beast. She runs back to the house. She finds the big beast. She makes noise every minute. She loves the beast. The big beast becomes a man. He is a young leader. They are very good together. They live in the big house. Love is good for them. They stay together for life.

### A2
Once upon a time, a man lived in a big town. He had lovely children. Bad business hit the family. They lost money and move to a small house far away. The sisters talk about the new life. Beauty, the youngest daughter, work hard and always smile.

One day, the father hear about the market. His goods arrive at the harbor. Before he leave, he ask about presents. The older sisters want nice clothes and things. Beauty only ask for a simple red plant.

The man reach the market. His goods are gone. He has nothing. During a dark and cold trip home, he lose his way. Deep white water was near him. Suddenly, he see a large house. It was light with many things. A big table was full of food.

The man enter the room. He eats a nice dinner and sleep in a bed. The next morning, he walk outside. He sees a beautiful garden and pick a red plant for Beauty. Immediately, a bad thing appear. A person speak to him. He says that he will kill the man for taking property.

The poor father explain the plant was a present for his daughter. The big thing agree to let him go. One daughter must come to live there. The father return home. Beauty decide to help and travel to the unusual house.

The individual look bad. However, he treat Beauty with great care. He gives her a lovely room. Every evening, they talk together. Beauty begin to feel deep love for the thing. She sees in a room that her father was not well. She asks to visit him.

The thing allow her to go home for a short time. She promise to return soon. Her family was happy to see her. Her stay become too long. She enjoy being with them. Suddenly, she remember the thing. She runs back to the house because she was worried.

She finds the poor individual down in the garden. She speaks while she hold him closely. Beauty tell the thing that she love him. Her love break the dark system. The thing change into a fine man. They live happy together.

### B1
Once upon a time, a rich man lived in a big town with his lovely children. Bad business hit the family. They lost all money. They moved into a small house far away. The sisters complained about their new life. The youngest daughter named Beauty worked hard. She always smiled at everyone in the house.

One day, the father heard good news. A ship with his goods had finally arrived at the edge. Before he left, he asked his children a question. He asked what kind of present they wanted him to bring back. The older sisters requested expensive clothes and gold. Beauty only asked for a simple red flower.

The man reached the place. He found that his goods were gone. He had nothing left. During his dark and cold journey home through the forest, he lost his way. He walked in deep snow. Suddenly, he saw a large house. It was bright with lights. The table was full of food.

The man entered the warm building. He ate a nice meal. He slept in a comfortable bed. The next morning, as he walked outside, he saw a beautiful garden. He picked a red flower for Beauty. Immediately, a terrible animal appeared. The animal shouted that he would kill the man for stealing property.

The worried father explained the situation. He said the flower was only a small gift for his youngest daughter. The big animal agreed to let him go. He said one of his daughters must come to live in the house. The man returned home. Beauty decided to give herself up. She traveled to the strange house.

Although the animal looked bad, he treated Beauty with great care. He gave her a lovely room. Every evening they talked together. Beauty began to feel deep love for the strange animal. However, she saw in a special glass that her father was sick. She asked to visit him.

The animal allowed her to go home for a short time. She had to promise that she would return soon. Her family was extremely happy to see her. Her stay became too long because she enjoyed being with them. Suddenly, she remembered the animal. She rushed back to the house because she was afraid that he was dead.

She found the poor animal resting still in the garden. She cried while she held him closely. Beauty told the animal that she loved him. Her love broke the dark spell. The animal immediately changed into a fine man. They lived happily together for all time.

### B2
Once upon a time, a rich man lived in a big town with his lovely children. After bad business hit the family, they lost their money and moved into a small house far away. While the sisters complained about their new life, the youngest daughter named Beauty worked hard and always smiled. She was very good to everyone in the family. Every single day, she helped her father and sisters with many different tasks around the small house. The family had very little money left, but Beauty always tried to keep everyone happy and full of belief about the future. Her older sisters often sat around and cried about their lost wealth, but Beauty never did that. Instead, she cooked meals, cleaned the rooms, and made sure that her father felt loved. The father often looked at his youngest daughter with great pride and deep feeling. He knew that life would be much harder without her gentle help and constant smile. They spent many quiet evenings together by the small fire, talking about better times. Although their clothes were old and simple, they still shared a strong family bond that money could never buy.

One day, the father heard that a ship with his goods had finally arrived at the port. Before he left, he asked his children what kind of present they wanted him to bring back. The older sisters requested expensive clothes and gold, but Beauty only asked for a simple red flower. She did not care about gold or fine dresses because she was happy with very little. The father promised that he would search everywhere for that special flower. He told his daughters farewell and walked down the road toward the busy port. The weather was cold, and the wind blew hard against his face as he traveled. He hoped that the news about his ship was true so that he could buy new things for his family. He wanted to give his children a better life and take away their daily worries. The journey was long, and he felt quite tired by the time he reached the large town by the sea. He walked quickly through the busy streets until he finally arrived at the harbor area. Ships came and went all day, carrying goods from distant countries across the deep blue water.

When the man reached the port, he found that his goods were gone and he had nothing. During his dark and cold journey home through the forest, he lost his way in deep snow. Suddenly, he saw a large castle which was bright with lights and full of food on a big table. The man entered the warm building, ate a nice meal, and slept in a comfortable bed. The next morning, as he walked outside, he saw a beautiful garden and picked a red flower for Beauty. Immediately, a terrible creature appeared, shouting that he would kill the man for stealing his property. The huge animal looked very angry, and his loud voice shook the ground beneath the man feet. The frightened father explained that the flower was only a small gift for his youngest daughter. The strange creature agreed to let him go if one of his daughters came to live in the castle freely. The poor father felt very sad, but he knew that he had no other choice to see his family again. He made the promise and quickly ran away from the dark place as fast as he could. When the merchant returned home, Beauty decided to leave her life there and travel to the strange castle to save her father.

Although the creature looked frightening, he treated Beauty with great care and gave her a lovely room. Every evening they talked together, and Beauty began to feel deep love for the strange animal. However, after she saw in a special glass that her father was sick, she asked to visit him. She told the master of the castle that she missed her family very much and needed to see her father. The creature allowed her to go home for a short time, but she had to promise that she would return soon. Her family was extremely happy to see her, but her stay became too long because she enjoyed being with them. Suddenly, she remembered the creature, and she rushed back to the castle because she was afraid that he was dead. She ran through the dark forest and entered the castle gardens as fast as possible. The wind blew cold around her, and her heart beat fast with fear and worry. She searched every corner of the large garden, calling out his name into the cold night air. The place was completely quiet, and no one answered her calls for a long time. She feared that her long delay had caused him too much pain while she was away from the castle.

She found the poor creature down in the garden, and she cried while she held him closely. Beauty told the creature that she loved him, and her love broke the dark spell. The animal immediately changed into a young prince, and they lived happily together forever. The prince thanked Beauty for her great love and deep care during such a difficult time. He explained how a bad spell had kept him in that terrible shape until someone could love him despite his looks. The old castle transformed into a bright and happy place filled with light and music. Soon after that happy event, Beauty brought her father and sisters to live with them in the grand house. The family never had to worry about money or food ever again. The sisters learned to be kind and helpful, just like Beauty had always been. They spent their days walking in the beautiful gardens and sharing wonderful meals together. The prince and Beauty ruled the land with wisdom, love, and fairness for the rest of their lives. Everyone in the country celebrated their happy union and remembered the story for many years.

### C1
Once upon a time, a wealthy merchant resided in a sprawling metropolis, surrounded by his cherished children and the opulence of his successful trade. Unforeseen economic ruin struck the family mercilessly, stripping away their vast fortune and forcing a relocation to a modest, remote cottage far from civilization. While the older sisters surrendered to perpetual lamentations regarding their fallen status, the youngest daughter, known simply as Beauty, embraced her strenuous new reality with quiet grace. Not a single day passed without Beauty tending tirelessly to the household chores, offering an unwavering smile that brought warmth to their humble dwelling. She cooked, cleaned, and comforted her brooding father, ensuring that affection remained the true currency of their impoverished household.

Her father often gazed upon his youngest child with profound admiration, acutely aware that their fractured lives would utterly collapse without her gentle resilience. During long evenings spent beside a flickering fire, they reminisced about past splendors while finding solace in each other's unbroken company. Though their garments were coarse and frayed, they shared a profound familial bond that no amount of lost wealth could ever replace. Fate intervened one bleak morning when word reached the merchant that a merchant vessel bearing his long-lost cargo had finally docked at a distant port. Before embarking on the arduous journey, he gathered his children to ask what tokens of affection they desired him to secure upon his return.

While the greedy sisters clamored for costly silks, jewels, and gold, Beauty requested nothing more elaborate than a simple red rose. She harbored no desire for material extravagance, content instead with the quiet beauty of nature and the comfort of her family. Her father promised faithfully to scour the marketplaces for that singular blossom before bidding his daughters a tender farewell. Stepping out into the biting wind, he felt the chill of the winter air lash against his weathered face as he set off down the muddy road. He prayed fervently that the rumors of his recovered cargo were true, desperate to restore financial security and spare his children further hardship.

The journey proved exhausting, testing the limits of his aging body as he traversed miles of rugged terrain toward the bustling coastal harbor. Upon arrival, however, crushing disappointment awaited him; his cargo had been entirely seized by creditors, leaving him utterly destitute. Left with empty pockets and a heavy heart, he began the long, solitary trek back through a dense forest as heavy winter snow began to fall. Blindfolded by swirling blizzards, he lost his bearings completely until a sudden break in the trees revealed a massive, illuminated castle glowing warmly against the dark woods. Drawn by the miraculous sight, he pushed open the heavy oak doors to find a lavish feast laid out upon a magnificent table, entirely unattended.

Overwhelmed by fatigue and hunger, the weary traveler partook of the magnificent meal before seeking refuge in a sumptuously prepared bedchamber. The following morning, as he wandered through the castle courtyards, his eyes caught sight of a breathtaking garden overflowing with vibrant flora. Remembering his promise, he snapped a single red rose from its stem, intending to present it to Beauty upon his reunion. Scarcely had the flower left the bush when a terrifying beast materialized before him, roaring with a fury that shook the very stones beneath his feet. The monstrous creature accused the trembling merchant of ungrateful theft, declaring that the penalty for such audacity must surely be death.

Groveling in terror, the desperate father explained that the plucked blossom was merely a humble token requested by his youngest daughter. The beast considered the plea before offering a harsh ultimatum: the merchant's life would be spared only if one of his daughters willingly surrendered to imprisonment in the castle. Knowing the impossibility of his choice, the heartbroken father accepted the cruel terms and fled the terrifying domain as swiftly as his legs could carry him. When he finally arrived home and recounted the harrowing tale, Beauty made an immediate and resolute decision to sacrifice her own freedom to save her father. She packed a modest bag and journeyed bravely into the forbidding wilderness, determined to fulfill the terrifying bargain.

Upon her arrival at the enchanted castle, the fearsome creature welcomed her not as a prisoner, but with surprising gentleness and respect. He assigned her a magnificent chamber filled with books and music, ensuring her comfort while granting her total autonomy within the sprawling estate. Every evening at dusk, they dined together, engaging in deep conversations that gradually dissolved Beauty's initial terror and replaced it with genuine affection. Despite her growing contentment, a heavy shadow fell over her heart when a magical mirror revealed that her beloved father had fallen gravely ill. She pleaded with the master of the castle for permission to visit her ailing family, promising upon her honor to return within a strictly limited timeframe.

The compassionate beast granted her request, though his sorrow was palpable as he watched her depart for the familiar embrace of her cottage. Her family rejoiced at her return, showering her with affection and making it agonizingly difficult for her to tear herself away. Blinded by the comfort of home, Beauty lost track of time, lingering far beyond the agreed-upon date until a sudden, chilling premonition struck her soul. Panic surged through her veins as she remembered the lonely beast, terrified that her careless delay might have cost him his very life. Without a moment's hesitation, she bolted out the door and raced desperately back through the dark, menacing forest toward the castle.

Arriving breathless at the gates, she rushed into the moonlit gardens, calling out his name into the chilling night air with mounting hysteria. Only silence answered her frantic appeals, amplifying the suffocating dread that gripped her chest as she searched every shadowy corner of the estate. At last, she discovered the lifeless form of the massive creature sprawled near the rose bushes, motionless upon the damp ground. Sobbing uncontrollably, she collapsed beside him, pressing her face against his fur and whispering that she loved him with all her heart. Scarcely had the confession escaped her lips when a blinding flash of light enveloped the garden, breaking the ancient curse that had bound him for ages.

To her utter astonishment, the monstrous beast melted away, replaced by a handsome prince whose grateful eyes locked onto hers with profound devotion. He explained how a wicked witch had condemned him to wander in monstrous form until a pure soul could love him despite his hideous appearance. The gloomy castle miraculously transformed, shedding its dark stone exterior to reveal a radiant palace echoing with joyous music and celebration. Not long after, Beauty brought her father and humbled sisters to reside within the grand estate, ensuring their financial security for the rest of their days. The reformed sisters learned the true value of humility and kindness, joining the royal family in peaceful harmony.

Years passed, but the kingdom continued to flourish under the wise and benevolent rule of the prince and his devoted queen. Every corner of the realm celebrated their enduring union, whispering tales of how true love had conquered darkness and transformed despair into eternal joy.

## st-0179 — The Tale of Jemima Puddle-Duck
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1

A big dog sees Jemima. The dog knows the man is bad. He runs to find two friends. They make a plan to help Jemima. They come to the house. The man tries to send them away. The dogs are strong. They run the bad man into the trees. Jemima sees everything.

The strong dogs bring Jemima back home. She learns a thing about men. She tries to make little ones again. She always stays close to the place. She feels well at home now.

### B1
Jemima was a good bird who lived on a farm with many other animals. She felt very sad because the farmer wife always took her eggs away every single morning. Jemima wanted to bring her own family in a quiet place without any trouble. She decided to look for a safe home far outside the land. She had lived on the farm for a long time. The farmer also did not know about her secret plan.

One fine morning, she walked into the forest. She met a good gentleman with long red hair. The gentleman spoke in a very nice voice and asked where she was going. Jemima told him about her problem with the eggs and the farmer wife. The friendly animal listened carefully and offered her a lovely house for her home. The gentleman listened to her story with great attention. He smiled at her and promised a lovely house in the woods. Jemima felt that he was a very kind person.

He asked her to stay in an old box at his house deep in the woods. Jemima was extremely glad because the place seemed very quiet and comfortable for her eggs. She carried several green things and soft hair to make a nice bed inside. The gentleman told her to bring plenty of fine food for the coming days. She had accepted his offer without any wait. She had brought many soft things to make her new bed warm. If she stayed in the old box, her eggs would be safe. The gentleman had prepared the box for her coming. Jemima was extremely glad because the place seemed very quiet. She brought green things and soft hair to build a comfortable bed. The gentleman told her to bring plenty of food. She felt that nothing could go wrong now. Her new home looked better than the old farm.

However, a good farm dog saw Jemima talking to the visitor near the trees. The dog knew that the red gentleman was dangerous and could not be trusted at all. He immediately ran to find two friendly country dogs to help save the poor bird. They quickly made a plan to follow Jemima and protect her from the bad plan. The farm dog had watched Jemima from behind the green plants. He had realized that the red gentleman was very dangerous. If he did not act quickly, something terrible would happen. He ran back to the farm to find his friends. The two friendly dogs agreed to help him right away. They made a plan to protect the poor bird. They knew the woods were full of danger. They ran back toward the forest to find Jemima. Their quick action would save her from a big mistake.

When they got to the forest house, the good dog asked about the missing bird. The nice gentleman tried to send them away, but the dogs were too strong and fast. They rushed inside the room. They drove the bad animal far away into the dark woods. Jemima was very surprised. She finally understood that her safe home was actually a bad plan. The dogs had come to the forest house before evening came. They had asked the red man about the missing bird. If the man refused to answer, the dogs would push past him. The gentleman tried to send them away from his house. The dogs were too strong and fast for him. They rushed inside the dark room. They drove the bad man far away into the trees. Jemima watched everything with a surprised face. She finally understood that her new home was a bad plan.

The good dogs brought the sad bird back home to the farm before night came. Jemima learned a valuable lesson about listening to people who promise wonderful things. She tried to bring eggs again later, but she always stayed close to the farm. The dogs had walked with Jemima all the way back to the farm. They had arrived before the sun went down. If she ever met anyone new again, she would be very careful. Jemima learned a valuable lesson about listening to people. She tried to bring eggs again later in the season. She always stayed close to the farm where she belonged. She never forgot the danger in the deep woods. Her life on the farm went back to normal. She was happy to be safe with the other animals again.

### B2
Jemima Puddle was a good bird who lived on a large farm with many other animals. She felt very sad because the farmer's wife always took her eggs away every single morning. Jemima wanted to bring her own family in a quiet place without any trouble. She decided to look for a safe home far outside the land. One fine morning, she walked into the forest and met a smart gentleman with long red hair. The gentleman spoke in a very quiet voice and asked where she was going. Jemima told him about her problem with the eggs and the farmer's wife. The good animal listened carefully and offered her a lovely house for her home. He invited her to stay in an old wooden box at his house deep in the woods. Jemima was extremely glad because the place seemed very quiet and comfortable for her eggs. She carried several green things and soft hair to make a nice bed inside. The gentleman told her to bring plenty of fine food for the next days. However, a smart farm dog saw Jemima talking to the strange visitor near the trees. The dog knew that the red gentleman was dangerous and could not be trusted at all. He immediately ran to find two friendly country friends to help save the silly animal. They quickly made a plan to follow Jemima and protect her from the trick. When they arrived at the forest house, the good dog asked about the missing bird. The gentleman tried to send them away, but the dogs were too strong and fast. They rushed inside the wooden room and chased the bad animal far away into the dark woods. Jemima was very surprised, but she finally understood that her safe home was actually a trap. The dogs brought the sad bird back home to the farm before night came. Jemima learned a valuable lesson about listening to strangers who promise wonderful things. She tried to bring eggs again later, but she always stayed close to the farm where she belonged.

Many days passed after that adventure in the deep forest. Jemima stayed near the farm house and walked around the green grass every afternoon. She saw the farmer and his wife working in the large field. Sometimes she talked with the other birds about the strange gentleman in the woods. The dogs watched her carefully to ensure she never made the same mistake again. Jemima understood that the farm was the best place for her life. She did not want to leave the safety of the buildings and the friendly animals. The sun shone brightly over the green hills during the long summer days. Jemima found small pieces of food near the big barn and ate them with joy. She felt peaceful while sitting under the shade of the tall trees. The other creatures on the farm welcomed her back and shared their daily meals with her. Life went on slowly and quietly for the bird who learned to trust her true friends. She never forgot the warning of the faithful farm dog and the two country friends. Whenever she felt tired, she walked back to her small corner near the main house. The farmer's wife continued to take some eggs, but Jemima was no longer looking for danger. She realized that a simple life at home was much better than any secret adventure in the dark woods. Every morning brought new light and peace to the farmyard where she lived. She spent her time swimming in the small pond and enjoying the company of the other animals. The story of her adventure became a famous tale among all the creatures living on the land. They often talked about how the brave dogs saved her from the wicked trick of the red gentleman. Jemima listened to these conversations with a quiet mind and a thankful heart. She knew that safety was more important than any promise made by a stranger. Thus, she lived happily for many years, always remembering the lesson of the forest house.

The seasons changed slowly from summer to autumn and then to cold winter days. Jemima stayed warm inside the wooden building where the farmer kept the other animals. She shared her space with cows and horses while the snow fell heavily outside. The friendly dogs often visited her to check if everything was fine. Jemima greeted them with a soft sound and walked a few steps closer to show her friendship. She appreciated the protection that the dogs provided against any possible danger. When spring finally returned, the green grass grew again and the flowers opened their petals. Jemima walked out into the bright morning light and breathed the fresh air. She looked at the tall trees in the distance and remembered the dark woods. A small shiver ran through her body, but she quickly forgot the bad memory. She was happy to be alive and well in her familiar home. The farmer noticed the bird and smiled while walking past the old barn. He knew that Jemima was a special part of the farm family now. She lived there for a long time, enjoying every single moment without any fear. The tale of the brave dogs and the silly bird was told again and again to every new animal that arrived. No one on the farm ever forgot the day when the red gentleman was chased away. Jemima Puddle continued her quiet life, swimming in the water and walking under the sun. She was a wise bird now, and her days were filled with peace and simple joys.

### C1
Jemima Puddle-Duck was a remarkably well-intentioned bird who resided upon a bustling, sprawling farm amongst a multitude of feathered and four-legged companions. Despite the daily comforts of the farmyard, a persistent sorrow weighed heavily upon her sensitive spirit. Every single morning, without fail, the farmer's wife confiscated her freshly laid eggs, leaving her nest woefully bare and devoid of maternal promise. Jemima desperately longed to establish her own family within a tranquil sanctuary far removed from such daily interference. With a resolute spirit, she determined to seek out a secluded, secure domestic haven far beyond the familiar boundaries of her home.

One particularly fine and luminous morning, she ventured boldly past the pasture and stepped into the dense, whispering shadow of the forest. It was there that she encountered an exceptionally urbane gentleman possessing remarkably long, russet-colored hair and a most persuasive demeanor. The stranger addressed her in a low, velvety tone, inquiring delicately as to the destination of her solitary morning journey. Impelled by a mixture of loneliness and grievance, Jemima unburdened her soul, confiding her ongoing domestic frustrations regarding the farmer's wife. The suave creature listened with an appearance of profound empathy, nodding understandingly at every complaint about the stolen eggs.

Capitalizing on her vulnerability, the gentleman offered her a charming, secluded habitation to serve as her permanent nursery. He cordially invited her to take up residence within a spacious, weathered wooden box situated deep within the labyrinthine thickets of the woods. Elated beyond measure, Jemima decided that the location possessed the absolute quiet and comfortable seclusion she had dreamed of. She immediately set to work gathering various damp green fronds and soft tufts of sheep wool, meticulously constructing an inviting nest inside the box. Before parting ways that afternoon, her new acquaintance instructed her to bring a generous supply of fine culinary herbs and grains for the coming days.

However, not all eyes on the farm had been blind to her clandestine interactions near the timberline. A remarkably astute farm dog had observed Jemima conversing intently with the suspicious, red-haired visitor among the trees. Knowing the true nature of the forest dweller, the dog instantly recognized that the gentleman was utterly untrustworthy and posed a grave danger. Without a moment of hesitation, he sprinted across the fields to enlist the assistance of two rugged country spaniels. Together, the trio rapidly formulated a strategic plan to track Jemima's movements and rescue the naive bird from an impending disaster.

When they finally arrived at the hidden woodland dwelling, the resolute dog boldly demanded the immediate surrender of the missing bird. The gentleman attempted to deflect their inquiries with practiced charm, trying desperately to send the determined intruders away. Yet, the dogs proved far too formidable and swift to be deterred by empty pleasantries or polite excuses. Charging past the threshold, they burst into the wooden chamber and pursued the cowardly trickster far into the darkening depths of the forest. Jemima stood frozen in absolute bewilderment, slowly coming to the agonizing realization that her supposed sanctuary was nothing more than a carefully baited trap.

Without affording her time to recover from the shock, the dogs gently but firmly escorted the chastened bird back toward the safety of the farm. They reached the perimeter of the buildings just as the twilight began to cast long shadows across the green grass. Jemima absorbed a profound and unforgettable lesson regarding the inherent perils of trusting strangers who offer miraculous promises. Though she occasionally contemplated laying another clutch of eggs, she henceforth resolved to remain strictly within the protective boundaries of the farm. She understood at last that her true home lay among the familiar barns and trusted companions who truly cared for her well-being.

Many peaceful days drifted by in the wake of her near-disastrous adventure in the dense forest. Jemima spent her afternoons strolling leisurely across the sunlit pastures, keeping within sight of the farmhouse and the bustling barnyard. Occasionally, she recounted her harrowing experience to the other poultry, warning them of the charming stranger who lurked beneath the trees. The watchful farm dogs kept a benevolent yet vigilant eye on her movements, determined that she should never repeat her foolish mistake. Surrounded by the comforting rhythm of rural life, Jemima finally felt a deep sense of contentment settle over her daily routine.

The long summer days dissolved into the golden hues of autumn, and the seasons turned once more toward the biting chill of winter. Jemima found snug refuge inside the spacious timber stable where the farmer sheltered the livestock against the biting frost. She shared her modest space with docile cows and sturdy horses while heavy snow blanketed the peaceful landscape outside. Whenever the faithful farm dogs trotted past her enclosure, Jemima would offer a soft, appreciative greeting and step closer to show her enduring gratitude. She cherished the security they provided, recognizing that true safety was infinitely more valuable than any secluded woodland paradise.

When spring eventually returned to the valley, melting the snow and coaxing delicate wildflowers from the damp earth, Jemima stepped out into the invigorating morning air. She glanced toward the distant, shadowy treeline of the forest and felt a momentary shiver pass through her feathers. Yet, that lingering unease quickly vanished as she embraced the vibrant life of the farmyard once more. The farmer noticed the plump duck waddling near the pond and smiled warmly at the familiar sight. She lived on that expansive farm for many long and joyful years, her days filled with simple pleasures and the comforting presence of true friends.

The dramatic tale of the brave dogs and the gullible duck became a cherished piece of farmyard lore. Older animals recounted the legendary rescue to every new arrival, ensuring that the cautionary tale would never be forgotten. Jemima listened to these familiar retellings with a serene mind and a deeply thankful heart. She knew beyond a shadow of a doubt that genuine security mattered far more than any enticing promise made by a stranger. Thus, she spent the remainder of her days swimming serenely upon the glassy surface of the farm pond, completely at peace with the world.

## st-0180 — The Tale of Squirrel Nutkin
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Once, a group live in a forest. They play together under the big tree. The day is very good. Nutkin is a small person. He has much life. He do not show good mind to anyone.

One morning, the group go across water. They make small things. They use little bits. They come to a land. They get food for the year. Nutkin do not work.

Nutkin play and make music. Old Brown is an old person. He lives on the land. Old Brown do not like music. Nutkin run to Old Brown. He plays with the old person.

Nutkin bring little things every day. He asks small questions. Old Brown sit down. He never give an answer. Nutkin play and look happy. He thinks Old Brown is low.

Nutkin continue the game. He plays too close. He says bad things. The other people watch with care. Old Brown remain very still.

On the last day, Nutkin go too far. He sits on a place. He takes a part. Old Brown become very big. He holds the small person. Nutkin try to run away.

Old Brown give a part back. Nutkin break free. He runs away. He lose his long hair. He is very bad. He never play with anyone again.

The group stay on the land. They work every day. They find good food. The year is long. They live well together. Nutkin stay away now. He thinks about the day. He learns a big thing.

### A2
Once upon a time, little animals lived near a big water. They had fine days playing together under the trees. A young animal named Nutkin lived among them. Nutkin had much energy, but he never showed respect. One morning, the animals made a trip across the water. They went to a place in small water boats. They began to take food for the coming months. However, Nutkin did not want to work. He wanted to play all day long.

Nutkin moved from tree part to tree part and sang songs. An old animal named Old Brown lived on that land. Old Brown was a common animal of the air. He did not like sound or bad behavior. Nutkin ran to the tree of Old Brown. He began to play around the old animal. Nutkin brought little things like food each day. He asked the old animal a question every time. Old Brown sat and never answered. Nutkin danced and laughed around the tree.

Day after day, Nutkin continued this game. He became even more sure of himself. He played too close and said bad things. The other animals watched with worry. They knew trouble was near. Old Brown remained fine. Finally, on the last day, Nutkin went too far. He sat right on top of the seat. He pulled the back part of the old animal. That action made Old Brown very sad.

He caught the little animal fast. Nutkin tried to run away. The strong animal held him hard. Old Brown gave the person a big moment of learning. Nutkin broke free and ran away fast. He lost his beautiful back part during the run. That made him very bad and silent. From that day on, Nutkin never played that game again. He learned to show good respect.

### B1
Once upon a time, little animals lived in a forest near a big river. They played together under tall trees and enjoyed the warm weather every day. Nutkin was a small animal full of energy and life. He never showed respect to anyone in the forest. He had always jumped around and played games with his friends. If he works today, he will help his group. The other animals knew his character well, but they tried to keep calm. Nutkin always laughed and sang songs instead of doing any real work. The sun gave light brightly over the green land while the forest animals played. They lived a simple life and shared food during the spring and summer seasons.

One bright morning, the animals made a long journey across the water to an island. They made small boats from old tree parts and dried wood. They began to collect food for the cold winter season. Nutkin did not want to help or work with the others. Instead of working, Nutkin jumped from branch to branch and sang songs. An old bird named Old Brown lived alone on that island. Old Brown did not like noise or trouble during his day. Nutkin ran to the large tree and began to bother the old bird. If Nutkin makes a noise, Old Brown will become very angry.

Nutkin brought gifts like food and small food items to Old Brown each day. He asked the old bird a question every single time he came close. Old Brown sat quietly on his branch and never answered the strange questions. Nutkin danced around the tree and laughed at the quiet bird. Nutkin continued this game and felt very sure of himself. He played close to Old Brown and said bad things about the bird. The other animals watched with deep worry because danger was near. Old Brown remained calm during the day and ignored the animal. Nutkin had thought the bird would never respond to his funny games.

On the last day of the trip, Nutkin went too far. He sat on top of Old Brown and pulled his back part. Old Brown became very angry and caught the small animal fast. Nutkin tried to run away, but the strong bird held him fast. Old Brown did not hurt him, but taught him a big lesson. Nutkin finally broke free and ran away fast through the trees. He lost his beautiful back part during the quick escape. He never bothered anyone again after that strange day. If he had not pulled the bird, he would have kept his back part safe.

### B2
Once upon a time, a group of little animals lived in a beautiful forest near a big river. They had many fine days playing together under the trees and enjoying the warm weather. Among them, there was one very wild animal whose name was Nutkin. Nutkin was full of energy, but he never showed respect to anyone in the forest. Every single day, Nutkin found new ways to make other people feel bad. His parents always told him that bad behavior would bring great trouble later in life. However, Nutkin did not listen to any advice because he thought he knew everything. He believed that he could do whatever he wanted without any negative results. The forest was quiet, but Nutkin often made loud noises that disturbed the peace. Other animals tried to talk to him, but he just laughed and ran away quickly. Life in the forest continued like this while the warm season slowly changed into autumn. If he had listened to his parents, he would not have caused so much trouble. The animals that lived near the river were often upset by his loud behavior. He always assumed that nothing bad could ever happen to someone like him. Every single member of the community hoped that he would change his mind soon. However, the young animal continued to act without any care for the rest of the group. He believed that his wild actions were completely fine and normal.

One autumn morning, all the little animals decided to make a journey across the water to an island. They made small boats from old tree parts and used little sticks for sails. When they arrived at the island, they began to collect food for the coming winter. However, Nutkin did not want to work like the other animals. Instead of working, Nutkin jumped from branch to branch and sang loud songs. An old bird lived on that island, and his name was Old Brown. Old Brown was a quiet bird who did not like noise or bad behavior. Nutkin ran straight to the tree where Old Brown lived and began to bother him without fear. Nutkin brought little gifts like food items and small pieces of wood to Old Brown each day. Every time he gave a gift, he asked the old bird a silly question. Old Brown sat quietly in his house and never answered any of the questions. Nutkin danced around the tree and laughed because he thought the old bird was too slow. Small boats were used by the group to cross the river. Food that was needed for the winter was collected by the busy animals on the island. The group spent many hours working while Nutkin played around the trees. Nothing could stop the little animal from making noise all day long. The other animals watched him with deep concern as the days passed quickly.

Day after day, Nutkin continued this game and became even more confident. He played too close to Old Brown and said things that were quite bad. The other animals watched him with worry because they knew that danger was near. Old Brown remained calm while the little animal made fun of him. Many days passed like this while the visitors collected food on the island. Nutkin thought that Old Brown would never do anything to stop him. He spent his hours jumping around and shouting without any care in the world. The other animals often talked about how dangerous Nutkin's actions really were. They hoped that nothing terrible would happen before the end of their visit. Every warning was ignored by the silly animal who only cared about his own fun. If Old Brown had wanted to attack, he could have caught the silly animal much earlier. The words that were spoken by Nutkin were not nice at all. Everyone watched while the little animal continued his silly games every single afternoon. The visitors often wondered how long Old Brown could stay calm. They knew that every action had a result and that trouble would come soon. Still, Nutkin did not care about any of their warnings or advice.

Finally, on the last day of their visit, Nutkin went too far. He sat right on top of Old Brown's chair and pulled the old bird's tail. That action made Old Brown very angry, and he caught the little animal fast. Nutkin tried to run away, but the strong bird held him tight. Old Brown did not hurt him, but he taught the proud animal a hard lesson. After a long struggle, Nutkin broke free and ran away as fast as he could. He lost his beautiful tail during the escape, which made him very sad and quiet. From that day on, Nutkin never bothered anyone again and learned to show proper respect. The other animals were surprised to see how much Nutkin had changed. He returned home safely and spent the rest of his days being good to everyone. His tail was lost while he was trying to run away from the big bird. The lesson that was taught by Old Brown was never forgotten by the young animal. If he had known the danger, he would have behaved much better during the trip. The journey back across the water was quiet and peaceful for all the animals. Nutkin sat alone in his boat and thought about everything that had happened. He finally understood that bad behavior always brings serious results in the end.

### C1
Once upon a time, within the verdant embrace of a timeless forest near a broad, meandering river, a community of small woodland creatures made their home. Here, countless fine days were spent playing beneath the sheltering canopy while the gentle warmth of the summer sun lingered in the air. Among them lived a singularly wild and exuberant creature whose name was Nutkin. Nutkin overflowed with boundless energy, yet he consistently withheld any semblance of respect from every resident within the forest. Day after day, without fail, this restless animal contrived fresh methods to vex his neighbors and cause quiet distress.

His parents had repeatedly cautioned him that such reckless behavior would inevitably harvest great trouble in the future. However, Nutkin lent no ear to their prudent advice, operating under the absolute conviction that he possessed supreme wisdom. He genuinely believed he could act entirely without restraint and suffer no negative consequences for his insolence. Though the forest was naturally tranquil, Nutkin frequently shattered the peaceful atmosphere with loud, disruptive noises. Whenever other animals attempted to counsel him, he merely laughed aloud and scampered away with astonishing speed.

Life in the woodland continued along this discordant path while the warm season slowly surrendered to the crisp breath of autumn. Had he only heeded the wisdom of his elders, he would never have generated such widespread agitation. The various creatures dwelling near the riverbank found themselves continually exasperated by his obstreperous conduct. Nutkin invariably assumed that misfortune was reserved solely for others and could never touch someone of his cleverness. Every single member of the community secretly hoped that this arrogant youth would soon alter his perspective, yet he remained stubbornly unchanged.

The young animal persisted in his thoughtless course, utterly indifferent to the comfort and harmony of the wider group. He remained convinced that his wild escapades were entirely normal and acceptable. Then, on a crisp autumn morning, the entire community resolved to embark on a journey across the water to a distant island. Constructing diminutive vessels from weathered timber and utilizing slender twigs for sails, they prepared for the voyage. Upon their arrival at the island, they immediately set about gathering vital provisions for the approaching winter.

Nutkin, however, harbored no intention of participating in the arduous labor undertaken by the others. Rejecting any notion of honest work, he preferred to leap effortlessly from branch to branch while shouting frivolous songs. An elderly, solitary bird named Old Brown resided upon that very island. Old Brown was a creature of profound silence who held a deep aversion to unnecessary noise and disorderly conduct. Without a trace of fear, Nutkin hurried straight to the ancient tree where Old Brown made his dwelling and began to pester him.

Every single day, Nutkin brought paltry offerings of various food items and small fragments of wood as supposed gifts for the old bird. Accompanying each offering, he invariably posed an absurd and teasing question. Old Brown merely sat motionless within his hollow residence, refusing to offer a single word in response. Nutkin would then dance frantically around the trunk, highly amused because he mistook the bird's stoic patience for mere sluggishness. Small boats remained tied along the shore while the industrious animals labored tirelessly across the island.

While the busy group spent long hours collecting necessary winter stores, Nutkin scampered aimlessly through the upper branches. Nothing appeared capable of halting the persistent clamor he produced from dawn until dusk. The other animals watched his antics with growing anxiety as the days slipped swiftly past. Day after day, Nutkin sustained this audacious game, growing progressively bolder with every passing hour. He danced perilously close to Old Brown, uttering remarks that crossed the boundary from annoying to genuinely offensive.

The visiting animals observed his escalating recklessness with palpable dread, sensing that a severe reckoning drew near. Through it all, Old Brown maintained his composure while the small creature continued to mock him mercilessly. Many days elapsed in this fashion while the collective harvest slowly grew. Nutkin remained confident that Old Brown lacked the inclination or the will to ever retaliate. He spent his hours shouting and cavorting with absolute abandon, deaf to the silent warnings of his peers.

The community frequently debated the immense peril inherent in Nutkin's persistent provocations, hoping disaster would spare them until their departure. Every prudent piece of advice was utterly dismissed by the foolish animal, who cared only for his own immediate amusement. Had Old Brown chosen to strike earlier, he could easily have seized the arrogant creature long before. The words that fell from Nutkin's tongue were consistently discourteous and provocative. Each afternoon, the visitors watched with bated breath, wondering precisely how long the old bird's patience could endure.

They understood innately that every action carries a definitive consequence and that retribution was merely a matter of time. Still, Nutkin brushed aside all premonitions of danger, blind to the gathering storm. Finally, on the absolute last day of their excursion, Nutkin crossed the ultimate boundary of acceptable behavior. Landing directly upon the back of Old Brown's favored seat, he insolently seized the old bird's tail and pulled it hard. That single, reckless act instantly ignited the fury of Old Brown, who lashed out and pinned the tiny intruder with astonishing speed.

Nutkin fought desperately to escape, but the powerful bird held him fast in a firm, unyielding grip. Though Old Brown inflicted no physical injury, he resolved to administer an unforgettable lesson in humility. Following a frantic and exhausting struggle, Nutkin finally wrenched himself free and bolted for safety with all his remaining strength. He discovered to his horror that he had left his magnificent tail behind in the grasp of the bird, a loss that plunged him into sudden sorrow and silence. From that fateful moment onward, Nutkin never troubled another living soul and embraced a quiet, respectful demeanor.

The other animals looked on with profound astonishment at the remarkable transformation that had overtaken their formerly unruly companion. He returned home humbled, spending the remainder of his days extending kindness to everyone he met. The harsh lesson so effectively imparted by Old Brown was permanently etched into the young animal's memory. Had he recognized the true extent of the danger beforehand, he undoubtedly would have conducted himself with far greater decorum during the trip. The return voyage across the water proved remarkably serene, with Nutkin sitting in solitary reflection as he finally comprehended that arrogance always meets a bitter end.
