## st-0181 — The Tale of Mrs. Tiggy-Winkle
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Kirpi lives in a country. She has a job. She works hard every day. People bring things to her house. She always does a good job. However, some things go away. They are lost every week. She works with things. She is a person who washes. Many people need her service.

One morning, a person comes. The person comes to the door. Several small things are lost. They are lost near water. Kirpi hears the story. She says yes to the person. She wants to find them. She walks up a high place. The way is up. She looks under every tree. She hears a sound. The sound is from a house. She opens the door.

Water is there. A woman works. The room is good. Things stay on lines. Kirpi sees lost things. She sees them on a table. The woman sees her. She gives her water. They spend an hour together. Kirpi puts things into a large hand carrier. She thanks her friend. She walks back down home.

From that day, no things go away. Kirpi has a good home. She lives there now. She helps every person. She works every day. She is very happy. Her friend is also happy. Life is good for both women. They help people every week. No more things are lost now.

### A2
Once upon a time, Kirpi lived in a green country. Kirpi was a very good worker every single day. Many people brought bad clothes to her house. She always cleaned them well. However, some small clothes went missing from her place. This was a big problem for Kirpi. She wanted to know why clothes were lost. Every time, she tried to do her job right. People knew her well. She did not like to lose any item. Her life was about helping others. Mornings and evenings, she worked hard.

One morning, a little person came to the door. Several small white clothes were lost near the water. Kirpi listened well to the person. She promised to find every single item before night. The person looked worried about the missing things. Kirpi told the person not to worry. She would go to look for the clothes. The person thanked Kirpi for her help. Kirpi got ready to leave her house. She checked her clothes and took a small container. Her friend watched her go outside. The light was up in the open air.

Kirpi started to walk up a high land. The way was not wide and quite hard. She looked under every green plant for clothes. Then she heard a sound from a rock house. A nice image of hot water filled the air. Inside, a nice female was cleaning clothes. The walls were very clean. Many cleaned items were on lines. Kirpi saw the missing clothes on a table. The woman welcomed her visitor with hot drink. They spent a happy hour together. Kirpi put all her property into a clean container.

She thanked her friend and walked back home. From that day, no more small clothes went missing. Kirpi felt very happy about the result. Her work was back to normal again. People brought their clothes every week. Nobody lost anything at her house. Kirpi continued to help everyone in the country. She became a very big worker. Her friend from the rock house often came to visit. They drank hot drink and talked for hours. Life was good for Kirpi. She always remembered that special time.

### B1
Once upon a time, Kirpi lived in a green country. Kirpi was a good person who worked hard every single day. Many people brought things to her house because she always cleaned them well. However, some small clothes often went missing during the busy season. If someone lost something, Kirpi would always try to help them find it. She had worked there for many years. She had built a very good name in the whole country. Every person in the town knew her well. They trusted her with all their property. She never lost anything before. She always kept everything safe.

One fine morning, a little person came to the door with a big problem. Several pocket items and small white clothes were lost somewhere near the water. Kirpi listened carefully while the visitor told the whole story about the missing pieces. She promised that she would find every single item before the sun went down. She had already finished her morning work when the visitor arrived. If she finds the items, the visitor will be very happy. She told the visitor not to worry. She prepared herself for a long walk up the hill. She took her bag and left the house.

Kirpi started to walk up a high hill where the path was narrow. She looked around every tree and under every green plant for the lost clothes. After a long time, she heard a soft sound coming from a tiny stone house. When she opened the door, a wonderful smell of warm water filled the air. Inside the little room, a nice person was busy washing clothes in a small pool. The walls were clean, and many freshly washed items hung on lines across the top. Kirpi smiled because she saw all the missing clothes resting safely on a hard table. She had never seen such a nice place before. If she looks closer, she will see her own items too. She walked inside and met her friend.

The person welcomed her visitor and offered some hot tea to drink. They spent a happy hour together talking about the place and the lovely spring weather. Kirpi collected all her returned property and put them carefully into a clean bag. She thanked her new friend and walked back down the hill toward her own home. From that day on, no more small clothes ever went missing in the country. She had solved the big problem successfully. She had returned every single item to its proper place. If anyone asks about the story, Kirpi tells the whole story with a smile. Her life returned to normal after that special day. She was very happy now.

### B2
Once upon a time, a small animal named Kirpi lived in a green country. Kirpi was a very good person who worked hard every day. Many people brought their dirty clothes to her house because she always cleaned them well. However, some small clothes often went missing during the busy season. This problem made many people feel very sad and worried about their things. Kirpi wanted to ensure that every single item was returned to its proper owner. She always hoped that she could find the missing things before the sun went down. The valley was very quiet on that specific morning when a new visitor arrived at her door. A little person came to the front entrance with a big problem that needed immediate attention. Several pocket items and small white things were lost somewhere near the stream. Kirpi listened carefully while the visitor told the whole story about the missing pieces. She promised that she would find every single item before the sun went down behind the high hill. She knew that her work was important for the community. Every person in the valley trusted her completely. If anyone lost something, they would come to her house. She always welcomed them with a warm smile. The green country was a peaceful place where people helped each other. Kirpi loved her life and the daily tasks that she performed. Her house was small, but it was always clean and tidy. People often said that she was the best worker in the entire region. Her good reputation spread far and wide across the green fields. Even children knew her name and waved to her when she passed by. She spent hours washing and drying different types of fabric. The water came from a clear stream that flowed near her home. She used natural soap that made the clothes smell fresh and sweet. Every piece of clothing was treated with great care. Nothing was ever damaged while it was in her hands. She checked every pocket before the washing process began. If she found any coins or paper, she kept them safely until the owner returned. This honesty made her even more popular among the local residents.

Kirpi started to walk up a high hill where the path was narrow and rough. She looked around every tree and under every green plant for the lost clothes. After a long time, she heard a soft sound coming from a tiny stone house. When she opened the door, a wonderful smell of warm water and clean air filled the place. Inside the little room, a nice creature was busy washing clothes in a small container. The walls were clean, and many freshly washed items hung on lines across the top part of the room. Kirpi smiled because she saw all the missing things resting safely on a wooden table. The worker welcomed her visitor and offered some hot tea to drink. They spent a happy hour together talking about the valley and the lovely spring weather. The friendly creature explained why those items had been taken. They had been collected by mistake because the wind had blown them away. Kirpi understood the situation and forgave her new friend immediately. They agreed that such an accident should never happen again. They shared stories about their lives and laughed together. The tea was hot and sweet, which made them feel very comfortable. The room was cozy, and the fire in the corner gave a nice heat. Kirpi looked around the stone house and admired the wonderful arrangement. It was a quiet place where someone could rest and work in peace. The other worker was happy to have a visitor to talk with. They promised to visit each other more often in the future. Spring days were long, and there was plenty of time for friendship. They watched the sun move slowly across the blue sky. Birds were singing in the trees outside the window. It was a perfect moment that neither of them would ever forget. The air was filled with the sweet scent of flowers from the garden. Kirpi felt a deep sense of joy in her heart. She knew that her long walk up the hill had been worthwhile. The mystery of the missing clothes was finally solved. Everything was clear, and peace was restored to the valley. They finished their tea and prepared for the journey back down.

Kirpi collected all her returned property and put them carefully into a clean bag. She thanked her new friend and walked back down the hill toward her own home. From that day on, no more small clothes ever went missing in the country. People were very happy because their things were always safe now. Kirpi continued to work hard every single day to help anyone who needed her assistance. Life in the green country was quiet, and everyone lived together with good friendship. The small animal became famous among all the residents of the region. Many years passed, but people still remembered the great work of the wonderful worker. Whenever any problem happened, the community knew exactly who could help them find a solution. The little stone house on the hill remained a symbol of friendship and good work for the future. New families moved into the valley and heard the story about Kirpi. They were always amazed by her kindness and dedication. She became a true legend among the younger generations. Her name was mentioned with great respect at every public meeting. Even though she grew older, her hands remained strong and steady. She taught her skills to anyone who wanted to learn the trade. Many young people came to her house to receive proper training. She showed them how to wash clothes without causing any damage. She explained the importance of honesty and hard work in life. Her students respected her as a wise and patient teacher. The community grew larger, but the spirit of the valley never changed. People supported one another through good times and bad times. Kirpi lived a long and happy life surrounded by friends. When she finally retired, the whole town threw a big party for her. There was music, dancing, and plenty of delicious food to eat. Everyone gave her wonderful gifts to show their deep appreciation. She smiled and thanked them for their love and support. Her legacy lived on in the hearts of everyone in the green country. The little stone house and the stream remained important places for the local history. And so, the quiet valley stayed peaceful and happy forever.

### C1
Once upon a time, within the verdant embrace of a tranquil valley, resided a remarkably industrious creature named Kirpi. Each morning heralded the arrival of villagers bearing bundles of soiled garments, trusting entirely in her meticulous craftsmanship. Not a single person doubted her integrity, for she approached her labor with profound dedication and gentle grace. Yet, as the busy season reached its fevered peak, a peculiar phenomenon began to plague the community. Delicate handkerchiefs and small white linens inexplicably vanished from drying lines, leaving owners distressed and bewildered. Kirpi vowed to restore order, determined that every missing stitch should find its way back to its rightful master.

The valley remained hushed and still on that particular morning when a distressed visitor arrived at her threshold. A tiny figure stood at the entryway, clutching a lamentable tale of lost pocket items and misplaced garments near the stream. Kirpi listened with unwavering patience, absorbing every detail of the grievance with empathetic solemnity. She offered a firm promise: before the sun descended behind the high ridge, every single item would be recovered. Every inhabitant in the valley relied upon her completely, knowing her word was as dependable as the sunrise itself. Whenever misfortune struck, footsteps naturally turned toward her cottage, where a warm welcome invariably awaited.

Peace defined the green country, a realm where neighbors sustained one another through simple acts of kindness. Kirpi cherished her modest existence and the rhythmic cadence of her daily routines. Her cottage, though remarkably diminutive, radiated an immaculate charm that reflected her orderly spirit. Whispers throughout the region frequently proclaimed her the finest laundress in the entire district. Her sterling reputation transcended the immediate fields, reaching even the youngest children who waved cheerfully as she passed. Hours vanished swiftly as she immersed herself in washing various textiles with crystal water channeled from the nearby stream.

Utilizing exclusively natural soaps, she ensured that every garment emerged smelling faintly of wildflowers and crisp air. No fabric ever suffered damage while resting in her capable and tender hands. She invariably inspected every pocket with meticulous care before immersing the clothes into the basin. Should she discover forgotten coins or stray bits of paper, she preserved them safely until their owners returned. Such steadfast honesty elevated her status among the local residents, cementing her legacy as a pillar of virtue. With the morning sun climbing higher, Kirpi prepared herself for a journey that would finally unravel the recent mystery.

She commenced a steady ascent up the steep hillside, where the footpath grew increasingly narrow and rugged. Her sharp eyes scanned beneath every fern and behind every weathered trunk for any trace of the missing apparel. After a considerable climb, a faint, soothing sound drifted toward her from a secluded stone dwelling nestled among the rocks. Upon pushing the heavy wooden door open, she was greeted by an intoxicating wave of warm steam and pristine air. Within the cozy chamber, another industrious creature stood waist-deep in soapy activity, furiously scrubbing garments. Clean lines stretched across the low ceiling, bearing an astonishing multitude of freshly laundered items swaying gently.

A wave of realization washed over Kirpi as she recognized the missing linens neatly arranged upon a nearby wooden table. The resident worker turned from her basin, instantly offering a radiant smile and a steaming cup of tea. They spent a delightful hour conversing about the valley, the pleasant spring weather, and the quirks of fate. The friendly creature soon explained the peculiar disappearance: strong gales had swept the unattended garments away from the valley. They had been collected entirely by accident, whisked upward by the capricious mountain winds. Kirpi readily understood the situation and absolved her new acquaintance of any wrongdoing without a moment of hesitation.

Mutual laughter echoed off the stone walls as they shared anecdotes from their respective lives. The sweet, steaming tea provided immense comfort against the lingering chill of the upland breeze. Kirpi admired the ingenious arrangement of the dwelling, a sanctuary designed for both relentless labor and profound peace. Delighted to entertain a guest, the resident creature eagerly proposed frequent visits once the spring matured. Long afternoons stretched before them, offering ample opportunity to cultivate a lasting and meaningful friendship. Through the window, melodic birdsong drifted inward, harmonizing with the gentle crackle of the corner hearth.

It was a suspended moment in time, an idyllic tableau neither of them would ever truly forget. The heavy scent of blossoming gardens drifted on the draft, filling the room with an unspoken joy. Kirpi felt a profound contentment settling deep within her chest, knowing her arduous climb had been profoundly worthwhile. The frustrating mystery of the missing clothes had dissolved completely, restoring absolute harmony to the valley below. Having finished their tea, they began gathering the recovered property with meticulous care. Kirpi packed the items securely into a sturdy bag, preparing for the descent back toward her own hearth.

Bidding her gracious host a warm farewell, she navigated the winding path downward with a lighter step. From that eventful afternoon onward, not a single piece of clothing vanished mysteriously from the district again. The villagers rejoiced, comforted by the absolute security of their belongings under Kirpi's watchful eye. She persisted in her daily labor, offering unwavering assistance to anyone burdened by circumstance. Decades flowed onward, yet the community never forgot the remarkable dedication of the valley laundress. Whenever a communal dilemma arose, everyone knew precisely whose wisdom to seek for a reliable resolution.

The tiny stone cottage on the hillside endured as a permanent emblem of cooperation and honest toil. As succeeding generations of families settled into the valley, they learned the legendary tale of Kirpi with wide-eyed wonder. Though time inevitably silvered her hair, her hands retained their steadfast strength and admirable precision. She willingly imparted her trade secrets to eager apprentices who sought instruction at her doorway. Emphasizing diligence and absolute integrity, she shaped her students into respected members of the expanding township. The community flourished around her, yet the foundational spirit of mutual support remained completely unaltered.

Kirpi lived a wonderfully protracted life, surrounded by cherished companions and enduring gratitude. When she finally laid aside her washing basins for retirement, the entire town organized a magnificent celebration. Music resonated through the green meadows, accompanied by abundant feasts and heartfelt expressions of admiration. She accepted their tokens of affection with characteristic modesty, smiling warmly at the assembled crowd. Her luminous legacy lived on within every heart across the green country, inspiring future generations. And thus, the tranquil valley persisted in enduring peace, forever guarded by the memory of tireless labor.

## st-0182 — The Tale of Benjamin Bunny
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Peter Rabbit and Benjamin Bunny walk. They walk near a big house. Peter feels very small today. Benjamin wants to see a room. They walk to a high place. Benjamin goes down the wall. Peter stays on top now. A large dog appears there. Both little friends feel very bad.

They stay under a big thing. The large thing falls over them. They cannot move out now. They sit in the dark night. They think how to return home. Old Mr. Bunny comes with a long hand. He helps his child and another. He is very big and strong. He takes them both home. He gives them work to do.

They walk home with old Mr. Bunny. They are very happy to be back. The day is long and full. They sit down in the house. They look at the old man. He gives them food and water. They eat the food now. They feel good after the walk. Peter stays still all day long. Benjamin looks at the small room.

The two friends sit in the house. They look at the old man again. He gives them more food to eat. They eat and they feel well. The day is long and hard. The light is down now. They want to rest in the room. Old Mr. Bunny helps them out. They go to rest in the room. The little friends are well protected.

### A2
Once upon a time. Little Benjamin Bunny went for a walk. Peter went with him. They walked near a big garden. Peter felt very worried. He had trouble there before. Benjamin was full of force. He wanted to see the plant house again. They walked without noise through the ground. They came to a high wall. Benjamin moved down easy. Peter stayed on top. He remembered his past bad act. Suddenly, a large animal appeared near them. Both family members felt very worried.

The fine animals went under a large box. They wanted to stay safe. Then, the box fell completely over them. They could not move out at all. They sat together in the night. They wondered about home. After some time, old Mr. Bunny arrived. He had a big piece of tree material. He saved his son and brother child. He felt quite cross. They went into the bad garden. He took them home. He gave them a little task as work. They were going to remember this day.

The light went down behind the high wall. Night came over the green ground. The little animals sat in the house. They did not talk much. Old Mr. Bunny looked at them. He had a serious face. Peter knew he made a bad choice earlier. Benjamin thought about the big animal near the box. They both knew they must follow the rule next time. Life in the garden was full of risk.

Morning came with great light. The light shone on the little house. Benjamin and Peter got up early. They ate some food at the table. Old Mr. Bunny watched them close. He told them to stay inside the place. The little family agreed to follow the plan. They did not want any more trouble. They played games near the door all day long.

Many days passed after that event. The little animals grew up fast. They always walked on the right way. They never went near the high wall again. Other animals in the country knew their story. Peter and Benjamin became smart little animals. They told their friends about the big animal. Everyone learned a good thing from the past. Good feelings came back to the family.

### B1
Once upon a time, little Benjamin Bunny and his friend Peter Rabbit went for a long walk. They walked near a big garden. Peter had trouble there before, so he felt very careful. Benjamin, however, was quite strong in his mind. He wanted to see the food house again. They walked quietly through the field until they reached a high wall.

Benjamin easily jumped down, while Peter stayed on top. He remembered his past mistake. Suddenly, a large cat appeared near them. This made both family members very afraid. The fast animals quickly hid under a large bag. They stayed safe from the cat. Unfortunately, the bag fell completely over them. They could not move out at all.

They sat together in the dark and wondered how they would return home. They have wondered for some time. If they stay longer, they will have more trouble. After some time, old Mr. Bunny arrived with a big stick. He brought his son and younger relative back. He was quite angry because they had gone into the bad place. He took them both home and gave them a little task as a lesson.

They have learned a hard lesson today. If they go out again, they will stay careful. Peter has understood the big danger. Benjamin has understood the rule too. They will never go near that place again. Old Mr. Bunny watched them closely because he wanted to keep them safe. The evening came very quickly. They sat inside the warm house and ate some good food together. Peace returned to the little family.

Life went on for the animals. They played games in the green field. Days passed by very slowly. The light appeared brightly every morning. They always remembered the big cat and the high wall. Fear kept them away from danger. They have grown much older now. Every single member felt happy. They lived safely through the year.

Sometimes they talked about that story. Peter usually shook his head, and Benjamin just smiled at him. They knew they had been lucky. Luck saved them from the cat. They promised to follow their father. Good behavior brought peace to their home. That story stayed in their minds. They never forgot the big lesson. Everything was good at the end.

### B2
Once upon a time, little Benjamin Bunny and his cousin Peter Rabbit went for a long walk near a big garden. Peter had experienced trouble there before, so he felt very careful. Benjamin, however, was quite bold and wanted to see the vegetable house again. They walked quietly through the grass until they reached a high wall. Benjamin easily jumped down, but Peter stayed on top because he remembered his past mistake. Suddenly, a large cat appeared near them, which made both cousins very afraid. The smart animals quickly hid low within a large container to stay safe from the cat. Unfortunately, the container fell completely over them, and they could not move out at all. They sat together in the dark, wondering how they would ever return home. After some time, old Mr. Bunny arrived with a big stick and rescued his son and cousin. He was quite angry because they had gone into the closed garden. He took them both home and gave them a little task to do as a penalty. They walked along the long road while the sun was high in the sky. The day was warm, and many people were out in the country. Benjamin and Peter had always enjoyed playing games together, but this day was different. They had planned to find some fresh food near the vegetable house. If they had known about the large cat, they would have stayed at home. Such problems often happen when young animals do not listen to their parents. Mr. Bunny had told them many times that the place was dangerous. The high wall was built to keep small animals away from the valuable plants. Peter knew that rule well, which is why he stopped at the top. Benjamin, who was less careful, went inside without thinking about the result.

When the big cat appeared, both cousins felt their hearts beat fast. They ran quickly to the container, hoping the animal would not see them. The container was heavy, and once it fell over them, escape was impossible. Minutes passed slowly while they sat in the dark space. Every sound outside made them think the cat was coming closer. They hoped someone would come to help them before it was too late. Old Mr. Bunny was looking for them across the whole area. When he found the container, he used his big stick to move it away. Both cousins were very glad to see him and quickly ran out. Mr. Bunny spoke to them firmly about going where they should not be. They walked back to their home with their father, feeling sorry for their actions. That experience taught them a valuable lesson about following important rules. From that day forward, they always listened to old Mr. Bunny and stayed away from trouble. They learned that ignoring parents always leads to terrible danger. The sun began to set behind the high hills as they finished their long walk. Each cousin promised never to break the rules again. The evening air became cool, and they felt tired after such a difficult day. When they finally reached their home, they went straight to bed. They slept deeply and dreamed of quiet days without any fear. Their father watched over them while they rested quietly. He knew that they understood the lesson completely. Life returned to normal for the little family in the quiet country.

### C1
Once upon a time, in the quiet expanse of the English countryside, little Benjamin Bunny and his cousin Peter Rabbit set out on a long walk. The path wound gracefully near the edge of a massive, walled garden that cast a long shadow over the damp grass. Peter, having experienced immense trouble within those very boundaries before, felt a heavy sense of caution gripping his chest. He remembered the terrifying sights and sounds of his previous adventure, resolving to keep his distance from the forbidden domain. Benjamin, however, possessed a much bolder spirit and burned with a stubborn desire to see the vegetable house once again.

They walked quietly through the tall, dew-soaked grass until they reached the imposing height of the red-brick wall. The morning air carried the faint, sweet scent of growing things, tempting the young rabbits even further into danger. Without a moment of hesitation, Benjamin easily jumped down onto the forbidden soil on the other side. Peter stayed firmly perched on top of the brickwork because he remembered the bitter taste of his past mistake. He looked down at his cousin with anxious eyes, wishing Benjamin would reconsider his foolish and reckless plan.

Suddenly, a large, heavy-footed cat appeared near them from behind a thick patch of overgrown gooseberry bushes. That unexpected apparition made both cousins freeze in place as a wave of cold terror washed over them. Peter gasped in horror from his elevated vantage point, while Benjamin scrambled frantically to find a secure hiding place. The smart animals quickly hid low within a large, overturned wicker basket to stay safe from the menacing predator. They held their breath, praying that the sharp-eyed feline would simply wander away and leave them in peace.

Unfortunately, the basket was unbalanced, and the sudden movement caused the heavy container to fall completely over them. In a fraction of a second, their makeshift shelter became an inescapable trap that blocked out the morning light. They could not move out at all, wedged tightly together in the musty darkness beneath the wicker ribs. Outside, the soft thud of padded paws suggested that the terrible beast was prowling dangerously close to their prison. Every fiber of their being tingled with dread as they realized how utterly helpless their current situation had become.

They sat together in the suffocating dark, wondering how they would ever return home to the safety of their burrow. Benjamin felt a bitter sting of regret for ignoring his cousin's warnings and rushing blindly into the trap. Peter whispered reassurance through trembling whiskers, though his own heart hammered wildly against his ribs. Minutes stretched into agonizing hours while they listened to the muffled rustle of leaves just inches away. They pinned all their remaining hopes on a miracle, praying that someone would come to their rescue before nightfall.

After some time, old Mr. Bunny arrived upon the scene armed with a formidable big stick and a stern expression. He had been searching the countryside for his wayward son and nephew with mounting anxiety and irritation. Spotting the trapped basket near the wall, he strode forward with purposeful steps and knocked firmly on the wicker. He used his big stick to lift and move the container away, letting a flood of bright daylight blind the captives. Both cousins were profoundly glad to see him, scrambling out quickly from the dark and dusty prison.

Mr. Bunny was quite angry because they had disobeyed his explicit warnings and gone into the closed garden. His eyes flashed with disappointment as he lectured them on the foolishness of courting such unnecessary peril. He took them both by the ear and led them away from the dangerous wall without a moment of hesitation. As a penalty for their disobedience, he assigned them a tedious little task to accomplish on the journey back. They were forced to carry heavy loads of garden herbs in small baskets strapped securely to their backs.

They walked along the long road while the sun was high in the sky and the afternoon heat pressed down heavily. The day was warm, and many people were out in the country enjoying the pleasant weather of the late season. Benjamin and Peter had always enjoyed playing games together, but this day was utterly devoid of any joy. They had planned to find some fresh food near the vegetable house, but their adventure had ended in utter misery. If they had known about the large cat waiting in the bushes, they would have stayed safely at home.

Such problems inevitably happen when young animals refuse to listen to the wise counsel of their parents. Mr. Bunny had told them many times that the place was dangerous and guarded by unfriendly creatures. The high wall was deliberately built to keep small animals away from the valuable and tempting plants inside. Peter knew that rule well, which is why he had wisely stopped at the top instead of jumping down. Benjamin, who was far less careful, had gone inside without giving a single thought to the inevitable result.

The sun began to set behind the high hills as they finally finished their long and exhausting walk home. The evening air became cool and crisp, offering a welcome relief after such a difficult and stressful day. When they finally reached their cozy burrow, they went straight to bed without a single word of complaint. They slept deeply and dreamed of quiet, peaceful days without any fear or looming threats of danger. Their father watched over them lovingly while they rested, knowing that they understood the lesson completely.

Life returned to normal for the little family in the quiet country, undisturbed by further foolish adventures. Each cousin silently promised never to break the sacred rules of the warren or test their parents' patience again. The experience had burned a permanent mark into their memories, teaching them the high cost of disobedience. From that day forward, they always listened to old Mr. Bunny and stayed far away from trouble. They learned once and for all that ignoring parental wisdom unfailingly leads to terrifying and regretful consequences.

## st-0183 — The Tale of Mr. Jeremy Fisher
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Mr Jeremy Fisher lives in a little house. His house is near the water. He decides to go out on a day. He wants to get food. He wants small food for a time. He gets his things ready and leaves home. He walks down to the water. He finds a small boat there. Mr Jeremy Fisher gets into the boat. He moves away from the land. He sits down with a long branch. He waits and feels the good time. He looks at the water.

The state of things changes fast. Black things appear over him. Hard water falls down. Mr Jeremy Fisher tries to go back. A big one bites his line. The one pulls him down into the water. The water is bad. He loses his best branch. A big body tries to get him. He swims away fast. He goes up onto the land. He is wet and he moves. He decides never to do it again.

Mr Jeremy Fisher stays at his house. He looks at the water every day. He thinks about the big body. He wants no more problem today. He stays home and is very quiet. He sits by the door. He has no food from the water. He thinks about the day. He looks at the small room. He feels no need to go out. He stays in his house. He is safe and well.

### A2
The man lived in a little house near water. He wanted to go fishing on a fine day. His plan was to catch small fish for food. He prepared his things and left home. He walked down to the water. He found a small wooden vessel. The man moved into the vessel. He pushed away from land. He sat down and started to fish with his long stick. He thought about how nice the afternoon felt. He wanted to catch many fish. He looked at the clear water. He felt very pleased today. His heart was full of joy. He waited for a long time. He sat still in the small vessel. The star above was hot upon the water. He hoped to catch a big fish. His family would eat the food later. He loved this calm place by the water. Many small fish moved near the vessel. He watched them move around the lower part. He kept his long stick steady in his hands. Every minute felt good. He smiled as he thought about his plan.

Suddenly, the conditions changed. Dark figures appeared above. Strong water began to fall from the heavy color. The man tried to pull his vessel back. A big fish bit his line. The fish pulled him down into the water. The water was very cold. He lost his favorite fishing stick. A large animal tried to catch him. He moved away as fast as possible. He went up on the green plant area. He was moving fast and not free from the falling water. The man decided to never go fishing in the falling water again. He ran back toward his little house. His clothes were full of water from top to lower part. He felt cold and without energy from the event. The big animal stayed in the deep water. The man reached his front door without danger. He opened the door and went inside. He sat by the fire to get hot. His heart beat fast from fear. He promised himself to be full of care next time. The bad condition continued outside for hours. He looked out the window at the dark condition. He was pleased to be home and without danger again.

### B1
Fisher had a small house near the water. He had lived in that nice home for many years. One fine morning, he decided that he would go fishing. He wanted to catch small fish for his dinner. Therefore, he prepared his fishing things and left his house. He always enjoyed spending time by the blue water. If the weather stayed fine, he would catch many fish. He had already checked his tools before he started his journey. Everything was ready for a great day on the lake. He walked down to the edge of the water slowly and quietly. He found a small boat waiting for him there.

Fisher climbed into the boat and pushed away from the land. He sat down and started to fish with his long stick. While he was waiting, he thought about the afternoon hours. He felt that the day was very nice and quiet. The sun looked down upon the calm water around him. He had never felt such peace in his whole life. He hoped that a big fish would take his line soon. Suddenly, the weather changed and dark forms appeared above the sky. Heavy rain began to fall into the water all around him. Fisher tried to pull his boat back quickly. However, a large animal hit his line very hard. The wild animal pulled him down into the cold water.

The water was very cold, and he lost his stick. A large animal tried to catch him in the deep lake. He swam away as fast as possible from the danger. He climbed up onto the green land, shaking and damp. Fisher sat on the green land for some time. He was damp from the rain and felt very cold. He decided that he would never go fishing in storms. He had learned a very important lesson that afternoon. If he went out again, he would watch the weather.

He walked back to his small house with slow steps. He had lost his favorite tool in the deep water. Still, he was glad that he had survived the event today. He would remember this day for a very long time. He has never forgotten the lessons of that trip. If he plans another trip, he will check the sky first.

### B2
The man lived in a nice little house near the water. He decided that he would go fishing on a bright day. His plan was to catch small fish for his dinner. Therefore, he prepared his fishing gear and left his home. He walked down to the edge of the water and found a small boat. The man climbed into the boat and pushed away from the land. He sat down and started to fish with his long stick. While he was waiting, he thought about how nice the afternoon felt. Suddenly, the weather changed and dark clouds appeared above him. Heavy rain began to fall into the water all around him. The man tried to pull his boat back to the shore quickly. However, a big fish bit his line and pulled him down into the water. The water was cold, and he lost his favorite fishing stick. A large animal tried to catch him, but he swam away as fast as possible. He climbed up onto the green grass, shaking and wet from the rain. The man decided that he would never go fishing in the rain again. He had spent many hours preparing for that journey. The house was warm, which made him feel much safer. If he had known about the weather, he would have stayed inside. Although the animal was large, it could not reach the green grass. Every single moment spent in the water felt dangerous. His friends often told him that the lake was full of surprises. He understood why people usually checked the sky before leaving. The small boat moved away while he was fighting the big fish. He hoped that someone might find it floating near the shore. After the rain stopped, the air became very quiet and calm. He looked across the water and wondered about his lost gear. Nothing could change the fact that his favorite stick was gone. He walked back to his house with a very tired body. The evening came quickly, bringing a sense of relief to him. He sat by the warm fire and dried his wet clothes. Dinner was simple because he had caught nothing during the trip. He promised himself that he would be much more careful next time. Life by the water was full of strange events. He loved his little home, despite the recent trouble. Tomorrow would bring another chance to do things differently. For now, rest was the only thing that mattered to him. He closed his eyes and fell into a deep sleep.

The man woke up early the next morning. The sun was shining brightly across the calm water. He walked out of his house and looked toward the lake. Everything looked quiet, as if nothing bad had happened. He remembered the big fish that had pulled him under. A small smile appeared on his face despite the memory. He decided to make a cup of hot tea instead of fishing. While he drank his tea, he read a book by the window. The book was about animals that lived near the water. He wondered if the same animal was still watching him. Of course, he could not be sure about anything. The morning air felt fresh and cool against his skin. He felt grateful that he had survived the strange adventure. If he went back to the lake, he would use better equipment. His little house offered all the protection that he needed. He spent the rest of the day cleaning his wet clothes. By afternoon, he felt completely normal again. The experience taught him a very important lesson. Nature was powerful, and people had to respect it. He would always remember the cold water and the dark clouds. Even so, he still loved his quiet life by the lake. The sun began to set, painting the sky in soft colors. He closed his front door and prepared for another night. The man knew that he was safe inside his home. He looked forward to whatever the future might bring him. He thought about the boat that had moved away. He hoped that the boat was safe somewhere near land. Perhaps a local person would bring it back to him. The fire in the room burned with a bright light. He ate some simple food and drank more hot tea. His body felt tired from all the work in the water. He knew that he must rest for another big day. The sound of the wind outside was very quiet now. He checked his clothes to make sure they were dry. Everything was in order inside his small living space. He smiled again when he thought about his bed. The night grew darker as the hours passed slowly. He closed his eyes and slept until the morning came.

Many days passed after the strange event at the lake. The man lived quietly and focused on small daily tasks. He spent his mornings reading books and drinking hot tea. Sometimes he walked near the edge of the water to watch the birds. He always checked the sky before stepping outside his door. The memory of the big fish remained fresh in his mind. He knew that nature demanded respect from every person. His friends often visited him to talk about the old days. They brought food and shared stories about local events. He told them about the large animal in the deep water. They listened with great interest and agreed with his caution. Nobody wanted to face such danger during a simple trip. He felt happy that he had survived the terrifying moment. His little house provided a safe place from every storm. The warm fire kept him comfortable during cold evenings. He realized that life was precious and full of unexpected turns. Every single night brought peace and deep rest to him. He promised to keep his distance from the deep lake. The sun rose again, bringing light to the beautiful world. He smiled as he started another peaceful day at home.

### C1
Mr. Jeremy Fisher inhabited an impeccably damp little house situated near the marge of a vast and glittering water. It was upon a morning of singular brightness that he resolved to indulge in the gentle art of angling. His ambition was merely to secure a brace of small minnows for his evening repast. Accordingly, he meticulously prepared his tackle and stepped out into the luminous air. Descending the grassy slope to the water's edge, he discovered a diminutive wooden boat resting quietly among the reeds. Without hesitation, he clambered inside and pushed away from the solid land. He settled himself upon the wooden thwart and began to cast his long bamboo rod with practiced ease. While he waited for a bite, he reflected upon how pleasant the afternoon felt. The sunlight danced upon the ripples, and a profound sense of tranquility enveloped him. Suddenly, however, the atmosphere underwent a swift and ominous transformation. Dark, swollen clouds gathered overhead, blotting out the benevolent sun with alarming speed. Heavy drops of rain began to pelt the surface of the water all around his tiny vessel. Alarmed by the tempest, the fisherman attempted to pull his boat back toward the safety of the shore. Scarcely had he turned his craft when a monstrous fish struck his line with violent force. The creature was so powerful that it dragged him clean over the gunwale and down into the murky water. The temperature of the lake struck him as intensely cold, shocking his senses. In the chaotic struggle, he let go of his favorite fishing rod forever. A vast, shadowy animal lunged toward him from the depths, attempting to engulf him entirely. Exhibiting remarkable agility, he swam away from the menace as fast as his trembling limbs could propel him. He scrambled up the verdant embankment, dripping wet and shivering violently from the sudden chill. Standing upon the secure turf, the beleaguered man vowed that he would never venture fishing in the rain again. He had spent many hours preparing for that disastrous journey, only for it to unravel in moments. Fortunately, his little house stood nearby, and its promise of warmth made him feel vastly safer. Had he possessed any foreknowledge of the tempest, he would have remained indoors by his hearth. Although the massive aquatic beast was formidable, it possessed no means to reach the green grass where he stood.

Every single moment spent submerged in that treacherous water had felt perilous in the extreme. His well-meaning friends had frequently warned him that the lake was harboring countless unpredictable surprises. Now he fully understood why sensible people invariably consulted the sky before embarking on such expeditions. His small boat had drifted away into the mist while he was engaged in his aquatic struggle. He could only hope that someone might eventually recover it floating safely near the opposite shore. Once the torrential rain finally ceased, the surrounding air grew remarkably quiet and calm. He gazed across the glassy expanse of the water and mourned the loss of his cherished gear. Nothing could alter the bitter truth that his finest rod was gone forever. With a heavy sigh, he walked back toward his dwelling with a thoroughly exhausted body. The evening arrived with unusual swiftness, bringing a profound sense of relief to his tired spirit. He seated himself beside a crackling fire and dried his soaked garments with meticulous care. Dinner proved exceedingly simple, consisting of meager scraps since his expedition had yielded nothing. He promised himself faithfully that he would exercise far greater caution during future outings. Life by the margins of the lake was undeniably fraught with strange and unexpected events. He loved his cozy little home intensely, despite the recent trauma he had endured. Tomorrow would inevitably dawn with another chance to conduct his affairs quite differently. For the present moment, however, absolute rest remained the only priority of any consequence. He closed his weary eyes and sank quickly into a deep, dreamless slumber. With the arrival of the next morning, the man awoke remarkably early. The sun was shining brightly once more, casting a golden hue across the calm water. He stepped out of his front door and looked toward the shimmering lake. Everything appeared preternaturally quiet, as though no disaster had transpired the previous afternoon. The memory of the colossal fish dragging him beneath the surface flashed vividly through his mind. Despite the unpleasant recollection, a small, ironic smile crept across his weathered face.

He resolved to abandon all thoughts of fishing in favor of a comforting cup of hot tea. While sipping his warm brew, he sat by the window and immersed himself in a volume detailing aquatic wildlife. A nagging curiosity prompted him to wonder if the identical creature was still watching him from the depths. Of course, he could never attain absolute certainty regarding such matters. The morning air felt invigoratingly fresh and cool against his sensitive skin. He felt profoundly grateful for having survived his bizarre and terrifying adventure. Should he ever return to the lake, he determined to equip himself with vastly superior apparatus. His modest little cottage offered all the fundamental protection that any reasonable creature could possibly require. He spent the remainder of the day laundering his wet clothes and restoring order to his parlor. By the time afternoon arrived, his customary equilibrium had completely returned. The harrowing experience had imparted a stern and unforgettable lesson about the forces of the natural world. Nature was undeniably powerful, demanding the utmost respect from all who crossed her path. The memory of the freezing water and the gathering dark clouds would remain with him forever. Even so, his affection for his quiet, solitary life by the lake remained entirely undiminished. As the sun began its descent, it painted the firmament in delicate shades of amber and rose. He carefully closed his front door and bolted it securely against the approaching night. He possessed absolute confidence that he was entirely safe within the confines of his sanctuary. He looked forward with quiet optimism to whatever the mysterious future might eventually unveil. Many peaceful days elapsed following the dramatic incident at the lake. The man lived quietly, devoting his time exclusively to humble daily chores and quiet contemplation. He spent his mornings reading edifying books and sipping fragrant tea by his sunlit window. Whenever he strolled near the water's edge, he merely observed the waterbirds with detached curiosity. Before stepping outside his threshold, he invariably cast a cautious glance upward at the shifting sky. The memory of the giant fish remained a sharp reminder that nature required constant deference.

His acquaintances occasionally dropped by for afternoon visits to reminisce about bygone eras. They brought gifts of wholesome food and shared local gossip regarding various neighborhood happenings. He recounted his dramatic encounter with the monstrous beast lurking in the deep water. They listened with rapt attention, nodding solemnly and validating his newfound sense of caution. Not a single person among them wished to court such peril during a leisurely outing. He felt immensely fortunate to have escaped such a grim fate with his life intact. His snug dwelling continued to serve as an impenetrable fortress against every conceivable storm outside. The comforting hearth fire kept him thoroughly warm during the increasingly crisp autumn evenings. He recognized with absolute clarity that human existence was remarkably precious and deeply unpredictable. Every single night brought renewed peace and restorative rest to his weary frame. He maintained his solemn vow to keep a safe distance from the center of the lake. The sun rose majestically once more, illuminating a world of breathtaking beauty. He smiled gently as he commenced another thoroughly uneventful and harmonious day at home. The quiet routine provided him with a profound and abiding sense of fulfillment. Nothing could disrupt the serene rhythm of his days now that he understood his limitations. He brewed his tea, tended his garden, and enjoyed the simple pleasures of solitude. The lake glittered in the distance, a silent neighbor holding its many secrets beneath the surface. He felt completely at peace with his surroundings and with himself.

## st-0184 — The Tale of Tom Kitten
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
A mother animal lives in a house. She has three little children. They do not keep things right. Morning comes for a big party. She puts things on Tom Kitten. She tells them to walk. Tom Kitten does not hear well. He walks on the open land.

His clothes parts fall off now. The wear is too big. A small animal finds the things down. She thinks they are good food. She puts them into her face. Tom Kitten feels very bad now. He looks all over for them. The mother comes back to them.

She sees the broken wear. She is very bad with them. She sends them to their room. They sit in the small area. Company comes to the house later. The small children stay behind. They lose the big party. They do not follow rules.

Little ones remember this story. They want to do well. They look at the mother now. She gives them good food. They sit and eat the food. The party is very big. The children are good now. Everyone is very happy.

They stay in the room long. They think about the party. Mother animal looks at them again. She wants them to learn. Rules are important for every child. Tom Kitten sits very still now. The day ends with no more problem. They rest until the next morning.

### A2
Once upon a time, a mother lived in a nice house. She lived with three little kids. The kids were very bad animals. They did not like to keep their clothes clean. One fine morning, the mother wanted to dress the boys. She had company for a party. She put a pretty dress and nice clothes on Tom. Tom was one of the brothers. He was a small child.

The mother told the kids to walk in the garden. She told them not to spoil their clothes. However, Tom did not listen to good guidance. While he was walking around the green land, the fast things began to fall. The things fell off his clothes one by one. His sister tried to help him. The clothes were simply too large for his body. Soon, another bad animal found the fine things on the ground. An animal thought that they were lovely little food. She put them into her face.

Tom felt very bad. His clothes were now broken. His special items were missing. He tried to look for them through all the place. He could not find anything in the green land. After a short time, the mother came back to the garden. She wanted to see her children. She saw the open clothes and the missing things. She became bad with the boys. She sent them up to their room.

She told them to stay there for the rest of the day. The poor kids had to sit. They sat in the dark room without any play. The mother had company later that evening. The bad brothers were not able to join the party. They had to remain up. They did not follow simple rules about their clothes. Even today, little children remember that story. They try to be good children every single day.

### B1
Once upon a time, a mother cat lived in a nice house with three little kids. The kids were very bad animals who did not like to keep their clothes clean. One fine morning, the mother wanted to dress the boys for a party with some company. She put a pretty shirt on Tom Kitten, who was one of the brothers. She wanted him to look good for the guests who would arrive later that day. Tom Kitten was a young cat who did not always listen to good advice. His mother hoped that he would act well while they had company at home. She told the kids to walk carefully in the garden and not hurt their clothes. However, Tom Kitten was a strange child who did not care about simple rules. He ran outside and played near the trees where the ground was full of green plants. While he walked around the earth, the small ties on his shirt fell off. His sister tried to help him. The clothes were simply too large for his small body. She picked up the items from the floor, but more pieces fell off his arms. Tom Kitten felt very sad. His clothes were now broken, and his special things were missing. He tried to look for them everywhere, but he could not find anything in the garden. Soon, another bad animal named Puddle-Duck found the bright items on the ground. She thought that they were lovely little food objects and put them into her mouth. She did not know that those small pieces belonged to the young cat in the garden. Tom Kitten continued to search for his lost property, but the search was difficult. After a short time, the mother came back to the garden to see her children. When she saw the open shirt, she became extremely angry with the boys. The ties were missing. She looked at Tom Kitten and spoke to him with a very big and serious voice. She told them that they had broken her rules and hurt their afternoon. She sent them away to their bedroom. She told them to stay there for the rest of the day. The poor kids had to walk up the steps. They sat quietly in the dark room without toys. They had no way to leave the space because the door was closed behind them. Although the mother had company later that evening, the bad brothers could not join. They had to remain away because they did not follow simple rules about their clothes. The guests came inside the house and enjoyed the evening with the glad mother cat. Meanwhile, the two brothers sat on the bed and thought about their mistakes today. They realized that they should have listened to their parent before going outside to play. Even today, little cats remember that story and try to be good children every single day.

Life in the house returned to normal after the guests finally left for their own homes. The mother cat checked the bedroom. She saw that the boys were resting quietly on the bed. She hoped they had learned a valuable lesson about following rules and keeping clean. The following morning, the sun rose brightly over the green fields and the quiet village. Tom Kitten woke up early and promised himself that he would never break his clothes again. His sister came into the room and smiled at him because everything was calm now. They went down to the kitchen where the mother had prepared a warm breakfast. The family sat together at the table and enjoyed their morning meal without any trouble. From that day forward, the young cats always tried their best to be nice and careful. They knew that good behavior would bring them joy and keep them safe from problems. When visitors came to the house, the brothers welcomed them with happy faces and clean clothes. The mother cat was very proud of her children because they had finally changed their ways. Every single member of the family lived happily together for many years in that lovely house.

### B2
Once upon a time, a mother cat lived in a nice house with three little kids. The kids were very bad animals who did not like to keep their clothes clean. One fine morning, the mother wanted to dress the boys for a party with some company. She put a pretty jacket and nice clothes on Tom Kitten, who was one of the brothers. The mother told the kids that they should walk carefully in the garden and not ruin their clothes. However, Tom Kitten was a silly child who did not listen to good advice. While he was walking around the grass, the buttons on his jacket began to fall off one by one. His sister tried to help him, but the clothes were simply too large for his small body. Soon, another bad animal named Puddle found the bright buttons on the ground. She thought that they were lovely little food items and put them into her mouth. Tom Kitten felt very sad because his clothes were now broken and his special items were missing. He tried to look for them everywhere, but he could not find anything in the grass. After a short time, the mother came back to the garden to see her children. When she saw the plain jacket and the missing buttons, she became extremely angry with the boys. She sent them away to their bedroom and told them to stay there for the rest of the day. The poor kids had to sit quietly in the dark room without any toys to play with. Although the mother had company later that evening, the bad brothers were not able to join the party. They had to remain away because they did not follow simple rules about their clothes. Even today, little cats remember that story and try to be good children every single day.

Tom Kitten was a young cat who lived with his family in a house. He had brothers and a sister who played outside in the garden during the morning. The mother cat wanted to prepare her sons for a nice evening with company. She chose a lovely jacket for Tom Kitten to wear for the special event. She told the young cats that they must not dirty their nice clothes while playing. Tom Kitten went out to the garden and walked upon the green grass. The jacket was too big for his body, and the buttons began to drop. His sister tried to fix the problem, but the buttons fell down onto the soil. A bird came along and saw the bright buttons on the ground. The bird thought the buttons were food and put them into her mouth. Tom Kitten searched everywhere in the grass, but he could not find his things. When the mother returned to the garden, she looked at Tom Kitten and his ruined jacket. She became very angry because the clothes were broken and the buttons were gone. She sent the young cat away to his room high above for the rest of the day. The brothers had to stay inside the dark room without any toys or games. They could not join the party because they failed to follow simple rules. Cats still remember this story today and try to be good children. Each morning starts with wonderful light shining through the window of the house. The mother always hopes her family will grow up to be happy and good. Every single member of the group knows that rules must be followed carefully. When people break rules, bad things happen to them just like Tom Kitten. Therefore, children should always listen to their mothers and keep their clothes clean. Life is much better when everyone respects the family and does what is right. Even if the garden looks fun, young cats must remember to protect their nice things.

The sun shone brightly upon the lovely house where the family lived all year. Tom Kitten often played games with his brothers in the green grass outside. The mother cat looked at the children and hoped they would learn good habits. She wanted every boy to understand that clothes must be kept clean and tidy. If the children had listened to her advice, nothing bad would have happened. However, Tom Kitten did not care about the rules during his morning walk. He ran across the garden while the wind blew softly through the trees. His jacket was too large, which caused the shiny buttons to fall down. A small bird saw the buttons on the ground and picked them up quickly. Tom Kitten looked everywhere for his lost items, but he found nothing at all. When the mother returned, she saw the ruined clothes and became very angry. She sent him upstairs to his bedroom so that he could think about his actions. The poor cat sat in the dark room while the party started downstairs. He knew that he should have been more careful with his special clothes. Now the day was completely ruined because he failed to follow simple instructions. His sister tried to help him earlier, but the damage was already done. Such problems always arise when young people refuse to listen to their parents. The mother spent the rest of the evening with her special company downstairs. Meanwhile, the bad brothers remained quietly in their room without any toys. This famous story is still told to young cats across the country today. Every child who hears the tale learns why rules are important in life. People must always respect their families and keep their personal things safe. If anyone breaks the rules, they will surely face similar problems later. Therefore, everyone should try to be good and do the right thing daily. Life becomes wonderful when children listen and live happily with their mothers.

### C1
Once upon a time, within the welcoming warmth of a thoroughly respectable house, a meticulous mother cat lived alongside her three remarkably spirited little kittens. These young felines possessed an almost boundless energy, viewing the domestic order not as a comfort to be maintained, but as a tedious boundary meant to be tested. They were notoriously careless concerning their attire, showing a complete disregard for the pristine garments provided by their exceedingly patient parent. Morning after morning, she attempted to instill in them a sense of propriety, yet her gentle admonitions consistently drifted away upon the household breeze. It was against this domestic backdrop that a particularly momentous day dawned, promising social obligations that required the utmost sartorial vigilance.

On this specific, sunlit morning, the mother cat intended to receive esteemed company for a rather formal afternoon gathering. Wishing for her sons to appear presentable before the arriving guests, she subjected them to an extensive and deeply resented dressing process. She selected a remarkably pretty jacket for Tom Kitten, one of the more restless brothers, fastening it securely across his diminutive shoulders. The garments were undeniably fine, crafted from rich fabric and adorned with brilliantly polished buttons that caught the morning light. Before releasing them into the fresh air, she offered a stern yet affectionate warning regarding the preservation of their clothes.

She instructed her children to walk with extreme caution along the gravel paths of the enclosed garden, strictly forbidding any rough play. Tom Kitten, however, possessed a mind entirely occupied by the thrilling pursuits of youth rather than the prudent advice of maturity. He trotted out into the dew-kissed grass, feeling the gentle morning wind ruffle the fine fabric of his newly acquired apparel. Unaccustomed to such restriction, his small limbs moved with a jerky impatience that immediately threatened the structural integrity of the outfit. The jacket was regrettably somewhat too large for his slender frame, creating a dangerous slackness in the way the cloth hung.

Scarcely had he taken a dozen steps upon the lawn when the first gleaming button groaned against the sudden tension. With a metallic clink that went almost entirely unnoticed by the distracted kitten, the fastening detached itself from the cloth. It tumbled silently downward, disappearing into the dense carpet of green blades that covered the vibrant garden floor. Not far behind, a second button followed its predecessor, yielding to the relentless momentum of the kitten's frantic leaping and turning. Tom Kitten continued his merry dash across the lawn, entirely oblivious to the gradual dismantling of his immaculate appearance.

Observing the unfolding catastrophe from a nearby stone bench, his sensible sister hurried over to intercede. Recognizing the impending doom of the jacket, she attempted to gather the remaining fastenings and restore them to their rightful places. Her small paws fumbled desperately with the oversized cloth, pulling and smoothing in an urgent bid to rescue her brother from disgrace. Alas, the garments were simply too massive for their tiny bodies to manage, rendering her well-intentioned efforts entirely futile. Before she could secure a single stray piece, the unfortunate buttons slipped entirely from her grasp and vanished into the soil.

Fate, ever eager to complicate matters, soon introduced another resident of the garden into this rapidly developing domestic drama. A curious creature named Puddle, wandering through the overgrown borders, happened to notice the bright metallic objects resting upon the earth. Mistaking the brilliant, reflective surfaces for some novel and delectable form of sustenance, she approached with eager curiosity. Without a moment of hesitation, she scooped the lovely little items into her mouth, tasting the cold metal with mild confusion. Finding them singularly unpalatable yet stubbornly holding onto her discovery, she ambled away, leaving the young kitten entirely destitute.

Tom Kitten suddenly halted his frantic games as a cold realization settled into the pit of his small stomach. He looked down at his chest, discovering a gaping emptiness where the shining ornaments had resided only moments before. A profound sense of sorrow washed over him, turning the bright morning into a scene of absolute desolation. Dropping to his knees among the damp blades of grass, he began searching frantically for the lost treasures. He parted the thick vegetation with trembling paws, peering into every shadowy crevice, but he could find nothing at all.

After a relatively short interval, the front door of the house creaked open, announcing the return of the mother cat. She stepped into the garden to inspect her children before the arrival of her esteemed company, expecting a scene of serene obedience. Instead, her gaze fell upon Tom Kitten, whose fine jacket now hung loose and pitiful, entirely devoid of its distinctive buttons. A terrible silence descended upon the lawn as the full extent of his disobedience registered in her widened, unblinking eyes. The initial shock swiftly transformed into a towering fury that seemed to darken the very sunlight filtering through the trees.

Without uttering a single word of inquiry, she seized the ruined garment and delivered a swift, unyielding verdict upon the offenders. She banished the trembling brothers from the sunlit garden, ordering them immediately to their lonely bedroom high above the ground. The poor children had no choice but to trudge indoors, their heads bowed low under the heavy weight of their mother's wrath. They were confined to a dimly lit room where no toys or games were permitted to alleviate the crushing boredom. There they sat upon the wooden floor, listening miserably to the faint sounds of preparation drifting up from the lower levels.

Downstairs, the mother cat welcomed her sophisticated guests into the parlor, gracefully hosting the evening party as though nothing had occurred. Laughter and pleasant conversation floated through the house, serving as a constant, echoing reminder of the celebration taking place without them. Tom Kitten and his brother remained imprisoned in the dark chamber, regretting the foolish choices that had led to their undoing. They understood now that rules were not merely arbitrary restrictions, but essential boundaries designed to protect them from such misery. Had they listened to the initial advice, they would have been mingling happily below instead of nursing their grievances in solitude.

Even to this day, generations of young cats are raised upon this cautionary tale, learning wisdom through the mistakes of the past. Every morning begins with wonderful light streaming through the windows, offering a fresh chance to demonstrate good character and obedience. The mother cat always hopes that her family will grow up to understand the immense value of respect and careful behavior. For every sensible member of the household knows that when rules are broken, unpleasant consequences inevitably follow. Therefore, wise children always listen to their mothers, keeping their clothes pristine and their lives wonderfully free from needless sorrow.

## st-0185 — How the Leopard Got His Spots
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Long ago, a big creature lives in the land. He lives among high green things. His color is bright like gold. It matches the land and the day. A man also lives there. The man is a worker. The worker wants to find the big creature. Every day, he tries to see him. He always fails. The gold creature stays in the green place. After weeks, the worker has a friend. They change their plan. They go to a water place. The friend says to change their look. They use black color on themselves.

The big creature comes down to water. He sees the two men. He is very interested. He walks close to look. The worker watches every move. He puts his hand on the creature. Each part leaves a black point. The color touches the gold body. The big creature moves back. He goes into the green place. He looks down at his body. He sees many little points. ### C1
Long ago, within a vast and arid expanse where the sun beat down without mercy, a magnificent big cat lived a life of quiet contentment among the high, whispering trees. His sleek hide possessed a smooth, uniform golden color that matched the sun-baked sand and the harsh glare of the midday light. Because he looked precisely like the dry, sun-bleached field, the stealthy predator could capture his daily food with remarkable ease. No creature could spot him against the golden grass until it was far too late to escape his sudden pounce. Life flowed in a predictable, comfortable rhythm for the great cat beneath the endless, shimmering sky.

Not far away, a skilled hunter also lived in that same expansive territory, clad always in garments of a deep, dark brown shade. The man who hunted was famously wise, yet catching the elusive golden animal proved to be an exceptionally difficult problem. Every single day, without fail, the determined man tried to find the wild creature as it roamed the open plains. Yet despite all his patience and tracking skill, he invariably failed to secure his elusive prize. The golden animal remained perfectly hidden while the sun shone brightly across the vast, featureless landscape.

Had the man who hunted been slightly faster or more observant, he might have intercepted the creature much earlier in the season. Instead, the clever beast always seemed to vanish into the shimmering heat waves just before the trap could close. After many weeks of fruitless searching, the man who hunted and a trusted wise friend decided to change their strategy completely. They packed their few belongings and traveled far across the dusty plains until they reached a wonderful, shaded water hole. It was a vital oasis where the thirsty cat frequently came to drink during the hottest hours of the day.

Arriving long before dusk, the wise friend turned to the man who hunted and spoke with quiet authority about their next move. He explained that they must alter their outward appearance entirely if they wanted any hope of achieving success. Without changing how they looked, the keen senses of the great cat would detect them long before they got close enough. The hunter listened carefully to this counsel, knowing that conventional methods had already proven entirely useless against the beast. They gathered their supplies and prepared for the pivotal encounter that would soon unfold at the water's edge.

Opening a small jar they had brought along, they used dark, earthy paint to cover their skin from head to toe. They applied the thick pigment carefully so that the glaring desert light would not reflect off them and reveal their presence. Scarcely had they finished camouflaging themselves when the brush parted and the wild cat stepped out into the open. The magnificent animal came down slowly toward the water, unaware of the two dark figures standing motionless near the trees. Instantly, the cat stopped in his tracks, his amber eyes wide with intense curiosity at the bizarre sight before him.

Never before in all his long days had the creature seen such strange, dark figures moving through his territory. Driven by an irresistible urge to investigate, he walked much closer to examine them with cautious, deliberate steps. The man who hunted stood completely still, watching every single movement of the approaching animal with bated breath. His heart hammered against his ribs, but he maintained his discipline, waiting for the exact right second to act. The cat lowered his head slightly, sniffing the unfamiliar air as he tried to make sense of the painted men.

Suddenly, moving with lightning speed, the man who hunted reached out his hand and pressed his fingers firmly against the warm skin. Each fingertip left a distinct dark spot where the heavy paint transferred directly onto the smooth yellow body. The cat became utterly surprised by the sudden touch, letting out a startled hiss that echoed across the quiet pool. With a powerful bound, he quickly jumped backward, fleeing in panic straight into the safety of the tall field. He did not stop running until he was far away from the strange water hole and the puzzling encounter.

Once he felt safe enough to pause, the confused animal looked down anxiously at his own sleek body. To his absolute astonishment, he saw many dark, round marks covering his back and flanks in a completely new pattern. From that day forward, the great cat was no longer plain yellow, but proudly carried distinct spots everywhere. Back at the oasis, the man who hunted felt a deep sense of satisfaction because his difficult plan had finally worked. The animal had certainly learned a valuable, albeit startling, lesson about remaining overly curious near the water hole.

From that fateful moment on, every single cat of this particular kind carried the exact same dark marks down through the generations. Even now, people still tell this old, cherished story across the hot and dusty country where the events originally took place. Many years passed after that famous event, but the descendants of the great cat never forgot the hard lesson. They always looked around very carefully, checking every shadow before they dared to approach any water hole in the land. Their caution kept them safe from traps and ensured that no hunter would ever catch them quite so easily again.

The men who hunted also remembered vividly how the simple application of paint had changed the appearance of the big cat forever. No one in the local villages ever tried to use that exact trick again, because the method was now universally known. Elders told the tale to every young child who grew up in the region, passing down the wisdom of the past. They explained patiently that clever ideas and adaptability could solve difficult problems when physical strength alone was simply not enough. The painted trick became a legend, whispered around evening fires as a reminder of nature's endless surprises.

As the decades rolled by, the spotted cats grew into a strong, thriving group that easily ruled the green fields and open plains. Their new patterned skin helped them hide much better than before, which made them even more successful hunters than their ancestors. Life went on quietly and peacefully for the animals under the warm, comforting sun of that magnificent region. And so the story remains an important, enduring part of the rich history shared by everyone living in the country today. The legacy of the spots endures, a permanent reminder of a clever plan hatched long ago by an oasis.

## st-0187 — The Cat That Walked by Himself
