Woman explained the agreement. Man said the cat must also hunt mice. The cat agreed to hunt mice, but he always remembered his wild freedom.

When the wild woods call him, the cat goes out alone. He walks through the cold water and plays in the dark night. However, when the weather turns bad, he always returns to the warm house. When the wild woods call him, the cat goes out alone. He walks through the cold water and plays in the dark night. However, when the weather turns bad, he always returns to the warm house.

### B2
Long ago, the world was wild and new. All the animals were wild, and each animal walked by himself. The cat was the wildest of all because he walked by himself. All places were similar to him. He did not care about other animals or people. He just wanted to be free in the wild woods. Every single tree and plant was open for him. He spent his days running through the tall grass. He watched the small birds fly above his head. The world was full of wonderful things to see. He never stayed in one place for too long. He enjoyed the fresh air of the morning. He liked the quiet moments of the dark night. Nothing could stop him from going where he wanted. He was completely free and happy with his life. He found great joy in every single step that he took. No one could tell him what he should do. He lived as he wished without any problem. The sun shone down upon his back while he ran. The wind blew through his soft hair.

Then man came into the dark stone room and lived with woman. Woman made a warm home and lit a bright fire. The wild dog came first, and he agreed to help man. Man promised to give him food in exchange for his help. The dog learned to guard the house during the night. Next came the wild horse, and he also agreed to work for man. He traded his fast running for fresh grass. Then the wild cow came to share her milk with the family. The family was very happy with all these useful animals. They had milk, meat, and protection from danger. Life in the house became much better than before. Every day brought new tasks and new rewards. The man and woman worked hard together. They built a strong fence around their home. They made sure everything was safe and secure. They provided food for every animal that lived there. The house grew larger as more time passed. The children played near the strong fence. They laughed and sang songs while the sun was high. The animals rested in the shade of the trees.

Finally, the smart cat saw everything from a high tree and decided to visit. He walked down slowly and looked into the room. Woman told the cat to leave immediately. She did not want another animal in her clean house. But the cat spoke softly and played a very smart game. He promised that if woman called him a good cat, he would stay by the warm fire forever. Woman agreed to this strange trade. The cat slept in the house during the cold night. He lay down close to the fire and closed his eyes. He felt very comfortable in the warm room. He knew he had made a good choice. The woman smiled when she saw him sleeping there. She thought he looked very nice by the fire. The house was quiet and calm for a long time. The cat was happy with his new sleeping place. He enjoyed the heat that came from the wood. The woman gave him a small piece of meat. He ate the food and made happy sounds with pleasure. The man looked at the cat with great interest. They accepted that the cat would stay.

When man came back, he wondered why the cat was there. Woman explained the agreement to him. Man listened carefully to what she said. Then man said the cat must also hunt mice. The cat agreed to hunt mice whenever they appeared. He promised to keep the food safe from small animals. The cat always remembered his wild freedom. Whenever the wild woods call him, the cat goes out alone. He walks through the wet grass and plays in the dark night. However, when the weather turns bad, he always returns to the warm house. Man and woman welcome him back because he keeps the house safe from small mice. Yet, the cat always remembers that he is the cat who walks by himself. All places are similar to him. He lives both in the wild and in the home. His life is balanced between freedom and comfort. He knows that he can leave whenever he wants. No one can change the heart of the cat. He is happy with his two sides of life in the world. The story of the cat lives on through time. He remains the freest animal of all.

### C1
In the primordial epoch when the earth was still unscarred by civilization, creation existed in a state of wild and pristine isolation. Every living creature navigated the untamed expanse alone, answering only to the ancient imperatives of instinct and solitude. Of all the inhabitants of that nascent world, the cat was by far the most fiercely independent. To his discerning eye, every geographical boundary was entirely interchangeable, and all spaces possessed an identical, inviting emptiness. He harbored no interest in the nascent social structures of his fellow beasts, nor in the distant, plotting figures of mankind.

His sole ambition was to roam unhindered through the primeval woods, where every fern and towering oak offered an open invitation. He spent his diurnal hours darting through the undulating sea of tall grass, tracking the erratic flight of small birds overhead. The terrestrial realm was a boundless tapestry of wonders, yet he refused to anchor his existence to any single coordinate. He welcomed the crisp, invigorating chill of the morning air with the same equanimity as the profound stillness of the moonless night. No external force could dictate his trajectory, for he derived an intense, unalloyed joy from every deliberate step upon the earth.

Then came the pivotal transition when man abandoned the shadowy cavern and stepped out into the open landscape to live with woman. Together, they carved out a domestic sanctuary, sealing the chill of the wilderness beyond a crude but effective perimeter of stacked stones. Within this nascent hearth, woman kindled a bright, steady fire that promised warmth against the encroaching shadows of the dark. It was the wild dog who first breached this boundary, drawn by the undeniable promise of sustenance in exchange for unwavering loyalty. Man promised him abundant scraps, and in return, the dog readily accepted the solemn duty of guarding the perimeter through the night.

Not long after, the wild horse emerged from the distant horizon, compelled by the seductive prospect of effortless nourishment. He willingly traded his blistering speed across the open plains for a dependable supply of fresh, tender grass near the dwelling. Soon followed the wild cow, offering her rich milk to enrich the daily provisions of the newly established family. With each addition, the domestic sphere grew more prosperous, transforming the precarious struggle for survival into a sustainable routine. The family rejoiced in their newfound security, possessing milk, meat, and a protective barrier against the unpredictable dangers outside.

Life within the enclosure steadily improved, marked by an orderly rhythm of sunrise labor and sunset repose. The man and woman worked in tireless harmony, constructing a robust fence that fortified their growing home against the wider world. They ensured that every subservient creature within their domain was adequately fed, fostering a miniature ecosystem of mutual dependence. As seasons turned, the physical footprint of the homestead expanded to accommodate their ambitions and their growing peace. Near the sturdy wooden fence, their children played beneath the afternoon sun, their laughter echoing above the resting animals.

High up in the branches of a spreading oak, the astute cat observed this intricate domestic machinery with cool detachment. Intrigue finally outweighed his disdain, prompting him to descend from his leafy perch and approach the forbidden threshold. He padded silently toward the open doorway, fixing his amber gaze upon the interior where the woman tended the hearth. Instantly, upon catching sight of the intruder, the woman demanded that he remove himself from her meticulously tidy domain. She had no desire to harbor an unpredictable creature who refused to contribute to the collective labor of the household.

Rather than retreating into the woods, the cat deployed a calculated strategy of soft persuasion and gentle charm. He spoke in a low, mesmerizing cadence, proposing a clever bargain that hinged upon a very specific domestic validation. He promised that if the woman would utter the words 'good cat' just once, he would earn the permanent right to sleep by the fire. Intrigued by his audacity and swayed by his gentle demeanor, the woman agreed to the curious and unconventional terms. That very evening, the cat curled himself into a compact shape directly before the hearth, relishing the radiant heat.

He closed his eyes, instantly recognizing the profound comfort of the blazing wood and the soft floor beneath him. A soft smile crossed the woman's face as she watched the formerly wild creature settle into such absolute tranquility. The interior remained hushed and harmonious for hours, undisturbed by the anxieties that plagued the untamed wilderness outside. He expressed his contentment through a deep, rhythmic vibration in his throat that resonated softly in the quiet room. Sensing a worthy addition to the household, the woman offered him a tender morsel of meat, which he accepted with practiced grace.

When the man returned from his grueling labor in the fields, he paused at the threshold, bewildered by the feline interloper. The woman patiently recounted the details of their arrangement, explaining the precise terms of the cat's presence. The man listened intently, nodding slowly as he weighed the practical implications of housing such an independent creature. He stipulated that if the cat was to remain, he must prove his utility by hunting the troublesome mice that infested the grain stores. The cat readily assented, pledging his nocturnal services to protect the family's hard-earned provisions from destructive rodents.

Yet, despite his integration into the domestic fold, the cat never truly relinquished the wild instincts of his ancestors. Whenever the ancient call of the woods reached him through the open air, he would slip out alone into the gathering dusk. He would prowl through the dew-drenched grass and stalk the shadows, reveling in the untamed freedom of his former life. However, no matter how far he roamed, the biting chill of severe weather invariably drove him back to the safety of the hearth. The man and woman always welcomed his return, valuing his silent vigilance against the mice that threatened their winter stores.

Deep within his consciousness, the cat maintained a fierce awareness of his dual identity and his unyielding autonomy. To him, all places remained fundamentally equal, bridging the vast gulf between the savage woods and the civil hearth. His existence became a masterclass in balance, teetering delicately between the intoxicating thrill of liberty and the luxury of comfort. He knew with absolute certainty that he could depart forever at any moment the whim struck his independent mind. No human authority could ever truly tame his spirit, ensuring that he remained forever the cat who walked by himself.

## st-0188 — How the Rhinoceros Got His Skin
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
One day, a Rhinoceros lives. He lives on a land part. He has no body part. He has a thing like a cloth. The cloth has little bits on it. He eats food all day. He eats food good all day. A man comes from a big boat. The man builds a place for life. The man makes a good food good.

The Rhinoceros finds the food. He walks through many big plants. The man leaves the good food. The bad person eats the whole good food. The man sees the place. The place has no food. The man is very bad to him. The Rhinoceros goes home. He wants to stay. The man puts little good bits down. The man puts them under the cloth.

The big person gets up. He feels very bad. He tries to move on a big stone. The bad feeling grows much more. He runs into the water. He turns in the soft earth. The soft earth becomes not soft now. The light is on it. His cloth changes a lot. His body part gets many lines. The Rhinoceros keeps this body part now.

He lives a long time. He is a big person. He looks at the water. He feels good now. The man stays in the place. No food is there again. The Rhinoceros stays away. The day ends well. He has his new lines. He is glad now.

### A2
Once upon a time, an animal lived near the sea. The people called him the Rhinoceros. He had no body cover like other animals. Instead, he wore a common dress. He had no good mind and lived alone. He ate the whole food without asking.

The man returned and looked at his empty place. He was very cross with the animal. The man wanted to stop him from taking things again. He waited until the animal went home to sleep. The clever man gathered old food parts. He also got plant materials. তিনি filled them inside the dress. The animal woke up and felt very different. He tried to hit himself against a rock. The bad feeling only grew much worse. He ran into the cold water. He moved around in the land and water. The land stuck to his large body. It became hard quickly in the hot light. His dress changed completely. This happened because of the land. His body became full of lines. They stayed there always. Every animal now has a big body. This is how the animal got his look. He will remember this day for a long time. The man is going to watch him closely. The animal will never take food again. He is going to eat other things now. The sea is cold and blue. The trees are green and high. The sun is high in the air. The animal stays in the water. He feels much better now. The man goes back to his house. Everything is low and peaceful again. ### B1
Once upon a time, an animal lived on an empty island near the sea. This animal had no skin like other animals. Instead, he wore a coat that went from his back to his head. He had no manners and lived by himself. He ate sweet food all day long. One fine morning, a man from a ship arrived on that island. The man built a place to cook food. The man made a big fire and made sweet food. It smelled wonderful across the water. When the animal smelled the food, he came walking through the trees. He wanted to see what was happening. Because there were no tables, the man had to leave his food on the ground. He went away for a short time. The animal climbed up and ate the entire meal without asking anyone.

After the man returned, he looked at his empty place and was very angry. The man decided that something must be done. He wanted to make sure the animal would never steal again. He waited until the animal went back home to sleep under a tree. Then the man gathered a lot of old food pieces and sweet things. He put them inside the animal coat. When the animal woke up, he felt extremely bad all over his big body. He tried to move against a sharp rock. However, the terrible problem only grew much worse. In order to find relief, the poor animal ran into the cool water. He rolled around in the ground. The earth stuck to his body and dried up quickly in the hot sun.

Because of the dry earth, his body changed completely. The coat was pulled too hard by the dry earth. His skin became full of deep lines and strange shapes. Those shapes stayed there long. From that day until now, every animal has walked around with a thick coat. This happened because he ate a man food. And that is how the funny animal got his skin. If you go to the island, you will see many animals walking around. They have lived there for a very long time. The man has gone back to his home across the sea. The island is now quiet again. No animals steal food from men anymore. They have learned a good lesson from that strange day. Everything is peaceful now. The story remains in our minds always.

### B2
Once upon a time, there lived a certain animal who was called the Rhinoceros on an empty island near the blue sea. This creature had no skin like other animals. Instead, he wore a smooth coat that buttoned up from his tail to his head. He had no manners and lived by himself. He spent all day long eating food. One fine morning, a man from a ship arrived on that island. The man built a place to cook food. The man made a big fire and prepared a very sweet cake which smelled wonderful across the water. When the animal smelled the food, he came walking through the trees to see what was happening.

Because there were no chairs or tables, the man had to leave his food on the ground while he went away. The animal climbed up onto the place and ate the entire cake without asking. After the man returned, he looked at his empty place and was very angry with the animal. The man decided that something must be done so that the creature would never steal again. He waited until the Rhinoceros went back home to sleep under a tree. Then the man gathered a lot of old cake pieces and sweet food which he filled inside the animal coat. When the animal woke up, he felt extremely sick all over his big body. He tried to move himself against a sharp rock, but the terrible problem only grew much worse.

In order to find relief, the poor creature ran into the cool water and rolled around in the earth. The earth stuck to his body and dried up quickly in the hot sun. Because of the dry earth and the old buttons that were pulled too tight, his smooth coat changed completely. His skin became full of deep folds and ugly lines which stayed there forever. From that day until now, every Rhinoceros has walked around with a thick coat because he ate a man food. And that is how the funny animal got his skin. The story of this animal is known by many people who visit the island today.

They often look at the heavy skin and remember the old days when the man made his meal. If the animal had not eaten the food, his skin would have remained smooth and soft. However, the event changed his life forever, and he had to live with the heavy folds. The man who built the place on the island never saw the animal again after that morning. He returned to his ship and sailed away across the blue sea. The island remained quiet and empty for many years following the incident.

Visitors who come to the place today can still see the big animal walking near the water. They always talk about how the skin became so thick and full of lines. It is a strange tale which parents tell their children when they go on holiday together. The children listen with great attention and wonder about the funny creature on the island. They learn that taking food without asking can lead to very surprising results in life. And so the story continues to be shared from one generation to the next across the world.

Many years passed after that single morning on the empty island. The great animal continued to live his quiet life near the blue sea. Other animals would sometimes look at him from far away. They could see the thick folds that covered his body from head to tail. None of them understood why his smooth coat had changed into such heavy skin.

People who sailed past the shore would point at the creature and tell the old story to their friends. They spoke about the man who made the sweet cake and left it on the ground. Every person on the ship listened with wonder as the tale was shared again and again. The memory of the event remained alive in the minds of everyone who visited the region. Children grew up hearing about the silly animal that could not control his appetite. They learned that taking things without permission would always bring trouble in the end.

Even today, tourists arrive on boats to catch a sight of the famous creature near the water. They walk along the sand and take pictures of the thick lines upon his body. Guides tell the story with a smile while the giant animal rests under a green tree. He does not mind the people who watch him from a safe distance. His heavy skin protects him from the sun and keeps him strong throughout the long day.

Life on the island moves slowly for the animal who lost his smooth coat so long ago. The man from the ship never returned to the island to check on his old enemy. Perhaps he lived a long and happy life in another part of the world. Yet his action changed the history of the island and every creature that lived there. The strange event became a tale passed down through families for generations. Whenever someone asks about the heavy folds, the same explanation is always given.

It all began with a sweet cake, a not satisfied animal, and a smart man who wanted teaching. Many people still share this tale today. The island remains a popular place for visitors from around the world. They walk near the water and look for the large animal. The animal continues to live his quiet life under the sun. Every child learns the lesson of the sweet cake and the thick skin.

### C1
Once upon a time, upon an uninhabited island near the endless blue expanse of the sea, there lived a certain animal known as the Rhinoceros. This creature possessed no skin such as other beasts wore, but instead was clad in a smooth, gleaming coat that buttoned up tightly from his stubby tail right to his pointed horn. He had neither manners nor companionship, choosing to live entirely by himself in solitary indifference to the world around him. All day long he did nothing save consume vast quantities of foliage, driven by an insatiable appetite that knew no bounds. His existence was a monotonous routine of grazing until one fine morning when the quiet solitude of his domain was interrupted forever.

A man from a ship arrived on that very shore, stepping out from the foam with a heart full of purpose and a mind for domestic comfort. Without delay, the traveler constructed a temporary hearth of flat stones, creating a sturdy place to cook his daily meals. He kindled a great fire of dry wood, letting the amber flames leap into the salt air while he prepared a magnificent, sweet cake. The pastry was rich with spices and honey, sending a fragrance so intensely wonderful that it drifted far across the gentle water. Borne upon the breeze, the aroma reached the solitary animal deep within the interior of the island, awakening his curiosity.

When the animal smelled this miraculous food, he could not resist its pull and came crashing through the dense tropical trees to investigate. He emerged upon the sunlit beach, his small eyes fixing upon the cooking place where the sweet creation sat cooling in the morning light. Because there were no chairs or tables anywhere to be found, the busy man had been forced to leave his precious meal unguarded on the ground while he walked away. Seizing the opportunity without a moment of hesitation, the creature clambered heavily onto the stone structure. Before the owner could return, the animal had devoured the entire cake in a single greedy swallow, leaving nothing behind.

Moments later, the man returned from his brief absence, only to find his cooking place bare and his culinary labor utterly ruined. He looked down at the empty stones and felt a deep surge of anger toward the magnificent yet intolerable beast standing nearby. Realizing that mild words would be wasted, the man decided that a stern lesson must be administered so the creature would never steal again. He concealed his frustration beneath a calm exterior and waited patiently until the heavy animal wandered back to his shady resting spot. Under the branches of a sprawling green tree, the giant soon fell into a deep, contented slumber, completely unaware of impending retribution.

While the creature slept soundly, the clever man gathered a vast quantity of stale, sticky cake crusts and intensely sweet crumbs from his remaining provisions. Working with quiet determination, he filled every available space inside the animal's unbuttoned coat, packing the sugary mass tightly against the bare hide. He secured the buttons so firmly that the smooth garment strained against the sudden bulk, pressing the sweet mixture directly into the sleeping flesh. When the animal finally stirred and woke from his slumber, he did not feel refreshed as usual, but instead experienced a violent, burning sickness. An unbearable itch spread across his massive body, filling him with a frantic desire for relief that he could neither scratch nor soothe.

Desperate to alleviate his torment, the poor creature rushed blindly toward a jagged stone formation near the edge of the shore. He rubbed his enormous sides against the sharp rock with all his might, hoping to scratch away the misery hidden beneath his clothes. However, this desperate action only made the terrible problem infinitely worse, grinding the sticky sugar deeper into his sensitive skin. Finding no comfort against the stone, the suffering beast plunged straight into the cool water and thrashed about in the wet earth. The damp soil clung fiercely to his heated body, baking rapidly under the relentless rays of the tropical sun until it formed a hard crust.

Under the combined pressure of the dried earth and the old buttons that were now pulled far too tight, his entire appearance changed. The once smooth and gleaming coat was stretched beyond endurance, warping into a permanent prison of thick, heavy folds. Deep lines and overlapping ridges formed across his shoulders and flanks, staying fixed in place forever as the transformation became complete. From that fateful day until the present era, every single rhinoceros has walked the earth wearing this heavy, wrinkled armor as a permanent reminder. All of this occurred simply because a creature with no manners chose to consume a meal that belonged to someone else.

The story of this strange transformation is still known to nearly every traveler who visits the island today. Tourists often stare at the heavy, folded skin and reminisce about the morning when the stranger baked his famous meal. If the animal had resisted temptation, his coat would surely have remained smooth and soft through all the passing years. Instead, the singular event altered his destiny forever, condemning him to bear the heavy burden of his own greed. As for the man who built the cooking place, he never saw the animal again after that eventful morning, choosing to sail away forever.

He returned to his vessel and raised the anchor, gliding away across the blue sea toward distant lands unknown. The island remained quiet and peaceful for many years following the incident, undisturbed save for the wind and the crashing waves. Yet the memory of what happened lingered on the breeze, whispered from one generation of travelers to the next. Visitors walking along the shore today can still catch a glimpse of the heavy animal resting lazily near the water's edge. Guides point toward the giant with a knowing smile, recounting the tale to every newcomer who steps onto the warm sand.

Children listen with wide eyes and breathless attention, pondering the strange fate of the creature who loved sweets too much. They learn the timeless lesson that taking things without permission often leads to surprising and permanent consequences in life. The animal himself does not mind the spectators who watch him from a safe distance, remaining entirely unbothered by their presence. His thick, folded skin protects him efficiently from the harsh sun and keeps him comfortable throughout the long tropical day. Life on the island moves with a slow, unchanging rhythm for the beast who lost his smooth coat so many centuries ago.

Tourists continue to arrive by boat, eager to photograph the remarkable lines and deep ridges upon the giant's broad back. Parents tell the amusing legend to their children as they walk together along the sparkling coastline at sunset. It all began with a simple sweet cake, an unsatisfied appetite, and a clever traveler who believed in practical instruction. The island remains a cherished destination for curious minds from all around the world who seek to witness living history. And so the tale is preserved, living on in the hearts of everyone who hears the story of the thick skin.

## st-0189 — The Beginning of the Armadillos
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Once, a little person lives. He is very different. He likes the world. He stays near the water. He likes to play. Another small person lives on land. They meet every day. They play near the water. The water is clear. The day is fine. They feel very good.

One morning, a man comes. He has a big plan. He wants them. They run from him. They move very fast. They go from him. The man can not reach them. He feels very bad. He decides to ask for help. He wants to get them. He looks around the place.

A mother appears. She looks at the situation. She knows they need help. She wants them to live. She changes their body. The body becomes strong. She helps the little person. She makes a new plan. The small person is secure now. The mother feels very good.

The body covers the whole person. The man tries to reach them. He finds a hard place. It is like a house. They are good now. The man cannot hit them. The body is very hard. They stay in the hard place.

### B1
Once upon a time, there lived a little animal who was very interested in the world. This small animal had soft skin and spent much time near the water, where he liked to swim. He swam every day and enjoyed the cool water during the hot summer months. Close by, another small animal lived on the land, and they often met during the day. They played together in the open fields and talked about many different things. The forest was large and full of green trees and tall plants. They had lived there for a long time without any trouble or fear.

One morning, a wise man came down to the water with a big plan. He wanted to catch the little animals, but they were too fast and could easily hide. The man tried to take them with his hands, but they ran away very quickly. Because the man could not reach them, he felt very sad and angry. He did not know what to do next to catch them. He decided to ask for help from someone who knew the forest well. He hoped that somebody could give him a good idea.

Soon, an old mother appeared and looked at the situation with great care. She knew that the little animals needed protection if they were going to survive. She spoke to the man and told him to leave the animals alone. Then she turned to the small animals and smiled at them quietly. Therefore, she used her power to change their skin into something much harder and stronger. She wanted them to be safe from any danger that might come near them. The change started immediately while the sun was bright above.

The soft skin slowly became a hard cover, which could protect their whole body completely. If the man tried to touch them again, he would only find a tough stone surface. Their skin felt like heavy rock when the man pressed his fingers against it. Thus, they were completely safe from any danger that might come near them. They did not need to run away now because the hard cover protected them well. The man saw the change and realized he could not hurt them. He turned around and walked back home without the animals.

From that day forward, the animals became the first small covered beings of the world. They could walk slowly across the land or roll into a ball when they felt afraid. When danger came, they closed themselves inside the hard cover and stayed quiet. Everyone in the forest was happy because the little animals finally had a wonderful home. The birds sang sweet songs in the trees above them. The other forest animals came to see the new cover and liked them very much. They have lived in peace ever since that wonderful day.

### B2
Once upon a time, there lived a little creature who was very unique and curious about the world. This animal had soft skin and spent much time near the water. He liked to swim and play there. Close by, another small creature lived on the land. They often met each other during the day. Every single morning, they enjoyed their quiet life near the river without any trouble. They could jump and run across the green grass as they explored every part of the forest. The sun shone brightly upon the trees. The water looked like silver because the sun shone upon the trees. They felt very happy because their home was full of food and safety. No danger could reach them while they played together in the warm sun. They always shared everything they found. It did not matter if it was fresh water or small plants. Such a wonderful friendship lasted for a long time without any dark clouds above them. They spent hours playing by the river. The green trees provided good shade from the bright sun. Every single plant grew well because the water was clean. They felt safe in the forest. No other animal bothered them. Their daily life was full of peace and quiet joy. They ran through the grass and jumped over small rocks. The warm wind blew gently through the leaves. They never felt tired of playing together. Every moment was special.

One bright morning, a normal man came down to the water with a big plan. He wanted to catch the little creatures. They were too fast and could easily hide from him. Because the man could not reach them, he felt very upset and decided to ask for help. He walked along the river bank and looked everywhere for the animals. His mind was set on capturing them before the afternoon sun reached the sky. However, the little animals were much faster than he expected them to be. They ran into the deep green plants where the man could not follow them at all. The man tried to catch them again. He failed completely every single time. He stood by the water and thought about what he should do next. His big plan was not working. Time was running out very quickly. The man looked around the river bank with great frustration. He wanted to take the small creatures back to his home. He spent hours searching every part of the tall grass. His efforts produced no results. The clever animals always stayed one step ahead of him. The man sat down by a large tree to rest his feet. He wondered how they could move so fast. He tried to think of a new way to catch them. Nothing came to his mind. The sun began to move across the sky. He knew that he must act soon if he wanted to succeed.

Soon, a wise mother appeared and looked at the situation with great care. She knew that the little animals needed protection if they were going to survive in the wild. Therefore, she used her magic to change their skin into something much harder and stronger. The wise mother spoke softly to the little creatures so they would not feel afraid. She told them that everything would be alright and that they should stay calm. With a wave of her hand, the magic began to work upon their bodies. The soft skin slowly became a hard shell. This hard shell could cover their whole body completely. If the man tried to touch them again, he would only find a tough stone-like surface instead of soft animal skin. Thus, they were completely safe from any danger that might come near them. The mother smiled as she watched the amazing change take place right before her eyes. She knew that her magic would protect them from any future trouble. The little creatures looked at their new bodies with great surprise. They felt different, but they also felt very strong. The wise mother told them that they no longer needed to fear the man. She advised them to use their new power wisely. They thanked the mother for her great help. The mother nodded and smiled gently before she left the area. The creatures felt ready to face whatever might happen next. Their lives had changed forever. They tested their new shells by moving around the soft ground. Everything felt secure. The magic had worked perfectly. They were safe.

From that day forward, the animals became the first tiny shell-bearing creatures of the world. They could walk slowly across the land or roll into a ball whenever they felt afraid. Everyone in the forest was happy because the little creatures finally had a wonderful home and protection. The man tried to touch them once more. His hands only found the hard shell. He realized that he could no longer hurt them. So, he walked away from the river. The little creatures celebrated their new life with great joy and dancing. They rolled into balls whenever they heard a loud noise or felt the wind blow too hard. Nothing in the forest could break their new protection, no matter how strong it was. Generations passed by, and the animals continued to live in safety. Their story became a famous ancient tale among all the creatures who lived in the green woods. The man never returned to bother them again. He found other things to do with his time. The little creatures grew and multiplied in peace. Their children learned how to roll into balls and protect themselves. The forest remained a peaceful place for all living things. Water flowed smoothly down the river every single day. The sun continued to shine upon the green trees. Friendship and safety ruled the woods. No danger could ever break their hard shells. They lived happily through every season of the year. The ancient tale was told to every new generation. Everyone remembered the wise mother and her great magic.

### C1
Once upon a time, in an age when the world was remarkably unhurried, there lived a creature of singular design who possessed an insatiable curiosity about the vast expanse around him. Gifted with skin as soft as velvet, he spent his hours lingering near the languid waters of a sun-drenched river, finding immense pleasure in swimming and aquatic play. Hardly a stone's throw away, another small creature made her home upon the firm earth, establishing a companionable rhythm with her aquatic neighbor. Each morning heralded a tranquil existence along the meandering riverbanks, free from any shadow of trouble or anxiety. Together, they would bound across the emerald grass, exploring every hidden nook of the forest while the sun cast a brilliant golden canopy over the towering trees.

Reflected in the gentle current, the sunlight turned the flowing water into a shimmering expanse of liquid silver that mirrored the cloudless sky. A profound sense of contentment settled over them, sustained by a habitat overflowing with nourishment and absolute safety. No looming danger could penetrate the sanctuary they shared as they basked in the warmth, celebrating their companionship without a care in the world. Whatever bounty they discovered was invariably shared, whether it was a pool of crystal water or tender shoots of vegetation sprouting from the damp soil. Such an idyllic friendship endured for months and years, untouched by any storm, anchoring their lives in a peaceful harmony of quiet joy.

They chased one another through the tall grass, leaping effortlessly over weathered rocks while a warm breeze whispered softly through the overhead leaves. Never once did they grow weary of each other's company, for every fleeting moment held a distinct and treasured magic. The green canopy offered cool shelter from the afternoon heat, ensuring that their playground remained comfortable and inviting. Every plant flourished in the pristine environment, nourished by water so pure it seemed almost enchanted. Unburdened by the worries of a larger world, they moved through the landscape with a carefree grace that defined their entire existence.

Then came a morning that shattered the quiet routine, heralded by the sudden arrival of a man bearing a very definite and ambitious plan. Armed with the intention of capturing the two small creatures, he descended upon the riverbank with determined strides. To his mounting frustration, the animals proved frustratingly agile, slipping away into the dense thickets before he could even close the distance. No matter how quickly he moved, the creatures remained elusive, vanishing into the greenery whenever he drew near. He attempted to corner them again and again, yet failure met every calculated move he made along the muddy banks.

Standing by the water, the man paused to reconsider his strategy as precious minutes slipped away beneath the climbing sun. His grand design was steadily unravelling, leaving him stranded in the tall grass with nothing to show for his exhausting pursuit. He searched every foot of the riverbank, his eyes scanning the foliage for the slightest twitch of movement, but the clever animals stayed persistently out of reach. Weary from the fruitless chase, he finally retreated to the shade of a large tree to rest his aching feet and ponder a new approach. Nothing feasible came to mind, and as the sun arched higher into the sky, he knew he had to act decisively if he wanted to succeed.

Just as the tension reached its peak, a wise mother materialized from the quiet woods, observing the chaotic scene with profound perception. She understood implicitly that the tiny creatures required staunch protection if they were to navigate the unpredictable hazards of the wild. Without hesitation, she decided to employ her considerable magic, intending to transform their vulnerable skin into something remarkably resilient. Approaching the frightened animals, she spoke in soothing tones to dispel their anxiety and reassure them of their impending safety. With a deliberate wave of her hand, the ancient transformation began, altering the very fabric of their physical forms.

Under the influence of the spell, their soft skin slowly hardened, thickening into an impenetrable armor that encased their entire bodies. Should the man attempt to touch them again, his searching fingers would encounter only a tough, stone-like surface instead of yielding flesh. Thus shielded, the creatures were rendered completely immune to any peril that might loom in their forest home. A gentle smile crossed the mother's face as she watched the astonishing metamorphosis unfold right before her eyes. She knew with absolute certainty that this enduring gift would guard them against any future trials.

The little creatures stared at their altered bodies in profound astonishment, feeling a strange new weight and an unprecedented strength. The wise mother reassured them that the man no longer posed a threat, advising them simply to utilize their newfound resilience with care. Overwhelmed with gratitude, they thanked her profusely for her timely intervention and her boundless wisdom. With a parting nod and a warm smile, the mother drifted back into the forest shadows, leaving them equipped for whatever lay ahead. They tested their new shells by shuffling across the forest floor, discovering that every step felt secure and remarkably stable.

From that remarkable day onward, they became the very first shell-bearing creatures to walk the surface of the earth. They could now traverse the land at a leisurely pace or curl tightly into impenetrable spheres whenever a sudden fear gripped them. News of the transformation rippled through the woods, bringing a quiet satisfaction to all the inhabitants who valued peace. When the man made one final attempt to capture them, his hands met only cold, unyielding shell. Realizing the futility of his efforts, he turned away from the river for good, abandoning his quest to master the forest.

In celebration of their deliverance, the creatures danced upon the mossy ground, rejoicing in the security of their new lives. Whenever a loud noise echoed through the trees or a fierce wind battered the branches, they simply rolled into tight balls and waited out the disturbance. No force in the forest, regardless of its strength, could penetrate their sturdy new defense. As generations passed, the resilient creatures continued to multiply in safety, passing down the secrets of their protective posture to their offspring. The river continued its smooth descent toward the horizon, while the sun warmed the green canopy above just as it had always done.

The dramatic events of that morning gradually crystallized into an ancient legend told among the diverse inhabitants of the woods. The man never returned to disturb the tranquil waters, having found entirely different pursuits to occupy his days. Friendship and safety reasserted their dominion over the landscape, guiding the creatures through every shifting season of the year. No lurking danger could ever breach the hard shells that guarded their peaceful community. Thus, the story of the wise mother and her wondrous gift lived on, remembered and retold by every new generation that called the forest home.

## st-0190 — Aladdin and the Wonderful Lamp
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
One young child lives in a large country. He lives with his mother. They have little money. Life is hard for them. One day, a man comes to them. The man calls the child family. The man wants to go far away.

They walk to a high place. The man shows a big rock to him. The child moves the rock. A small room is under it. The man tells the child to go down. The child must bring an old fire source. The child goes down into the room.

He finds the old fire source. The man wants only that thing. The child comes to the top. The man asks for the fire source. The child is not glad. He keeps the fire source.

The man puts the rock back. The child is in the room. He sits and starts to cry. His hand touches the fire source. A big power friend appears. The friend gives light.

The friend says he helps his leader. The child asks to go home. He is back with his mother. The child uses the power. His life changes now. They get good food.

They have a great new house. The child grows up. People love him around the city. He loves a woman. They live a good life. The power keeps them safe every day.

### A2

The man called the boy his family member. The man wanted the boy to go with him. They went far away together.

They walked for a long time. They reached a place near the high land. The man showed the boy a big rock. The rock was on the ground. The man asked him to move it. Under the rock, there was a small room. The room had an old step inside. The man told the boy to bring back an old light source.

The boy went down into the room. The boy found the light source. The room was dark. He saw many things in the room. The man only wanted the light source. The boy came to the top of the step. The man asked for the light source immediately. The boy was not sure. He kept the light source. He said he would give it after leaving the space.

The man became very bad. The man pushed the big rock back over the space. He left the boy there alone. The poor boy sat in the dark. He began to make a sound like an unhappy voice. He felt so bad. His hand touched the side of बचाने the light source. The light source was made of solid matter. Suddenly, a huge person appeared. The person brought a high light.

The great person told the boy he would do anything. The boy asked the person to help him return home. The boy was back at his house in a moment. His mother was there too. From that day, the boy used the light source to change life. With the person, they received nice food. They received fine clothes and a great home. The boy grew up into a happy man.

People all around the city loved him. He married a fine woman. They lived a good life together. The light source always kept them well.

### B1

### B2
Once upon a time, a young boy lived in a large country with his mother. They had very little money, and life was hard for both of them. One day, a strange man came to the house and called the boy his lost family member. The man wanted the boy to go with him to a secret place far away. They walked for a long time until they reached a remote mountain. The man showed the boy a heavy stone on the ground and asked him to move it. However, those bad memories faded away because his new life was full of joy. The power light remained in a safe place inside the house where nobody could find it. Sometimes the man would look at the metal light and think about the strange adventure. He knew that his future was secure because the powerful spirit served him well. His mother often told him stories about how they survived the hard times. They remembered their lucky stars every single morning and every single night.

No danger could ever touch them while the power light was near. The city grew larger around them, but their home remained a happy island of peace. Friends came and went, bringing joy and laughter to the household. The man and his wife raised children who also learned to appreciate the wonderful life they had. Every member of the family knew that love was the most important thing in the world. And so, they continued to live happily until the very end of their days. They were always glad for the amazing power that had saved them. Life brought many different days, but their home stayed filled with peace and light. The children grew up well and understood the value of hard work. Nobody in the family ever worried about money again. The spirit was always there to protect them from any trouble. Peace and joy filled every single moment of their shared journey.

### C1
In a sprawling, sun-baked kingdom where poverty cast long shadows across modest doorways, a young boy named Aladdin lived with his weary mother. Their days were defined by quiet desperation, for their meager savings vanished almost as quickly as they were earned. While other children chased dreams through bustling marketplaces, Aladdin spent his hours wandering aimlessly, burdened by the heavy weight of unfulfilled potential. His mother could only sigh when she looked into his eyes, wishing fortune might somehow smile upon their fragile household. They had learned to endure the bitter cold of winter and the stifling heat of summer with very little complaint, accepting hardship as their unchanging fate.

One afternoon, a stranger with sharp eyes and an imposing demeanor arrived at their threshold, claiming to be a long-lost uncle. The man spoke with honeyed urgency, showering the astonished mother with promises of a grand future and boundless wealth for her son. Aladdin, captivated by the foreign traveler's confident bearing and the lavish tales of distant lands, agreed without hesitation to accompany him. They walked for hours, leaving the familiar hum of the city far behind as the terrain grew increasingly desolate and forbidding. By the time the afternoon sun began to dip below the horizon, they reached a remote mountain pass where the wind howled like a lost creature.

Stopping before a massive slab of stone embedded in the earth, the stranger commanded Aladdin to lift it with all his strength. Though his muscles strained against the cold rock, the boy managed to roll the heavy boulder aside, revealing a dark subterranean opening. Beneath the stone, a narrow flight of stone steps vanished into absolute blackness, carrying with it a strange, damp chill. The man instructed Aladdin to descend into the cavity and retrieve a simple, tarnished metal lamp from an inner chamber. He warned the boy not to touch anything else, though tantalizing glimpses of glittering treasures could be seen half-buried in the dust.

Hesitant yet obedient, Aladdin groped his way down the slippery steps and stepped into a chamber that smelled of ancient earth. There, resting quietly among heaps of sparkling gems and golden coins, he spotted the dusty old lamp that the stranger desired above all else. He picked it up, feeling its surprising weight, and then hastily gathered a few bright stones just to test their value. With the lamp clutched tightly in his hand, he climbed back toward the pale square of light at the top of the stairs. When his head finally emerged from the opening, he held out the lamp, expecting a warm welcome from his supposed uncle.

Instead of offering a helping hand, the stranger demanded the lamp with a fierce, trembling desperation in his voice. Intimidated by the man's sudden fury and the dark look in his eyes, Aladdin hesitated and stated he would hand it over only after escaping the hole. A terrible rage twisted the stranger's features, and without a second thought, he shoved the heavy stone back into place. Plunged into absolute darkness and trapped far beneath the surface of the earth, Aladdin felt a suffocating wave of despair wash over him. He curled into a tight ball against the cold damp wall, weeping softly as the realization of his betrayal settled heavily upon his chest.

In his distress, his trembling fingers brushed blindly against the rough metal surface of the old lamp, accidentally rubbing its side. Without warning, the air grew violently warm, and a blinding light filled the subterranean space as a colossal figure materialized. Towering above the boy, the smoke-shrouded spirit declared in a voice like rolling thunder that he was bound to serve whoever held the lamp. Aladdin, staring in utter disbelief at the apparition, found his voice and commanded the powerful entity to bring him home immediately. In less than a heartbeat, the oppressive darkness vanished, and Aladdin found himself standing safely in the courtyard of his own modest house.

His mother rushed out to embrace him, weeping tears of relief as she gazed upon her long-absent son. From that fateful day forward, the trajectory of their existence shifted entirely, guided by the unseen power residing within the battered metal vessel. With a single quiet command, the spirit provided lavish meals, luxurious garments, and eventually a magnificent new residence fit for nobility. Aladdin grew from a neglected, aimless youth into a confident and gracious man whose generosity won the affection of the entire city. He eventually married a remarkably kind woman, and together they cultivated a harmonious life built upon genuine mutual respect and quiet joy.

Years drifted by peacefully, yet the memory of the dark mountain cave remained vividly etched in the corners of Aladdin's mind. He frequently reminded himself of the perils of trusting strangers too easily, keeping the miraculous lamp hidden securely away from prying eyes. Friends and neighbors often visited their spacious courtyard, sharing laughter and abundant food while marveling at the family's sudden fortune. His mother often recounted the tale of their miraculous turnaround, marveling at how drastically their fortunes had been altered by a single stroke of luck. Whenever shadows threatened to creep into their thoughts, the abiding warmth of their happy home quickly dispelled any lingering anxieties.

No external threat could penetrate the quiet sanctuary they had built, for the loyal spirit remained eternally vigilant. The city around them expanded and bustled with endless activity, but their household remained an island of profound tranquility. Their children grew up with a healthy appreciation for both comfort and humility, taught by their parents that character mattered above all possessions. Whenever challenges arose, Aladdin simply consulted the dependable entity, ensuring that obstacles were cleared before they could cause harm. They moved through life with steady grace, deeply grateful for the strange destiny that had plucked them from poverty and despair.

As time marched onward, the household settled into a rhythm of enduring contentment that seemed entirely immune to worldly troubles. Aladdin's children learned the value of hard work and empathy, carrying forward the lessons of their grandmother's early struggles. The miraculous lamp rested silently in its hidden alcove, a quiet guardian of a legacy built on love and resilience. Every morning brought fresh sunlight to their courtyard, and every evening offered a peaceful respite filled with shared stories and laughter. They understood that while fortune was fickle, the bonds they forged together were strong enough to withstand any trial.

Thus, they continued their journey through life hand in hand, anchored by the memory where it all began. The past remained a distant teacher, while the present was a vibrant tapestry woven from kindness, security, and quiet joy. Peace and light filled every corner of their days, leaving no room for regret or fear. And so they lived out their remaining years in perfect harmony, treasuring the miraculous gift that had saved them. Their story lived on in the hearts of those they helped, a timeless testament to hope found in the darkest places.

Every person in the community knew about the smart woman who defeated the group of bad men. No bad men ever returned to bother the family or take their money away again. Ali Baba often looked back at the past and felt grateful for his wonderful life. The house stayed warm and bright throughout every season of the year.

Sometimes the children asked questions about the day when their uncle was lost in the room. Ali Baba explained how important it was to be careful and true in this world. They listened with great attention while the old man shared his stories of the past. Morgiana usually smiled when she heard the children talk about the famous adventure. Peace remained inside the walls of the house because the bad men were gone forever. The memory of those difficult days slowly faded away as new happy moments arrived. Ali Baba spent his final years surrounded by people who loved and respected him deeply. His name was remembered by everyone in the town as a symbol of good fortune.

Many days passed after that moment. The family lived in peace. Ali Baba took care of his children. Morgiana helped every day. The money was safe. The town was quiet. Nobody came to bother them. The sun shone brightly. The house was warm. They had enough food. Life was good. The children grew up well. Ali Baba smiled. Morgiana worked hard. Everything was fine. The story of the secret place became a local tale. People talked about the smart woman. Ali Baba thanked her again. They shared many meals together. The doors were locked at night. No danger could reach them. The years went by quickly. The family stayed together. Joy filled the rooms. Peace stayed in their hearts. The old man rested. The young ones played. The world outside was large. Their home was a safe harbor. They remembered the past. They looked to the future. Nothing could break their happiness. Ali Baba knew he was lucky. Morgiana was always there. The town remained peaceful. The story ends here.

### C1
Ali Baba was a man of modest means who inhabited a dusty, sun-bleached town nestled at the edge of the great mountains. His days were spent gathering firewood among the jagged peaks, a laborious existence that offered little more than meager sustenance for his wife and children. One afternoon, while he was resting beneath the sprawling branches of an ancient cedar tree, the ground began to tremble beneath the thunderous approach of horsemen. Concealed among the dense foliage above, Ali Baba watched in breathless silence as forty heavily armed figures dismounted before a sheer granite cliff. Their leader, a formidable man with eyes like flint, stepped toward the stone wall and uttered a strange, rhythmic formula of words.

To the utter astonishment of the hidden woodcutter, the solid rock parted with a grinding groan, revealing a vast, glittering cavern stuffed with stolen riches. The bandits hauled heavy sacks of gold and precious gems into the subterranean vault before sealing the entrance with the same enigmatic incantation. Once the echoes of their departure faded into the vast mountain silence, Ali Baba descended from his perch with trembling limbs. Standing before the monolithic barrier, he recalled the precise cadence of the phrase and whispered the magic words into the wind. The stone slid open once more, and though fear clawed at his chest, curiosity drew him inside the dimly lit treasury.

Fascinated by the mountains of gold, Ali Baba filled a few modest sacks with coins, intending only to ease the crushing poverty of his household. He had not anticipated, however, that his brother, a man consumed by boundless greed and a desperate hunger for status, would notice his sudden, quiet prosperity. Driven by relentless jealousy, the wealthy brother forced Ali Baba to confess the secret location and rushed to the mountain with a string of heavily laden mules. He easily opened the stone door with the acquired words and feasted his eyes upon the unimaginable wealth scattered across the cavern floor. Intoxicated by the gleaming piles, he began gathering immense heaps of treasure, losing all sense of time and caution.

When the heavy stone door began to grind open again, the unfortunate brother panicked, completely blanking on the magical phrases he had memorized so hastily. Trapped by his own forgetfulness, he was discovered by the returning bandits, who showed no mercy to the intruder. It was not long before the ruthless leader of the thieves discovered that another person possessed knowledge of their hidden lair. Determined to protect their illicit sanctuary, the leader vowed to hunt down the man who had dared to plunder their hoard. Disguised as ordinary merchants driving a caravan of large oil jars, thirty-nine thieves slipped quietly into the town under the cover of dusk.

The cunning leader knocked upon Ali Baba's wooden gate, spinning a false tale of a weary traveler seeking shelter for the night with his cargo of oil. Ali Baba, ever hospitable and unsuspecting, welcomed the strangers into his courtyard and offered them food and a place to rest. Fortunately, a remarkably sharp-witted servant woman named Morgiana had been observing the peculiar caravan with mounting suspicion. Sensing an ominous threat lingering in the cool night air, she decided to investigate the large wooden vessels resting in the dark courtyard. Moving like a shadow through the silent house, she carried a small oil lamp and approached the first of the massive storage containers.

Peering over the rim of the vessel, she found herself staring directly into the cold, hardened eyes of an armed bandit waiting for a signal. With astonishing presence of mind, Morgiana realized that thirty-eight other men were concealed inside the remaining jars, poised to strike at midnight. Instead of panicking, she devised a swift and decisive countermeasure to neutralize the mortal danger hanging over her master's roof. She heated a large quantity of oil in the kitchen hearth and systematically poured the boiling liquid into every single container. Her brilliant and courageous intervention completely dismantled the bandits' plot before they could ever draw a weapon against the sleeping household.

When the thwarted leader finally realized his entire force had been destroyed, he fled into the night, abandoning any hope of revenge. Ali Baba was deeply shaken when he learned of the narrow escape, and he expressed his profound gratitude to the remarkable woman who had saved them all. From that day onward, a profound sense of security settled over the household, replacing the lingering shadows of fear with genuine warmth. The gold brought from the mountain provided comfortably for the family, yet they remained humble and mindful of the lessons taught by hardship. Ali Baba often sat in the sunlit courtyard, watching his children play and reflecting upon the strange turns of fate that had blessed his life.

Neighbors in the town began to speak of the modest woodcutter with immense respect, though few knew the full extent of the dangerous events that had transpired. Morgiana continued her daily duties with a steady hand and a joyful heart, treated less like a servant and more like a cherished member of the family. Years rolled by in a peaceful rhythm, punctuated by the cheerful laughter of growing children and the quiet contentment of quiet evenings. The heavy wooden doors were securely bolted each night, not out of paralyzing fear, but as a sensible precaution in a large and unpredictable world. Whenever the younger children asked about the old stories of the mountain and the mysterious cave, Ali Baba answered with patience and wisdom.

He emphasized the importance of honesty, caution, and the kind heart that had ultimately brought them such a fortunate destiny. Morgiana would often smile quietly from the doorway, listening to the familiar tale with the quiet satisfaction of a protector who had done her duty well. The memory of the menacing thieves faded slowly into the realm of local legend, remembered only as a distant storm that had broken and cleared. Inside the walls of the house, peace reigned supreme, filling every room with a lasting brightness that defied the passing of the seasons. Ali Baba spent his twilight years surrounded by those he loved, knowing that true wealth lay not in subterranean gold, but in loyalty and safety.

The sun continued to rise each morning over the quiet town, casting a golden light upon the rooftops and the distant, silent peaks. Nothing occurred to disturb the tranquility of their existence, and the family enjoyed an unbroken era of harmony and quiet joy. Ali Baba often looked back at his earlier struggles with a sense of wonder, marveling at how a poor woodcutter had found such enduring peace. The house remained a safe harbor against the uncertainties of life, anchored by the courage of a remarkable woman and the kindness of a grateful master. And so the days flowed onward like a gentle river, carrying the happy household into a bright and untroubled future.
