## st-0192 — The Voyages of Sinbad (tek sefer)
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Sinbad lives in a big city. He wants a great life. He joins a group of men. They go on a big place. They move on a large home. They go through the water. The long day is very good. They stay on the water.

The head man stops near land. It looks like a part of land. The people go down to walk. Sinbad stays under a big tree. His friends look around the place. The land moves with great force. The land is a still body. It opens up from day light. People run back toward the water. The thing goes down into water.

Sinbad stays on the land. The big home goes away. He has no food or money. A large thing moves down. It carries him to a high place. Sinbad finds good things there. He gives them to other people. He builds a new life. He returns home to his family. He is well to be right now.

He has a good time every day. He looks at the water and land. He feels very well at home. His family is very well too. They live well in the big city. Sinbad does not go away again. He stays with his family always.

### A2
Sinbad lived a good life in a big city. He wanted a great experience. On one early morning, he joined a group. He joined men in a large room. They went across the blue water. They traveled for many weeks. The trip was very quiet and fine. Sinbad had a good time with friends. They talked about the future every day. The leader dropped something near land. The land looked very nice. People went down to walk. They wanted to enjoy good food. Sinbad rested under a big tree. His friends looked around the area.

Suddenly, the ground began to move. The place was a huge sleeping animal. It moved from the hot fire. People started to shout out and run. They ran toward the water. The animal went down under the sea. Sinbad stayed behind on the land. His group went away across the water. He felt very worried about life. He had no food. He had no way home. A large animal flew down from above. It carried him to another high place.

From that place, Sinbad found value. He traded items with other men. He got more money. He built a new life in the area. He finally returned home to family. He brought great stories. He was happy to be well again. Sinbad would remember this event. He would remember for many years. He would tell children about the animal. Everyone in the city listened. They thought it was a great experience. Sinbad stayed home. He never went back to the sea.

### B1
Sinbad lived a comfortable life in a big city. He wanted a great trip. One bright morning, he joined business friends and boarded a large ship. They traveled across the blue water for many weeks. The journey was very quiet and calm. Sinbad felt happy to see the world. He had money and food for the long days ahead. Many people were on the ship with him. They talked and laughed during the morning hours. The weather was nice and the sky was clear. Sinbad thought that everything was completely fine. He loved the feeling of the wind. The large ship moved well through the deep water.

The man in charge dropped a heavy metal tool near a strange island. The island looked like green land. Passengers went off the ship to walk on the ground. They wanted to enjoy fresh food and rest. Sinbad rested under a tree while friends explored the beautiful beach. They walked around and looked at the trees. Some people made a small fire to stay warm. The day was long and full of fun. Sinbad sat down and closed his eyes for a moment. He did not know what would happen next. The friends talked about their home and family. Everyone felt safe on the land.

Suddenly, the ground began to move under them with great force. The island was actually a huge sleeping animal. It woke up from the heat of their fire. People started to shout and run back toward the water. The big animal went down into the sea. Sinbad was left behind on the land while his ship traveled quickly away. He felt very worried because he had no food and no way to return home. He looked around and saw only water and sky. The heavy waves hit the land hard. Sinbad did not know what he should do now. He wished that he was safe with his family. The big ship was already far away from the land.

However, a large bird flew down from the sky. The animal carried him safely. It took him to a high mountain. Sinbad stood on the high rock and looked down. He thanked the bird for its help. The wind blew cold air across the mountain top. Sinbad walked slowly to find a safe place. He had no food or water left. He hoped that he would find something good. The sun began to go down behind the hills. Night came quickly over the high place. Sinbad tried to sleep and wait for the morning.

From that place, Sinbad found valuable items. He traded them with other business men for money. He built a new life and finally returned home to his family with great stories. Even though the trip was dangerous, he was happy to be safe again. He told his family about the big bird and the strange land. They listened with wide eyes and big smiles. Sinbad decided that he would stay home for a long time. He never forgot the lessons of that hard journey. His life became quiet and calm once more. He always remembered the great mountain and the big animal. Sinbad was glad for his safe return. He has traveled many times since then, but this journey was special.

### B2
Sinbad lived a comfortable life in a big city, but he wanted a great adventure. One bright morning, he joined a group of men and boarded a large ship. They sailed across the blue water for many weeks, and the journey was very calm. After some time, the leader dropped metal down near a strange land which looked like green grass. The passengers went down to walk on the ground and enjoy some fresh food. Sinbad rested under a tree while his friends explored the beautiful beach. Suddenly, the ground began to move beneath them with great force. The land was actually a huge sleeping animal which woke up from the heat of their fire. People started to shout and run back toward the water as the animal sank down. Sinbad was left behind on the coast while his ship sailed quickly away across the waves.

He felt very worried because he had no food and no way to return home. However, a large bird flew down from the sky and carried him safely to another high mountain. From that place, Sinbad found valuable items which he traded with other people for money. He built a new life and finally returned home to his family with great stories. Even though the adventure was dangerous, he was happy to be safe again. He had many days to think about everything that happened during the long trip. The large animal was something which nobody could believe until they saw it. If he stayed in the city, he would never know such a wonderful world. Every person who heard the story was amazed by the strange events. Sinbad decided that another journey might happen in some future year.

He spent much time with his family, but his mind was always on the sea. The money which he brought back was used to help poor people in the city. His friends often came to visit him and talk about the past adventure. They remember the day when the ground moved beneath their feet. Life in the city was quiet, but Sinbad sometimes missed the open water. He would look at the ocean and think about the bird that saved him. No one could imagine how high the mountain was where he landed. The valuable items changed his life completely and brought him much success. When people asked him about the journey, he told them every detail. He explained how the large animal woke up and went under the waves.

The listeners were always surprised by the facts of the strange story. Sinbad knew that he was lucky to survive such a dangerous test. His family was glad that he finally came back without any serious injury. Years passed, and he remained happy in his comfortable home. Yet, the call of the ocean was something which he could never forget. Perhaps another adventure would wait for him when the time was right. If he had not gone to the sea, he would have missed the great world. The wonderful memories stayed with him through every single day. His friends always asked him to repeat the story again and again. He told them that life was full of surprising moments.

### C1
Sinbad lived a comfortable life within the bustling walls of a great city, yet a quiet restlessness continuously gnawed at his spirit. Though his coffers were adequate and his days passed without hardship, he yearned for something far grander than ordinary routine. One particularly bright morning, driven by an irresistible impulse, he joined a group of adventurous men at the harbor. Without hesitation, they boarded a magnificent ship that promised to carry them toward uncharted horizons. As the vessel slipped away from the familiar docks, Sinbad felt a profound sense of liberation take hold of him.

They sailed across the vast blue expanse of the ocean for many weeks under an endless sky. The journey proved remarkably calm, allowing the travelers to share stories and marvel at the changing colors of the water. No storms threatened their passage, and the gentle winds guided them steadily toward unknown destinations. After a considerable amount of time, the captain decided it was wise to check their bearings and replenish their supplies. The leader dropped heavy metal anchors down near a strange landmass which looked deceptively like a strip of green grass.

Eager to stretch their legs, the passengers descended from the ship to walk upon the unfamiliar ground. They breathed in the fresh coastal air and prepared a modest meal using firewood gathered from the shore. Sinbad sought out a quiet spot to rest under the comforting shade of a large tree while his companions explored the beach. The afternoon sun beat down warmly upon the landscape, creating an atmosphere of profound tranquility. No one suspected that beneath the peaceful surface, an ancient slumber was about to be disturbed.

Suddenly, the ground began to shudder beneath them with a terrifying and unnatural force. What they had mistaken for firm earth was in reality a colossal sleeping animal that had rested in the ocean for centuries. The intense heat from their cooking fire had penetrated its thick hide, rousing the creature from its deep slumber. Panic instantly replaced the previous calm as people started to shout in absolute terror. They scrambled frantically back toward the water, desperate to reach the safety of the waiting boats.

Sinbad was left stranded on the crumbling coast as the massive beast began to shift its weight into the sea. The ship's crew, fearing for their own survival, hoisted the sails and pulled away quickly across the rising waves. He watched with a sinking heart as his only means of escape vanished into the distance. Left entirely alone on the sinking shore, a wave of deep worry washed over him. He realized with grim certainty that he possessed neither food nor any conceivable way to return home.

Just as despair threatened to overwhelm his senses, a colossal shadow fell across the beach. A gigantic bird, legendary in its proportions, descended gracefully from the open sky. With swift precision, it scooped Sinbad up from the dangerous coast without causing him any harm. The creature carried him safely through the clouds, far away from the sinking monster and the treacherous sea. Eventually, it deposited him gently upon the stony summit of a high and desolate mountain.

Standing upon that elevated plateau, Sinbad looked around and noticed glittering objects scattered among the rocks. To his astonishment, these were remarkably valuable items left behind by previous visitors or the giant bird itself. He gathered as many precious goods as his pockets could hold, recognizing his incredible fortune. When other travelers eventually passed through the region, he traded these treasures in exchange for considerable wealth. This unexpected windfall allowed him to build a new life of security and eventually secure passage back to his family.

When he finally walked through his front door, his relatives greeted him with tears of joy and disbelief. He regaled them with great stories of his miraculous survival and the strange wonders he had witnessed. Even though the adventure had proven exceedingly dangerous, he felt profoundly happy simply to be safe again. He spent many quiet days reflecting upon everything that had happened during the long and arduous trip. The enormous animal he had encountered remained something which nobody could truly believe until they heard it from his lips.

Had he chosen to remain safely in the city, he would never have known such a magnificent and varied world. Every person who listened to the tale was utterly amazed by the bizarre events he described. Despite the hardships he endured, Sinbad decided that another journey might happen in some future year. He spent much time with his family, yet his wandering mind remained perpetually fixed on the distant sea. The substantial wealth which he brought back was generously used to help poor people throughout the city.

His loyal friends often came to visit him just to talk about the dramatic past adventure. Together, they vividly remembered the terrifying day when the solid ground moved beneath their feet. Life in the city was undeniably quiet, but Sinbad frequently missed the unpredictable rhythm of the open water. He would often gaze out toward the ocean and think about the magnificent bird that had saved his life. No one in the neighborhood could quite imagine the staggering height of the mountain where he had finally landed.

The valuable items had changed his daily existence completely, bringing him lasting success and comfort. Whenever curious neighbors asked him about the journey, he patiently recounted every single detail. He explained precisely how the colossal animal woke up and dragged itself back beneath the waves. His listeners were invariably surprised by the sheer scale of the strange story. Sinbad knew beyond a doubt that he was extraordinarily lucky to survive such a dangerous test.

His family remained deeply glad that he had finally returned home without sustaining any serious injury. As years passed by, he remained comfortably settled, yet the irresistible call of the ocean never truly faded. He understood that if he had never braved the sea, he would have missed the true vastness of existence. The wonderful memories stayed vividly with him through every single day of his remaining life. Whenever his friends asked him to repeat the story, he smiled and told them that life was always full of surprising moments.

## st-0193 — The Fisherman and the Genie
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
A man goes down to the water. He wants food for his family. He has a hard life. He puts his thing into the water. He does this one time. He does it two times. He does it three times. He does it four times. He wants a big thing. He wants something good to eat.

On the fourth try, he finds a thing. It is an old thing. It is an old place. The man has a little thing. He holds it in his hand. He uses it on the thing. He works hard to open it. He wants to see what is there. He takes the top off. He looks down into the open place.

Something comes out of the thing. A lot of black smoke comes out. The smoke grows very big. It becomes a large person. The person looks like a bad thing. The bad thing speaks in a big sound. He says the man must die today. The bad thing stays in the thing. He stays there for many years. The man feels bad. He is very no good. He tries to stay still.

The man asks a question. How can a big body be there? It is in that small place. The person says he really lives there. The bad thing turns back into smoke. He goes down into the thing. The man is very fast. He puts the top back on the thing. He closes the place right away.

The bad thing speaks from the thing. He wants to come out. He wants to give money to the man. The man does not follow him. He takes the thing in his hands. He puts the closed thing into the water. The thing goes down into the deep water. The thing stays there. The man stops the bad thing. He keeps his own life safe today.

### A2
Once upon a time, a poor man went down to the water. He wanted to catch fish for his family every day. He threw his line into the sea four times. On the final try, he did not get any fish. His line brought up a large thing made of hard matter. The man felt very interested about this object. He used an item to open the old box. Suddenly, a big set of dark air came out.

The air became a very large person. The large person looked bad and full of risk. He spoke in a strong voice to the man. The bad person told the man that he must die today. The person had stayed inside the small space for ages. At first, the poor worker felt much fear. Soon, he tried to stay calm and use his mind. The smart man asked a big question. He asked how such a large body could fit inside.

The person said that he really lived there. To show the story, the person turned back into air. He went down inside the thing again. As soon as the person was inside, the man acted. He quickly put the cover back on top. The person spoke with a high voice from inside. He promised to give money if the man would open it. However, the smart man did not believe the words. He threw the box back toward the deep sea.

The thing would stay there for all time. By use of his mind, the worker won against the bad problem. He saved his own life from death. He was going to be safe with his family. He was going to remember this strange day forever. The sea was quiet and blue again. The poor man walked back to his little house with joy.

### B1
Once upon a time, a poor man went down to the blue sea. He wanted to catch food for his family every single day. He threw his net into the water four times during the morning. On the final try, instead of fish, the net brought up something else. It was a heavy metal bottle with a strange mark on the top. The man was very interested, so he used a tool to open the container. He wanted to see if something valuable was hiding inside. He hoped that he could change his life with this find. The sun gave bright light upon the water while he worked. He had never seen such a strange object before in his life. The metal felt cold and heavy in his tired hands. He looked at the strange mark again and again with care. He wondered what secret the old container might hold for him today. The man has looked at the object for a long time. If he finds treasure, he will buy food for his children.

Suddenly, a huge amount of smoke came out of the bottle. The smoke slowly became a tall spirit standing by the water. The massive person looked terrible and spoke in a high voice. The man must die today because he had been held inside the prison for many ages. At first, the poor worker felt extreme fear in his heart. However, he soon remembered his mind and tried to stay calm. The man asked the spirit how such a big body could fit inside that small bottle. The spirit insisted that he really lived in the container. To prove the story, the spirit turned back into smoke. The smoke went back down inside the metal bottle. The spirit has told a strange story about the prison. If the spirit comes out again, the man will run away.

As soon as the person was inside, the quick man put the heavy cover back on top. The person shouted from inside. He promised to give money if the man would let him out. However, the smart man did not trust the big words. He threw the bottle back toward the deep sea. The bottle would remain there for all time. Through skill, the ordinary worker defeated the powerful threat. He saved his own life and walked back home to his family. The worker has saved his family from great danger. If he goes to the sea tomorrow, he will find peace.

### B2
Once upon a time, a poor man went down to the deep sea. He wanted to catch some fish for his family. He threw his net into the water four times during the day. On the final try, instead of fish, the net brought up a heavy metal vessel. There was a strange seal on the cover of the container. The man was very curious about this object. He used a tool to open the container carefully. He wanted to know what was inside. He hoped that something good would be found. He thought that money might be inside the metal vessel. He pulled the cover off the top of the bottle. He looked inside the dark space with great interest.

Suddenly, a huge cloud of smoke came out of the bottle. The smoke slowly became a giant spirit in front of the man. The massive creature looked terrible and spoke in a loud voice. The giant told the poor worker that he must die today. The spirit explained that he had been trapped inside the prison for many ages. He said that many people had tried to free him before, but nobody came. At first, the poor man felt extreme fear. He did not know what he should do to save his life. However, he soon remembered his mind and tried to stay calm. He knew that panic would not help him in this situation. He looked at the giant with a calm face. He decided that he must think of a plan quickly.

The smart man asked the giant a question about his size. He asked how such a huge body could possibly fit inside that small bottle. He said that he could not believe the story. The spirit insisted that he really lived in the container. He wanted to prove that his story was true. The giant turned back into smoke and went back down inside the metal vessel. As soon as the creature was completely inside, the quick man moved fast. He immediately put the heavy cover back on top of the bottle. The seal was placed back over the opening by the man. The giant was trapped again inside the metal container. He could not get out of the prison anymore.

The trapped giant shouted from inside the bottle. He promised to give wealth if the man would let him out. He said that he would make the worker rich. However, the smart man did not trust the words of the giant. He remembered how the giant had threatened to kill him earlier. He threw the sealed bottle back toward the deep sea. The container fell into the water where it would remain forever. Through a smart trick, the ordinary worker defeated the powerful threat. He saved his own life and walked back home to his family. He felt very happy that he was still alive. He never forgot the day when he met the giant spirit in the sea. Every person in the village knew about the great event. The man lived long and happy years after that day. He never opened any strange bottle again.

### C1
The morning air carried the sharp, salt-laden chill of the early tide as the impoverished fisherman walked down to the edge of the deep sea. His family depended entirely upon the meager catch he could pull from the unforgiving water before the sun reached its zenith. With calloused hands that spoke of a lifetime of grueling labor, he prepared his worn net for the first cast of the day. He hoped the ocean would finally show mercy and provide enough sustenance to quiet the persistent hunger of his children.

Throughout the morning, he threw his heavy net into the water four separate times, watching anxiously as each retrieval brought only disappointment. On the final try, however, the fabric sank with an astonishing heaviness that suggested a massive school of fish had been trapped. Instead of thrashing scales, the net brought up a bulky, corroded metal vessel that smelled faintly of ancient bronze and stagnant depths. A strange, intricate seal made of lead stamped with mysterious symbols covered the narrow opening of the heavy container. Fascinated by the artifact, the man turned the vessel over in his hands, wondering what priceless treasure might lie hidden within its metallic walls.

Driven by a powerful curiosity, he reached into his pocket and pulled out a small iron tool to scrape away the hardened lead. He wanted desperately to know what secret the container had guarded for so many centuries against the pressure of the sea. He harbored secret hopes that a fortune in gold coins or precious gems might spill out once the obstruction was finally removed. With a sharp twist of his wrist, he forced the heavy cover off the top of the bottle, breaking the ancient seal completely. Leaning forward with intense anticipation, he peered directly into the dark, silent space of the open neck.

Without warning, a thick, billowing cloud of dark smoke erupted from the mouth of the bottle with explosive force. The dense vapor rushed upward toward the sky, darkening the daylight before slowly condensing into a towering giant spirit. The massive creature possessed a terrifying visage, with glowing eyes and a towering frame that cast a deep shadow over the beach. Speaking with a voice like rolling thunder, the giant announced to the trembling worker that his time on earth had officially ended. The spirit declared that the fisherman must die today, offering no explanation beyond the bitter cruelty of his own long imprisonment.

The giant explained that he had been trapped inside the narrow prison for many centuries, enduring endless torment without relief. He recounted how countless mortals had attempted to free him during the early decades, but none had ever succeeded in breaking the seal. As time dragged on into millennia, the spirit grew bitter, vowing that whoever eventually released him would receive a cruel punishment rather than a reward. At first, the poor fisherman felt a wave of absolute terror freeze the blood in his veins. Paralyzed by the sudden threat, he struggled desperately to figure out how he might possibly save his own life from such a monstrous foe.

However, panic quickly yielded to reason as the clever man forced his racing thoughts to settle into a strategic calm. He understood instinctively that surrender or tears would only hasten his doom, leaving his family unprotected and destitute. Composing his features, he looked up at the terrifying giant with a deceptively tranquil face. He knew that physical strength was useless here, leaving him only his wits to counter the overwhelming power standing before him. Quickly formulating a plan in his mind, he prepared to test the giant's legendary arrogance with a carefully chosen question.

Addressing the towering spirit with feigned disbelief, the smart man asked how such a gigantic body could possibly fit inside that tiny bottle. He claimed it was physically impossible for a creature of such magnitude to occupy a vessel of that modest size. He stated plainly that he could not believe the story unless he saw the miraculous feat performed with his own eyes. Stung by the doubt cast upon his extraordinary nature, the proud spirit insisted that he truly lived in the container. Anxious to prove his absolute truthfulness, the giant accepted the challenge without a single suspicion of deceit.

Instantly, the massive form began to dissolve, turning back into a thick, swirling column of gray smoke. The vapor cascaded downward in a continuous stream, rushing swiftly through the narrow neck of the metal vessel. As soon as the last wisps of the creature were completely sucked inside the dark interior, the quick man moved with lightning speed. He grabbed the heavy metal cover and slammed it back onto the top of the bottle with all his remaining strength. Without pausing for a breath, he pressed the lead seal firmly back over the opening to secure the barrier.

Trapped once again within the impenetrable prison, the giant slammed against the metal walls and shouted in furious desperation. He promised vast amounts of wealth and eternal riches if the fisherman would only show mercy and let him out. He swore upon the ancient powers that he would make the poor worker the wealthiest man in the entire region. Remembering the deadly threat made just moments before, the wise man refused to trust a single word uttered by the captive spirit. He knew that freeing the monster a second time would be an act of supreme foolishness with fatal consequences.

Without hesitation, the ordinary worker picked up the heavy bottle and hurled it back toward the deep sea with all his might. The container plunged beneath the white-crested waves, sinking deep into the abyss where it would remain hidden forever. Through a clever trick, he had successfully defeated a powerful threat that possessed enough strength to crush a kingdom. Having saved his own life through quick thinking, he turned away from the shore and began the walk back to his waiting family. He felt an overwhelming wave of relief and happiness coursing through his veins as he realized he was still alive.

He never forgot the remarkable day when he encountered the giant spirit on the lonely stretch of ocean shore. Word of the miraculous event spread rapidly through the village until every resident knew the details of his incredible adventure. The man lived long and peaceful years after that fateful afternoon, sustained by the modest catches he pulled from the sea. He made a silent vow never to touch another strange object or open any mysterious bottle that might surface from the water again. Wisdom had replaced curiosity, guiding his steps safely through the remainder of his days.

### A1

They lived happily together for a long time after that great journey.

### B2
Once upon a time, an old man and woman lived quietly near a big river. One day, the woman found a huge fruit floating down the water. The journey across the sea was long and full of unexpected sights. Momotaro walked for many miles before he reached the final shore. The water was blue and cold under the bright morning sun. His animal friends stayed close beside him through every difficult moment. They shared their food and rested together under the large trees. Every step they took brought them closer to the dark island. The local people had suffered for years because of the bad creatures. Now, hope was returning to the land because Momotaro was coming. The wind blew softly as the small boat moved across the waves. They knew the fight ahead would require all their strength and skill. The dog kept watch while the bird flew high to check the path. The other animal helped carry the heavy bags of food and supplies. Nothing could stop them because their goal was clear and true. ### C1
Once upon a time, an old man and woman lived quietly near a great, winding river. Their days followed the quiet rhythm of the water, filled with simple tasks and peaceful silence. One afternoon, while washing clothes by the bank, the woman noticed a huge fruit floating down the current. It bobbed heavily over the small waves, glowing with an unusual, warm light. She reached out with a long branch and pulled the magnificent treasure toward the muddy shore.

When they carefully cut the strange fruit open in their small wooden kitchen, a wonderful baby boy was born inside. He cried out with a bright, healthy voice that filled the humble home with sudden life. Overjoyed by this unexpected gift from the river, they named him Momotaro, which meant fruit boy in their language. Momotaro grew up strong and remarkably smart because his devoted parents took great care of him every single day. He learned to read the signs of the forest and listen to the whispers of the flowing water.

As the boy matured into a capable youth, merchants and travelers brought troubling news to their quiet village. He learned that bad enemies were living on a distant island and troubling local people with their endless greed. The smart child decided with absolute conviction that he must go and save the country from those creatures. He could no longer sit by the river while his fellow citizens suffered under the shadow of fear. His mother prepared sweet millet dumplings for him to eat during his long and difficult journey across the unknown sea.

Before he left home, Momotaro met a friendly dog at the edge of the village who wanted to help him. The loyal animal sniffed the sweet food in his bag and pledged his fierce loyalty on the spot. Soon after that departure, a smart monkey and a good bird joined the small group as they walked. The animals liked the brave boy very much, so they readily agreed to follow him across the deep blue sea. Together, this unlikely band of travelers walked with steady purpose toward the distant island where the enemies lived.

The journey across the sea was long and full of unexpected sights beneath the wide, open sky. Momotaro walked for many miles before he finally reached the rocky shore at the edge of the world. The water was blue and cold under the bright morning sun, crashing against the dark stones with a loud roar. His loyal animal friends stayed close beside him through every difficult moment of the exhausting trek. They shared their food and rested together under the large trees whenever the shadows lengthened.

Every step they took brought them closer to the dark island where the menacing creatures made their home. The local people had suffered for years because of the bad creatures, hiding their belongings in fear. Now, hope was returning to the land because Momotaro was coming with his trusted companions. The wind blew softly as their small wooden boat moved steadily across the rolling waves. They knew the fight ahead would require all their combined strength, courage, and clever skill.

The dog kept a sharp watch ahead, while the bird flew high into the clouds to check the path. The clever monkey helped carry the heavy bags of food and essential supplies through the dense brush. Nothing could stop them because their goal was clear, and their hearts were entirely true. When they finally touched the dark sand of the island, they prepared themselves silently for battle. The enemies tried to look fierce, shouting threats from behind their iron gates, but the small team was not afraid.

When they arrived at the dark island, the group faced many difficult challenges and narrow stone paths. However, the enemies were completely surprised because the small team fought with astonishing power and coordination. The dog, the monkey, and the bird used their special skills to confuse and defeat the guards. Because they worked together as one united team, quick victory was easily achieved before the sun reached its peak. If they had not trusted one another completely, they would not have won the fierce battle.

If Momotaro had stayed home by the river, the entire country would have remained in grave danger. The frightened enemies surrendered unconditionally and gave back all the stolen money and valuable things they had taken. Momotaro and his grateful friends collected everything that rightfully belonged to the poor people of the mainland. They loaded the heavy treasures into large carts and prepared for the long journey back to their village. After the battle, the land across the sea became peaceful and quiet once again.

They returned home safely with their new animal friends and lots of wonderful food to share with everyone. The old parents were extremely happy and relieved to see their smart boy return unharmed from such peril. They lived happily for many years after that great adventure, surrounded by deep peace and quiet gratitude. People still tell the stirring story of Momotaro today, passing the ancient tale from generation to generation. Every child in the villages knows the famous tale of the brave boy who crossed the sea.

He showed the world that working together can solve any problem, no matter how impossible it might seem. The dog, the bird, and the monkey remained his good and faithful friends forever. The country was safe at last, and lasting peace returned to the rolling green land. The old parents were endlessly proud of everything he achieved through his courage and kindness. Life went back to normal, but the memory of the great journey stayed with them like a warm light.

Momotaro always remembered the valuable lessons he learned during his travels far beyond the river. The world outside their home was vast and intimidating, but they knew that home was always best. That is how the famous story ends, echoing softly through the valleys of the peaceful country. Every person who hears the inspiring tale feels happy and grateful for the quiet days they enjoy. The victory that was won by Momotaro brought enduring harmony to the land for all time.

## st-0195 — Urashima Taro
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Taro is a young man. He lives near water. He works for family. He gets food everyday. One day, small children make big problems. They hurt a small turtle. Taro feels bad for it. He asks the children to leave. The children go away.

Taro puts the turtle into water. It goes away. The next day, it comes back. It asks Taro to go with it. They go down under water. They reach a big place. A woman comes to the door. She is the turtle Taro helps. She gives him food and music. Taro stays in the place. He likes every moment there.

Taro misses his mother. He wants to return home. The woman gives him a box. She says not to open it. Taro goes back with the turtle. He reaches land. Taro looks around the place. Everything is different now. People are new to him. Nobody remembers his family name. Many years pass away. He sees the true time.

Taro feels sad and not glad. He opens the box. A white thing comes out. Taro grows old and not strong. He falls down. His life comes to an end.

### A2
Taro was a young man. He lived in a small village near the blue sea. Every day he went out in his little transport. He wanted to catch fish for his family. One afternoon, he saw a group of kids. They made trouble for a small animal. It was a small animal with a hard body. Taro felt sorry for the poor animal. He walked over to ask kids to leave it alone. The kind man picked up the animal. He put it back into the water. The animal went away. It turned around and looked at Taro. The next day, the same animal appeared near his transport. It spoke to him. It asked him to visit a great house deep under the water.

Taro was very surprised. He got upon the back of the animal. They went down through the cold water. They reached a large place made of white rock and money. A beautiful girl came out to the door. She was the animal that he saved. She thanked him and offered him good food and music. Taro stayed in the place for a few days. He enjoyed every moment. However, he began to feel bad for his mother and his village. He told the girl that he must return home. He was sorry to leave her. The girl gave him a big box. She told him that he must never open it.

Taro went back on the animal to the land. He stepped upon the ground. He looked around with a bad face. Everything changed completely. The houses were different. The people were unknown persons. Nobody remembered his name or his family. He realized that hundreds of years passed while he was away. The old man felt very calm and sorry. He forgot the word of the girl. He opened the big box. A white air came out. Taro felt his body grow old in a second. He fell upon the ground. His long life ended.

### B1
Taro was a young man who lived in a small village near the sea. Every day, he took his boat out to catch fish for his family. One afternoon, he saw a group of children making trouble for a small animal. Taro felt sorry for the animal and asked the kids to leave it alone. The kind man picked up the animal and put it back into the water. The animal swam away, but it looked back at Taro with a happy expression. The next day, the same animal appeared and invited him to visit a water place. Taro was surprised, but he climbed onto the back of the large animal. They went down through the water until they reached a house of gold and white stone. A beautiful woman came out to welcome him because she was the animal. She thanked him and offered him fine food and music inside the room. Taro stayed in the place for a few days and enjoyed every moment. However, he soon began to feel sad for his mother and his village. He told the woman that he must return home, though he was sad to leave. The woman gave him a heavy box, but she told him never to open it.

Taro rode back on the animal until he reached the familiar beach. When he stepped onto the land, he looked around because everything had changed. The houses were different, the people were new, and nobody knew his name. He realized that hundreds of years had passed while he was in the water place. Feeling very sad, the old man forgot the warning and opened the box. A white mass of smoke came out of the container up toward the sky. Taro felt his body grow old and weak in a single second. He fell onto the ground, finally returning to the past of history. If he had not opened the box, he would have stayed young. He has learned a hard lesson about the passage of time in this world. Many years have passed, and his family is gone. He will always remember the beautiful woman and the wonderful place under the sea. Taro will always keep these memories in his mind. The water place was very quiet and calm during his stay. He has understood that time moves fast when people are happy.

Life in the village continued without him through all those long years. The people who lived there now did not know his face or his name. He walked slowly along the beach where he had played as a boy. The sun came up brightly upon the water, but nothing looked familiar to him now. He sat down on a stone and thought about his mother and his old life. The heavy box remained in his hand as a reminder of his mistake. If he could change the past, he would make a different choice. He has accepted his end and the final stop of his long journey. The story of Taro remains an important lesson for everyone in the village. Children still listen to the old story when they gather near the water. They learn that being good and having time are valuable things in this life. Taro has found his final rest by the edge of the blue water. The waves wash softly against the land day after day. His name lives on in the history of the small town. Nobody will ever forget the man who traveled to the big house under the sea.

### B2
Urashima Taro was a young man who lived near the blue sea in a small village. Every day, he took his small boat out onto the water to catch fish for his family. One afternoon, while he was fishing near the coast, he saw a group of children making trouble for a small animal. Taro felt very sorry for the poor creature, so he walked over and asked the kids to leave it alone. The kind man picked up the animal and put it safely back into the blue sea. The creature swam away happily, but it turned around once and looked back at Taro with a smart expression.

The next day, while Taro was sitting in his boat, the same animal appeared near the surface of the water. The creature spoke to him and invited him to visit the wonderful sea building which stood deep under the waves. Taro was very surprised, but he climbed onto the shell of the large creature and began his journey. They went down through the cool water until they reached a grand castle made of white stone and gold. A beautiful woman came out to the door to welcome him, because she was actually the animal that he had saved. She thanked him for his help and offered him fine food and wonderful music inside the wondrous place.

The man stayed in the sea building for what seemed like a few days, and he enjoyed every single moment. However, after a short period, Taro began to feel sad for his mother and his small village. He told the woman that he must return home, though he was quite sad to leave her and the lovely area. The woman gave him a heavy box, but she told him that he must never open it under any condition. Taro rode back on the animal until he reached the familiar beach where he had started his adventure.

When he stepped onto the land, he looked around with a confused face, because everything had changed completely. The houses were different, the people were strangers, and nobody remembered his family or his name. He soon realized that hundreds of years had passed on earth while he was away in the sea building. Feeling very sad and full of sorrow, the old man forgot the warning of the woman and opened the hidden box. A white cloud of smoke came out of the container and rose quickly up toward the open sky. Taro felt his body grow old and weak in a single second as the power took effect. He fell softly onto the sand, finally returning to the dust of history where his true time waited.

Many years before these events, the village had grown around the bay where the blue water met the land. People lived peaceful lives and worked hard every single week to build their futures. Taro was just a simple person who loved his family and cared for the sea around his home. Whenever he went out in his boat, he always watched the waves and thought about the mysteries of the world. The ocean was deep and full of life, providing food and support for everyone in the community. No one could have guessed that a simple act of kindness would change his life forever. When he saved the small creature, he started a chain of events that led him into a different dimension of reality. The grand castle under the waves was more beautiful than anything he had ever imagined before. Music played softly through the halls, and the food tasted better than the meals in his mother house. He spent hours walking through the gardens and talking with the woman who ruled the underwater palace. She told him stories about the ocean and how the various creatures lived together in peace and harmony. Taro listened with great interest and felt that he had found a second home beneath the blue waves. Yet, the thought of his aging mother always remained in the back of his mind like a persistent shadow. He knew that she would worry about him if he stayed away for too much longer without sending any word. The woman understood his feelings and gave him the box as a final gift before his departure. She made him promise that he would never open it, because the container held a secret power. Taro agreed to her terms and thanked her for the wonderful time that he had experienced in the castle. The journey back to the surface was quiet and peaceful, taking him through the same cool water. When his feet touched the sand of the familiar beach, he felt a strange breeze blowing from the land. The world had moved on without him, leaving behind only memories and old stories told by strangers. Houses were rebuilt, trees grew tall, and the faces of the villagers were entirely unfamiliar to his eyes. He walked through the streets looking for someone who might remember his father or his mother. Every person he approached looked at him with surprise and told him that they had never heard his name. Realizing that time had played a cruel trick on him, he sat down on the ground and began to cry. The heavy box rested in his hands, feeling warmer than it had when the woman first placed it there. Overwhelmed by sadness and despair, he broke his promise and lifted the lid of the mysterious container. White smoke poured out into the air, carrying away the last remaining years of his youth in an instant. His skin grew wrinkled, his hair turned white, and his bones became weak as the years caught up with him. He lay down upon the sand and closed his eyes, drifting into a deep sleep that would last forever. The sea continued to crash against the shore, washing away the footprints of the man who had traveled through time.

### C1
Many years before these profound events unfolded, a quiet fishing village had grown organically around the azure bay where the deep blue water met the jagged land. Here, humble people lived peaceful lives and worked hard every single week to build sustainable futures for their growing families. Taro was merely a simple young man who loved his elderly mother dearly and cared deeply for the vast ocean surrounding his modest home. Whenever he pushed his wooden boat out into the open water, he always watched the rolling waves and pondered the ancient mysteries of the world. The ocean was unimaginably deep and full of vibrant life, providing daily sustenance and quiet comfort for everyone in the coastal community.

Every single day without fail, he took his small boat out onto the water to catch fish for his dependent family. One lazy afternoon, while he was fishing near the rocky coast, he noticed a group of cruel children making trouble for a small, defenseless creature. Taro felt an immediate pang of sorrow for the poor creature, so he walked over and firmly asked the kids to leave it alone. The kind man carefully picked up the frightened animal and placed it safely back into the cool blue sea where it belonged. The creature swam away happily into the depths, but before disappearing completely, it turned around once and looked back at Taro with a surprisingly smart expression.

No one in the village could have guessed that a simple act of spontaneous kindness would alter the entire trajectory of his life forever. When he saved that small sea creature, he unknowingly started a chain of extraordinary events that led him straight into a different dimension of reality. The next day, while Taro was sitting alone in his boat, the exact same animal appeared near the sparkling surface of the water. The grateful creature spoke directly to him in a gentle voice and invited him to visit the wonderful sea building standing deep under the mysterious waves. Taro was thoroughly surprised by this impossible invitation, but without a moment of hesitation, he climbed onto the shell of the large creature and began his improbable journey.

They descended slowly through the cool, shimmering water until they finally reached a grand castle constructed entirely of white stone and gleaming gold. A remarkably beautiful woman emerged from the majestic doorway to warmly welcome him, revealing herself to be none other than the grateful animal he had rescued the day before. She thanked him sincerely for his timely help and offered him fine food and wonderful music inside the wondrous palace. The grand castle under the waves was vastly more beautiful than anything he had ever imagined seeing during his ordinary life on land. Soft music played continuously through the vaulted halls, and the exotic food tasted much better than the simple meals in his mother house.

He spent countless hours walking through the breathtaking gardens and talking intimately with the mysterious woman who ruled the underwater palace. She told him fascinating stories about the deep ocean and how the various marine creatures lived together in perfect peace and complete harmony. Taro listened with great interest to every word she spoke and felt deep down that he had somehow found a second home beneath the blue waves. The man stayed in the wondrous sea building for what felt like only a few days, and he thoroughly enjoyed every single magical moment. However, after this brief period had passed, Taro began to feel a sudden wave of sadness for his aging mother and his small village.

The persistent thought of his mother always remained in the back of his mind like a quiet shadow, reminding him of his familial duties. He knew quite well that she would worry endlessly about him if he stayed away for too much longer without sending any reassuring word. He politely told the woman that he must return home immediately, though he was quite sad to leave her and the lovely underwater domain behind. The understanding woman listened to his plea and gave him a heavy, ornate box as a final parting gift before his departure. However, she made him promise solemnly that he must never open the mysterious container under any condition whatsoever.

Taro agreed completely to her strict terms and thanked her profusely for the wonderful time that he had experienced within her magnificent castle. The journey back to the surface was remarkably quiet and peaceful, taking him smoothly through the same cool water that had brought him down. When his tired feet finally touched the familiar sand of the beach where he had started, he felt a strange, cold breeze blowing harshly from the land. He rode back steadily on the faithful animal until he reached the exact shore, stepping out into a world that felt entirely alien to his senses. When he stepped onto the dry land, he looked around with a deeply confused face, because everything had changed completely while he was away.

The familiar houses were entirely different, the people walking past were complete strangers, and nobody remembered his family or his proud name. He wandered aimlessly through the unfamiliar streets looking for someone who might recognize his father or remember his mother. Every single person he approached looked at him with sudden surprise and told him firmly that they had never heard his name before. He soon realized with mounting horror that hundreds of years had passed on earth while he was away living inside the magical sea building. The world had moved on relentlessly without him, leaving behind only forgotten memories and old, faded stories told by passing strangers.

Houses had been completely rebuilt, tall trees had grown and died, and the faces of the villagers were entirely unfamiliar to his tired eyes. Realizing fully that time had played a cruel and irreversible trick on him, he sat down heavily on the cold ground and began to cry. Feeling very sad and full of unbearable sorrow, the weary old man completely forgot the solemn warning of the woman and opened the hidden box. The heavy box rested in his trembling hands, feeling noticeably warmer than it had when the woman first placed it into his care. A thick white cloud of smoke billowed out of the opened container and rose quickly up toward the open, indifferent sky.

Taro felt his physical body grow old, frail, and weak in a single fraction of a second as the hidden power of the box took immediate effect. His skin grew deeply wrinkled, his hair turned stark white, and his bones became painfully weak as the stolen centuries caught up with him at last. Overwhelmed entirely by sudden despair, he fell softly onto the warm sand, finally returning to the dust of history where his true time had always waited. He lay down peacefully upon the shore and closed his heavy eyes, drifting slowly into a deep, unending sleep that would last forever. The vast ocean continued to crash rhythmically against the shore, washing away the final footprints of the man who had traveled far beyond the borders of mortal time.

## st-0196 — The Grateful Crane (Tsuru no Ongaeshi)
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
An old man walks in the cold. He walks on the white land. He sees a white animal. A bad thing holds it. The man helps it. He sets it free. The animal goes up high. It moves quick now. The man looks at it. He goes to his home. He thinks about the animal every day. He wants it to be well.

A young woman comes to the house. She says she is lost. The family lets her stay. She becomes like a child to them. She wants to make something. She goes into a small room. She tells them not to look. Strange sounds come from the room. She gives them a good thing. The man takes it to market. He wants to sell the item. He wants to get money.

A good buyer pays money. The family has enough food now. They buy things for the home. The old woman feels very bad. She wants to look. She opens the door. A white animal is in the room. The animal goes away quick. It becomes the young woman again. She looks at the family. She knows they see her true form.

She speaks with a bad feel. She tells them her true fact. She leaves the family. She goes away and does not return. The family feels very bad. They remember the young woman. They talk about her every day. The house is still now. They think of her each day. They keep the thought in mind.

### A2
Once, a kind old man walked near the cold water. He saw a white animal in a bad place. The poor animal had a bad foot. The old man felt deep trouble. He set the animal free. The animal went high into the open air. It looked down with good eyes.

Later that evening, a lovely young woman came to his house. She lost her way in the cold. She asked for a place to stay for the night. The kind man and his wife welcomed her. She became like a daughter to them. She wanted to make a special piece of work. She entered a small room. She asked them never to watch her work. Special and nice sounds came from the room. She gave them something that looked like a light color. The old man took it to the town market. A person with money paid a lot for it. The family had enough food for months.

The couple grew very interested. The old woman opened the door just a little bit. A white animal was making clothes into the material. The animal noticed them. Its bad fact was now known. The animal went out the window. It became the lovely young woman again. She spoke with deep trouble. She was the animal the man saved. She could no longer stay. She thanked them and went away. The old man and his wife felt very bad. They remembered the young woman every single day.

They always hoped to see her again. Life went on for the family in the small town. They had enough food and money to live. The old man often walked near the town market. He looked up at the open air. He hoped to find the white animal once more. The old woman also thought about the special room. She never opened any other door like that. They lived together for a long time. The memory of the young woman stayed in their hearts. They would always tell the story to their friends. Everyone in the town knew the story. It became a big part of local history.

People would talk about the kind man and the animal. The house remained low in sound and very quiet. No other person ever came to stay like that again. The old couple grew older and older together. They were happy with the simple life they had.

### B1
Once upon a time, an old man walked through a cold forest.

He saw a white bird that was caught by a bad trap.

The poor animal could not move because its body hurt.

Feeling deep trouble, the man carefully set the bird free.

If he helps the bird, he will feel very happy today.

The bird has opened its big body and flown into the sky.

It looked down at the old man with grateful eyes.

Later that evening, a lovely young woman arrived at his house.

She explained that she had lost her way in the snow.

The kind man and his wife welcomed the stranger inside.

The girl soon became like a daughter to them.

One day, she told them that she wanted to produce material.

She entered a small room and asked them never to watch.

Strange and wonderful sounds came from behind the door.

When she finally emerged, she gave them a piece of material.

The material looked like snow.

The old man took the material to the town market.

A rich buyer paid a lot of money for it.

However, the couple grew very interested about the work.

The old woman opened the door just a little bit.

Inside the room, a thin white bird was producing material.

The bird noticed them and realized its secret was known.

The bird flew out the window into the air.

It changed back into the lovely young woman.

She spoke with trouble, explaining she was the bird he saved.

Because her identity was discovered, she could not stay.

She thanked them, spread her white body, and flew away.

The old man and his wife always remembered the kind bird.

They lived quietly in their small house for many years.

Sometimes they looked up at the sky and thought about her.

They wondered if she was flying far away from danger.

The winter snow came back many times over the years.

Each time, the old couple stayed warm inside their home.

They never forgot the strange and wonderful visitor they met.

Her memory stayed in their hearts for a long time.

Life went on peacefully for the old man and his wife.

They always shared stories about the lovely young woman.

Their friends in town often asked about the white material.

The couple only smiled and kept the old secret safe.

They knew that some things in life are quite magical.

Every morning, the sun rose over the quiet town.

The old man walked outside to check the cold weather.

He always looked toward the sky with a gentle smile.

He hoped that the white bird was safe and happy.

His wife usually joined him near the front door.

They stood together and watched the morning light shine.

Even without the magical material, they felt truly rich.

Their simple life together was full of quiet peace.

They had loved the bird like a real child.

That special memory lived forever in their minds.

The story of the bird became a local legend.

People in the town talked about it during cold nights.

No one ever saw the lovely young woman again.

Yet the old couple always remembered her tender face.

They knew she had been a very special friend.

Time passed slowly as the years went by.

The old man and his wife grew older together.

They always took care of each other every single day.

Their home remained full of warmth and love.

They never stopped believing in the magic of that winter.

Sometimes the wind blew softly against their window.

It sounded just like the wings of a white bird.

They looked at each other and smiled in silence.

They knew the world was full of wonderful surprises.

That one moment in the forest changed their lives forever.

They had done a good deed on a cold day.

The reward they received was a daughter's love.

Even though she had to leave, she left a lasting mark.

Their hearts remained connected to the beautiful white bird.

Every part of their story stayed fresh in their minds.

They lived out their days in peace and happiness.

This is the end of the wonderful winter story.

### B2
Once upon a time, a kind old man walked through a cold wood. While he walked, he saw a beautiful white bird that was caught in a trap. The poor creature could not move because its wing was hurt. Feeling deep pain for the living thing, the man carefully set the animal free. The bird opened its large wings and flew high into the cold sky. It looked down at the old man with soft eyes before disappearing completely. Later that same evening, a lovely young woman arrived at the small house. She explained that she had lost her way in the white cold and asked for a place to stay.

The kind man and his wife welcomed the stranger into their warm home. The girl proved to be very helpful, and she soon became like a daughter to them. One day, the young woman told the couple that she wanted to make a special piece of material. She entered a small room, closed the door, and asked them never to watch her work. For several days, strange and wonderful sounds came from behind the closed door. When the girl finally emerged, she gave them a piece of material that shone like silver and ice. The old man took the material to the town market, where a rich buyer paid a lot of money for it. Because of this sale, the family had enough food and money to live well for months.

However, the couple grew very curious about how the wonderful material was made. Although they had promised not to look, the old woman opened the door just a little bit one afternoon. Inside the room, there was no girl at all, but a thin white bird was putting wings into the material. The bird noticed them, turned its head, and realized that its secret was now known. The bird flew out the window and transformed back into the lovely young woman. She spoke to them with deep pain, explaining that she was the very bird which the man had saved. Because her true identity had been discovered, she could no longer stay with them as a human. She thanked them for their past help, spread her white wings, and flew away forever.

The old man and his wife lived on after the bird left their home. They remembered the lovely young woman who had brought such wonder into their daily life. Every morning, the man would walk near the wood and look up at the high sky. He hoped that he might see the white bird flying far away from any danger. Sometimes the old woman would sit by the window and think about the secret room. They never forgot the strange days when the girl made the shining material inside the house. Even though she was gone, her memory remained alive within their hearts every single day. The couple always told each other that deep care brought a true blessing to their door.

Many years passed after the bird departed from the small home. The old man and his wife often talked about the wonderful days they had shared. They remembered how the young woman brought light into their lives. The house was quiet, but love remained in their hearts. They walked together through the garden when the weather was nice. The memory of the white bird gave them peace. Life went on slowly for the old couple. They were happy because they had helped a living creature in need.

Every afternoon, the old man sat outside and looked toward the wood. He wondered if the white bird was safe in the high sky. The old woman prepared food in the kitchen and smiled at her husband. They knew that kindness always brings a true reward. Their story was known by people in the town. People talked about the magical material and the kind couple. The house remained a warm place of peace and hope. They lived the rest of their days with full hearts and gentle memories.

### C1
Once upon a time, during an unusually bitter winter, an old man walked through a cold wood. The skeletal branches overhead rattled like dry bones against a pale, bruised sky. He sought only a handful of fallen timber to warm his meager hearth against the frost. While he walked, a sudden movement caught his tired eye among the drifted snow. It was a magnificent white bird, its pristine feathers hopelessly snared in a hidden hunter's trap. The poor creature could not move because its wing was badly hurt. Feeling deep pain for the living thing, the man approached with slow and gentle steps. He knelt in the freezing snow and carefully set the trapped animal free from its cruel iron teeth. The freed bird opened its large wings and caught the wintry draft, rising high into the cold sky. It circled above the trees for a long moment before disappearing completely into the gray horizon. Not once did the old man regret the time he had lost nor the labor he had abandoned. He simply shouldered his meager bundle of sticks and returned to his quiet cottage. Later that same evening, just as the last amber embers faded, a timid knock sounded at their door. A lovely young woman arrived at the small house, shivering and pale from the biting wind. She explained in a soft, musical voice that she had lost her way in the white cold. With eyes wide with exhaustion, she asked for a modest place to stay until the morning light. The kind old man and his faithful wife did not hesitate to welcome the stranger into their warm home. They shared their meager bowl of rice and offered her the warmest corner by the hearth. The girl proved to be remarkably helpful around the house, performing daily chores with grace and quiet dignity. Within a few short days, her gentle laughter filled the silent rooms, and she soon became like a daughter to them. They marveled at how swiftly this stranger had woven herself into the fabric of their lonely lives. Yet, beneath her radiant smiles, she maintained a mysterious aura that neither of the old people could fully decipher. One evening, the young woman approached the couple with a serious and purposeful expression. She told them that she wanted to weave a special piece of material to repay their profound kindness. She entered a small back room, closed the heavy wooden door behind her, and asked them never to watch her work. For several days, strange and wonderful sounds echoed from behind the closed door, resembling the steady rhythm of a distant storm. The old couple obeyed her strange request completely, though curiosity gnawed quietly at their aging minds. When the girl finally emerged on the fourth morning, she looked terribly pale and exhausted from her ordeal. She handed them a piece of material that shone like silver and ice under the morning sun. It shimmered with an ethereal light, as if captured moonlight had been spun into threads of pure glass. The old man took the wondrous material to the town market, where a wealthy buyer paid a vast amount of gold for it. Because of this unexpected sale, the family suddenly had enough food and money to live comfortably for months. However, as the weeks passed, the couple grew increasingly curious about how the wonderful material was made. Although they had explicitly promised never to look, the old woman succumbed to her temptation one afternoon. She crept softly down the narrow hallway and opened the heavy wooden door just a little bit to peer inside. What she witnessed defied all reason and filled her chest with an overwhelming mixture of shock and sorrow. Inside the room, there was no girl at all, but a thin white bird was plucking its own feathers to weave into the fabric. The exhausted bird noticed the shadow at the door, turned its head, and realized with a start that its secret was now known. The bird flew clumsily out the open window, its remaining feathers fluttering in the draft, and transformed back into the lovely young woman mid-air. She spoke to them with deep pain in her voice, explaining that she was the very bird which the man had saved in the woods. Because her true identity had been discovered, she could no longer stay with them as a human being. She thanked them sincerely for their past help, spread her magnificent white wings, and flew away forever into the clouds. The old man and his wife lived on in quiet solitude after the bird left their home forever. They remembered the lovely young woman who had brought such immense wonder into their daily life. Every single morning, the old man would walk near the edge of the wood and look up at the high sky. He hoped against hope that he might catch a glimpse of the white bird flying far away from any danger. Sometimes the old woman would sit quietly by the window and think about the secret room where the magic had occurred. They never forgot the strange days when the girl made the shining material inside the little house. Even though she was gone, her memory remained alive within their grateful hearts every single day. The couple always reminded each other that deep care and unselfish actions brought a true blessing to their door. Many years passed after the bird departed from the small home, leaving them with only their memories. The old man and his wife often talked about the wonderful days they had shared before the door had opened. They remembered how the young woman had brought sudden light and purpose into their quiet lives. The house was often silent, but a lingering love remained deep within their aging hearts. They walked together slowly through the overgrown garden whenever the weather was mild and pleasant. The memory of the white bird gave them profound peace as they faced the twilight of their years. Life went on slowly and peacefully for the old couple who had learned the true value of mercy. Every afternoon without fail, the old man sat outside on the porch and looked toward the distant wood. He wondered if the white bird was safe and warm in the high, endless sky. The old woman prepared simple food in the kitchen and smiled warmly at her beloved husband through the doorway. They knew from their extraordinary experience that genuine kindness always brings a true and lasting reward. Their remarkable story gradually became known by people in the town, whispered with wonder from generation to generation.
