During the long afternoons, they sat together and talked about many things. Sometimes they played games near the edge of the water. The sound of the river brought peace to their minds. They never worried about the past because they were safe now. Their hard work had built a wonderful life for all of them. The community respected the brothers for their clever plan against the dangerous creature. Children looked up to the three animals as heroes of the land. Parents told the story before putting their little ones to sleep. It was a lesson about how clever thinking could solve big problems. No one ever forgot the day when the bridge was finally free. The water continued to flow past the stones year after year. New flowers grew along the river banks every single spring. Animals and birds shared the land in complete friendship. The green hill stayed green and full of life through every season. Even when winter came with cold wind and snow, the brothers stayed warm inside. They had enough food stored away for the entire cold period. By the time spring returned, they were ready to go outside again. They ran through the fields and enjoyed the warm sun upon their backs. Such moments made their lives rich and full of joy. Nothing could ever break the strong bond between the three brothers. They stayed together through every challenge that came their way. Their names became known across the entire country. People would travel miles just to look at the famous bridge. The brothers always answered questions about their great adventure with a smile. They explained how they worked together to defeat the scary creature. Listeners always thanked them for sharing such an amazing tale. The bridge remained strong over the rushing water for many decades. Generations of animals grew up hearing about the brave brothers. The memory of that day lived on in every heart within the community. Life on the hill continued to be wonderful and full of light. The three brothers knew they were lucky to have each other. They spent their remaining days in total happiness and quiet comfort.

### C1
Once upon a time, in a valley where the soil had grown dry and sparse, there lived three brothers of the caprine kind, known to all as the Three Billy Goats Gruff. Their ancestral field offered little more than dusty earth and stubborn weeds, threatening their survival as the seasons turned colder. Across a rushing, slate-grey river lay a miraculous green hill, bursting with clover and sweet grass where they might finally graze and grow strong. Between their barren pasture and that verdant refuge spanned a heavy wooden bridge, beneath the rotting planks of which lurked a monstrous and ill-tempered creature. The brothers knew they had to cross, yet none dared approach the forbidden span without a careful plan.

The smallest of the three brothers was the first to test his fate, stepping bravely onto the timber structure. As his tiny hooves struck the aged wooden boards, the sound went trip-trap, echoing loudly against the rushing water below. Instantly, an ugly, bellowing voice erupted from the shadows beneath the bridge, shaking the very foundations of the crossing. Who is that walking across my bridge, roared the furious creature, threatening to devour the little traveler for his evening meal. Trembling yet resolute, the smallest goat raised his chin and spoke with surprising firmness to appease the beast.

Please do not consume me, the clever little animal pleaded, explaining that his second brother, far larger than himself, was following closely behind. The creature, enticed by the prospect of a more substantial feast, paused to consider the proposal and let the small goat pass unharmed. Moments later, the second brother ventured onto the wooden path, his measured steps ringing out in a steady rhythm of trip-trap, trip-trap. Scarcely had the echoes faded when the bridge-dweller lunged upward once more, demanding to know the identity of the trespasser. Do not take me yet, the medium brother replied with practiced calmness, suggesting that an even greater prize was yet to arrive.

He reminded the greedy monster that his eldest brother, a beast of immense size and terrifying power, was approaching the riverbank at that very moment. Greed clouding his judgment, the creature agreed to wait once more, letting the second brother cross safely to the lush green grass beyond. At last, the biggest animal of the three arrived at the approach, his heavy hooves striking the earth with a sound that shook the very ground. The bad person jumped furiously onto the wooden path, screaming with rage as he beheld the massive beast before him. Who dares to walk across my bridge now, bellowed the creature, preparing for a confrontation unlike any he had ever known.

The biggest animal felt not a single shiver of fear, standing his ground with an unshakeable composure that unnerved his adversary. With a sudden, ferocious charge, he lowered his mighty head and delivered a devastating blow straight into the chest of the screaming creature. The monster lost his footing entirely, tumbling backward off the wooden beams and plunging deep into the swirling, cold water below. Carried away by the relentless current, the troublesome beast vanished from sight, never to be seen near the crossing again. The three brothers were finally reunited upon the magnificent green hill, their perilous journey ending in absolute triumph.

From that day forward, the animals could cross the river without a single moment of trouble or hesitation. They feasted upon the endless fresh grass and reveled in the deep comfort of their new and bountiful home. No other creature ever dared to challenge them on the bridge, leaving the path completely open to the peaceful breeze. Many years passed while the three brothers resided upon the hill, growing remarkably robust on the rich and abundant food. Every single morning, they wandered across the vibrant meadows, playing together and basking in the warm sunlight.

Sometimes they paused to look down at the river, remembering the dangerous afternoon when they had first faced the bridge-dweller. They understood clearly that their collective bravery and quick thinking had saved them from a terrible fate. Their family bond had only deepened through the ordeal, transforming them into local legends throughout the entire countryside. Other animals from distant regions soon discovered the safety of the river and visited the hill to share in the harvest. Friendship and calm settled over the valley, turning the once-feared crossing into a beloved landmark of peace.

Generations of young children listened wide-eyed as parents told the story of the bridge before tucking them in to sleep. The tale taught everyone that even the most daunting obstacles could be overcome with steady nerves and a clever strategy. Life continued quietly and harmoniously for the brothers, who welcomed every passing traveler with genuine kindness and warm hospitality. They loved sharing stories about their adventure during long summer evenings, while the river hummed a gentle melody below. Nothing could ever disrupt the profound tranquility that now defined their days upon the green and fertile hill.

Winter eventually arrived with biting winds and heavy snow, yet the brothers remained perfectly warm and secure within their shelter. They had stored enough provisions during the autumn months to sustain themselves comfortably through the coldest weeks of the year. When spring finally returned, melting the frost and bringing new flowers to the riverbanks, they bounded joyfully back into the open air. Their laughter echoed across the water, celebrating the simple and radiant beauty of a life earned through courage and cooperation. Visitors often traveled miles just to catch a glimpse of the famous bridge and speak with the legendary goats.

The brothers always answered questions about their great adventure with a modest smile and a welcoming gesture. They never tired of reminding people that true strength lies not merely in physical power, but in unity and calm resolve. The bridge remained steadfast over the rushing water, serving as an eternal reminder of what could be achieved together. Life on the hill persisted in a golden haze of contentment, untouched by worry or fear for the remainder of their days. Surrounded by friends and living in total harmony, the three brothers knew they had built something truly everlasting.

## st-0203 — East of the Sun and West of the Moon
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Once, a mother and child live near some green things. A big white bear comes to the house. The bear offers money to the family. The young girl goes with the bear. She sits on his back for the day. She goes for a long time every day.

Every night, the bear becomes a man. He speaks to her in the room. He tells her never to look at him. They live well and love each other. The mother wants the child to speak. The mother gives her a small light. Late one night, the girl uses light.

Water falls on his face and body. The man gets up from sleep now. He says an old woman makes a plan. He must leave for a high house. The house is far from the world. The girl gets up with no one near. She walks far and long every day.

She meets old women on her way. They give her things like good things. She uses these things to pass water. She comes to the high house now. The woman keeps the man in a room. The woman wants to join the man. The girl uses things to help well. She washes the cloth for the man.

The bad plan breaks and falls down. The man and the girl run away. They go home and live well together. The family is joyful in the house.

### A2
Once, a poor mother lived with a little girl. They lived near a big forest. One cold day, a great white bear came to their house. He offered a good life if the young girl went with him. She felt bad, but she agreed. Her family needed money very much. She got upon his back for a long travel. Every night after sleep, the bear turned into a fine man. He spoke well to her in the dark room. However, he told her never to look at his face with light. For a time they lived happily together. Soon, her mother wanted her to know the real thing.

Her mother gave her a small light. Late one night, the young girl lit the light to see him. Drops of hot water fell upon his face. The man became bad and went away. He explained that a bad person placed a bad thing upon him. Because she broke her promise, he had to leave immediately. He went to a far house. The girl became alone in a place. She had no idea how to find him. During her travel, she met old women. They gave her good things like a food and cards. She used these items to trade with friendly people. They helped her cross the sea and the high places.

Finally, she arrived at the high house. The bad person kept the man and planned to marry him. The girl used her food to deal with the woman. Then, she cleaned the color off the clothes. Only true love could do this work. The bad thing broke completely. The man and his woman went away together and lived happily. They were very happy now. She found love with him at last.

### B1
Once upon a time, a poor mother lived with her lovely child. They had a small house near the woods. One cold winter day, a great white bear came to their door. The animal offered a rich life if the young girl would go with him. Although she felt afraid, she agreed because her family needed money. She climbed upon his back for a long journey. Every day they traveled through the deep woods together. The animal walked with her for many hours without a stop. She held onto him hard because the cold wind blew all around them. They finally reached a quiet place where they could rest for the night. The bear was very kind to her during their entire time in the forest. She slowly began to feel safe with the large animal. They shared food and water before they went to sleep. The child knew that her family would receive the money they needed. This thought gave her calm while she closed her eyes. The white snow covered the ground outside their resting place. Morning came quickly, and they started their travel again.

Every night after everyone else went to sleep, the bear turned into a man. He spoke softly to her in the dark room. However, he told her that she must never look at his face with a light. For a time they lived well together, but soon her mother gave her a small light. Late one night, the child lit the light to see him. Then drops of hot water fell upon his skin. The man woke up with great pain. He explained that an enemy had placed a spell upon him. Because she broke her promise, he had to leave immediately. He went away to a high building far east of the sun. He went west of the sky too. The young girl woke up alone in a strange forest. She had no idea how to find him again. During her long and difficult travel, she met old women. They gave her wonderful items to help her on the way. She used these special things to trade with friendly people. Those people helped her cross the sea and the high mountains. She walked for many days and never stopped moving forward. The sun was hot during the day, and the night brought freezing cold air. She felt tired, but her strong will kept her going. Every person she met along the way gave her hope. She walked across the country and never stopped.

Finally, she arrived at the high building where the enemy kept the man. The enemy planned to marry him against his will. The girl used her fruit to trade with the woman. She washed the water spots off the shirt as only a true love could do. The spell broke completely. The man and his wife escaped together. They lived well ever after in their new home. Their friends came to visit them and shared great joy. The long journey was finally at an end for the two friends. They looked back at the difficult times with a smile. No more trouble could ever hurt them again. The sun gave light brightly upon their good faces every single day. They built a wonderful life together and shared everything. The story of their love spread across the entire country. People talked about the strong girl who crossed the mountains. Her love had won every single problem in the end. She found peace and joy in her new life.

### B2
Once upon a time, a poor mother lived with her lovely child in a small house near the woods. One cold winter day, a great white bear came to their door. The animal offered a rich life if the young girl would go with him. Although she felt afraid, she agreed because her family needed money. She climbed upon his back for a long journey. She rode through the dark forest while the wind blew around them. Every night after everyone else went to sleep, the bear turned into a man. He spoke gently to her in the dark room. However, he told her that she must never look at his face with a light while he was sleeping. For a time they lived happily together. Soon her mother wanted her to know the truth. The family gave her a small light to use during the night. Late one night, the curious girl lit the light to see him. Drops of hot water fell upon his skin from her hands. The man woke up with great trouble. He was explaining that a bad person had placed a spell upon him. Because she broke her promise, he had to leave immediately. He went to a distant castle east of the sun and west of the moon. The young girl woke up alone in a strange forest. She had no idea how to find him. During her long and difficult travel, she met old women. The women gave her wonderful gifts like a golden fruit and golden cards. She used these special items to trade with friendly people. The people helped her cross the sea and the high mountains. Finally, she arrived at the high castle. The bad person kept the man prisoner and planned to marry him. The girl used her golden fruit to trade with the woman. Then she washed the spots off the shirt as only a true love could do. The magic spell broke completely. The man and his love escaped together and lived happily after. The story of the bear and the girl is very famous. Many people read the tale in books during the winter season. The mother and her child lived in a poor house before the journey began. Money was important for the family to survive. When the white bear arrived, the young girl made a big decision. She left her home and traveled far away into the cold mountains. Every evening, the animal became a man who talked to her in the dark room. She felt happy with him until the secret was broken. The small light changed everything between them. When the drops fell upon his skin, he woke up and told her the truth. He had to travel to a distant castle far away. The girl did not stop because she loved him deeply. She walked through many places and met helpful women along the road. They gave her wonderful items to help her cross the water and the mountains. When she reached the high castle, she found the man again. She used her golden fruit and washed the shirt to break the spell. True love won the day, and they were free at last. They left the castle and returned to a wonderful life together. Every person in the country heard about their amazing adventure. The mother was glad that her child found happiness in the end. Years later, people still told the story of the bear and the castle. It remained a favorite tale for children and adults too. Love can overcome any difficult problem if people stay true to their promises. The journey was long, but the result brought peace and joy. Even now, readers enjoy the magic of this old story. Life is full of strange turns, just like the path across the mountains. We learn that breaking a promise can lead to great trouble. However, true love and hard work can fix almost anything. The girl showed great strength during her long and difficult travel. She never gave up hope even when she was completely alone. Her story continues to inspire readers all around the world today.

### C1
Once upon a time, in a modest cottage near the edge of a whispering winter forest, lived a destitute mother and her extraordinarily lovely daughter. Poverty pressed upon their lives with the heavy chill of the season, leaving their pantry bare and their hearth dangerously low on timber. It was on a bitterly cold afternoon that an immense white bear materialized at their threshold, his fur shimmering like drifted snow against the darkening trees. With a voice of startling refinement, the magnificent beast offered the young girl a life of boundless wealth if she would consent to accompany him. Although trembling with a profound, instinctive dread, she ultimately agreed because her family desperately needed the silver to survive. Without another word, she climbed upon his broad, muscular back, preparing herself for a long and perilous journey into the unknown.

They rode together for hours through the black, frozen forest while the fierce winter wind howled like a desolate spirit around them. Every branch groaned under the burden of accumulated ice, yet the bear kept his steady pace, shielding her from the worst of the biting gale. When they finally arrived at his secluded mountain dwelling, she was provided with luxurious chambers and every conceivable comfort. Yet, a strange mystery governed her existence within those stone walls once the sun dipped below the horizon. Every single night, after the household had fallen into a deep slumber, the great white bear transformed seamlessly into a man. He would enter her dark room, speaking in tones of profound tenderness that quickly chased away her lingering fears and initial hesitations.

He warned her repeatedly that she must never attempt to look upon his face with the aid of a light while he was sleeping. For a considerable time, they lived in a state of quiet, unbroken harmony, finding deep affection within the shadows of the nocturnal hours. However, human curiosity is a fragile and persistent thing, and soon her mother began to wonder about the truth of their arrangement. Prompted by whispered doubts and maternal anxiety, the family managed to send her a small, guarded taper to use during the night. Torn between her promise and a growing, insatiable desire to know her husband completely, the young girl succumbed to temptation. Late one night, as he slept beside her, she reached for the small light and cast its trembling flame across his sleeping features.

As she leaned closer to behold his face, three burning drops of hot tallow accidentally spilled from her unsteady fingers onto his bare skin. The man woke up immediately, his eyes filled with a heartbreaking sorrow rather than anger as he looked at her. He began to explain with great difficulty that a malicious troll stepmother had placed a cruel spell upon his very existence. Because she had broken her strict promise before the appointed time, the enchantment demanded that he leave her side instantly. He told her of a distant castle situated east of the sun and west of the moon, where he was doomed to marry another. Before she could offer a single plea for forgiveness, he vanished into the freezing night air, leaving her entirely alone in the world.

The young girl woke up stranded in a strange, desolate forest with no idea how to traverse the vast distances to find him. Despair threatened to consume her spirit, but her love for the transformed man proved stronger than her overwhelming grief. During her long and arduous travels across unfamiliar terrain, she encountered several elderly women dwelling in remote corners of the wilderness. Moved by her steadfast endurance, these wise figures offered her wonderful gifts, including a glowing golden fruit and a set of magical golden cards. She thanked them profoundly, knowing that these special items would serve as her only currency in the trials ahead. She used those precious gifts to trade with friendly strangers who alone possessed the knowledge to guide her across the treacherous sea.

With the assistance of the cards and the kindness of local folk, she crossed wide oceans and scaled impossibly high mountain passes. Her feet were blistered and her clothes were worn to shreds, yet her determination never once faltered throughout the grueling ordeal. Finally, after weeks of relentless searching, she arrived at the towering gates of the distant castle perched upon the jagged cliffs. It was the very fortress where the wicked woman kept the man prisoner and prepared for their impending, unwanted wedding ceremony. The atmosphere within the castle was cold and oppressive, heavy with the weight of dark magic and stolen joy. The clever girl knew she had to act quickly and precisely if she ever hoped to rescue the man she loved from his fate.

Approaching the haughty mistress of the castle, the girl produced her marvelous golden fruit and bartered for a single hour near the prisoner. Her second task was even more demanding, requiring her to wash out the stubborn spots of tallow from a royal shirt. According to the ancient rules of the spell, only the true love of the enchanted man possessed the capability to cleanse the fabric completely. While others had tried and failed, leaving the stains untouched, the young girl approached the basin with absolute confidence and steady hands. As she scrubbed the garment, the dark spots vanished magically under her touch, proving the purity and strength of her devotion. At that exact moment, the complex enchantment that had bound the man for years shattered into a thousand harmless fragments.

The heavy stone walls of the castle seemed to tremble as the power of the wicked spell dissolved into nothingness forever. The man and his true love embraced in the grand hall, their long separation ending in a moment of pure, unadulterated triumph. Together, they gathered the prisoners who had also suffered under the tyranny of the castle and prepared their swift escape. They fled the grim fortress before the mistress could marshal her dark forces, racing away into the bright, welcoming light of morning. After enduring so much hardship, they returned safely to a wonderful life filled with peace, laughter, and mutual understanding. Every person living in the surrounding country soon heard the remarkable tale of the bear, the castle, and the brave young girl.

The mother was overjoyed when her daughter returned home safely, having found true happiness at the end of such a dangerous path. Years later, elders still told the story of the white bear to wide-eyed children gathered around warm hearths during the deep winter months. It remained a cherished favorite for readers of all ages, prized for its timeless message about the enduring power of human affection. The narrative taught that true love and perseverance can overcome any obstacle, no matter how insurmountable it might initially appear. Life often presents unexpected twists and difficult choices, much like the rugged path the heroine walked across the snowy mountains. We learn from her trials that breaking a sacred promise leads to great trouble, but genuine remorse and hard work can repair almost anything.

The young girl had demonstrated extraordinary strength of character throughout her long, solitary journey across the uncharted wilderness. She never once surrendered her inner hope, even when the biting wind and the silent trees suggested she was entirely abandoned. Her inspiring story continues to resonate deeply with readers all around the world who appreciate the classic triumph of light over dark. Even now, modern audiences enjoy the quiet magic of this old story whenever the winter snow begins to fall outside their windows. It stands as a reminder that the human heart is capable of remarkable endurance when driven by a sincere and devoted attachment. Through patience and courage, the impossible becomes achievable, and the coldest winter always yields at last to the warmth of spring.

## st-0204 — Why the Sea Is Salt
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Long ago, two men live near water. One man is bad. The other man is kind. He has no money. He has no food. He is very low in spirit. The year is not good. The man has no food at all. He goes to his bad other man. He asks him for food. The bad man give him a small present. It is a small thing.

Little old men appear in the land. They want the small present. It is an old hand thing. The less fortunate man give it to them. They give him food. Then he see the thing make money. His family life become very good now. The bad man hear about the thing. He wants the thing too. He takes the thing at night.

He goes to his small house. Then he take it to water. He goes far from land. He turns it to make food. The thing make money and food. Then he turn it to make white food. He turns it for white food every day. He do not know how to stop it. The thing make more white food. The place on water go down.

The place go under water. The man is not able to stop it. The place go down into the water. The thing stay down in the water. It makes white food every day. This is why the big water is different.

### A2
Long ago, two brothers lived near the water. One brother had much money and wanted more. The other brother was poor and kind. The poor man always tried to help people every day. During a cold week, he had no food at all. He went to his rich brother for help. The rich man grew very bad toward him. He threw a small present at the brother. The poor man walked into the dark forest. Suddenly, little old men appeared around him. They wanted the unusual present back. It was a special small machine. He traded the item for food. Soon he learned the machine was full of power. When he turned it, food and money came out. Their lives became much better each day. The bad brother heard about the machine. He decided to take it at night. He took the machine to his small ship. Then he went away across the water. Far from land, he wanted water with taste. He turned the handle on the machine. He did not know how to stop it. Water with taste came out until the ship went down. The power machine remains at the lowest part. It turns without end and makes water with taste. Because of this, the sea water is full of taste. People still tell this story today.

Many years ago, people told stories about this machine. Families talked about the poor brother and the bad brother. The story passed from parent to child. Everyone in the town knew the tale well. The poor man lived a good life until the end. His family never had to worry about food again. They always shared their meals with other people. The bad brother never came back home. His ship went down in the deep water. No one saw him again after that night. The machine stayed under the sea. It kept working day and night. The water with taste spread across the world. Children grew up listening to this old story. They learned to be kind to everyone. Being greedy brings bad results in the end. Being good brings happiness and help from friends. The old story remains important today. People remember the lesson of the two brothers. They teach it to new generations every year. Life can change quickly for anyone. We should always try to help those in need. That is the true message of the tale.

### B1
Long ago, two brothers lived near the sea. One brother was rich and very hard. The other brother was poor and kind. The poor brother always tried to help people in need. He had a good heart and shared what little he had. Many people liked him because he showed care and support. His brother only cared about money and business. The rich man never shared his gold with anyone. He wanted more and more property each year. He did not care if other people suffered. They had lived in the same village for a long time. Everyone knew that the rich man was very unfair. He always kept his gold inside a big box. The poor man worked hard every single day. He had little food, but he always smiled. His family was happy despite having very little money. The rich man had everything, yet he wanted more. He never gave anything to poor people in the town. He thought only of his business and his gold. Life went on this way for many months. Then a cold winter came to the small town. The weather was bad, and snow fell everywhere. People stayed inside their houses to stay warm. The poor man had no food left for his family. He needed help, so he visited his rich brother.

During a cold winter week, the poor man had no food. He went to his rich brother for food. The rich man grew angry and threw a small gift at him. He told the poor man to leave his house immediately. The poor man took the strange gift into the dark forest. Suddenly, little old men appeared around him. They wanted the gift, which was a special small machine. The poor man traded the item for food. Soon, he learned that the machine was wonderful. When he turned the handle, the machine produced gold and food. Their lives became much better each day. They have lived happily since that time. The poor man thanked the little men for their help. He brought the wonderful machine back to his home. His family was amazed by what the machine could do. It made delicious food and bright gold pieces. They never worried about money or hunger again. They bought a new house and nice clothes. They also shared their food with other poor people. The rich brother heard about the wonderful machine. He decided to steal the machine while the family slept. He took the machine to his boat and went away.

Far from land, the rich man wanted the machine to make white water. He turned the handle, but he did not know how to stop it. The white water came out until the boat went down into the water. If he had known how to stop it, he would have saved his life. The wonderful machine remains at the bottom of the deep water. It turns round and makes the sea full of white water today. This is why people still tell this story every year. Everyone knows that bad actions bring bad results in the end. The rich man lost everything because he wanted too much. He never returned to his home or his family. The boat sank deep into the cold sea. The machine stayed at the bottom and kept working. It produces white water day after day. The sea became full of this white water. People remember this lesson when they look at the sea. They know that bad people lose everything in the end. Kindness brings good rewards to those who help others. The poor family lived a long and peaceful life. They always remembered the strange machine and the little men. The story passed from parents to children through time.

### B2
Long ago, two brothers lived in a small village near the sea. One brother was rich and wealthy, while the other was poor but very kind. Although the poor brother had almost nothing, he always tried to help people who were in need. During a cold winter week, the poor man had no food left in his house. He went to his rich brother to ask for a piece of bread. Instead of helping, the rich man grew angry and threw a small gift at him. The poor man took the strange gift and walked away into the dark forest. Suddenly, a group of little old men appeared around him with strange energy. They wanted the gift, which was a special small machine that could make anything. The poor man traded the item for food, but soon learned that the machine was special. When he turned the handle, the machine produced gold and good food for his family. Because the machine was so useful, their lives became much better each day.

The wealthy brother heard about the special machine and came to see it. He decided to steal the machine while the poor family was sleeping. He took the machine down to his boat and sailed away across the sea. Once he was far from land, the rich man wanted the machine to make salt. He turned the handle, but he did not know how to stop the machine. The salt poured out continuously until the boat became too heavy and sank. The special machine remained at the bottom of the ocean, turning forever. Because it never stops making salt, the water of the sea is full of salt today. This is why people still tell the story of the special machine. Many years passed after the boat sank into the deep water. People in the village would remember the poor brother and his kind heart. They knew that the world could be a difficult place for poor people.

However, the story of the machine gave them hope for a better life. Children listened carefully when their parents told the old story again and again. They learned that being kind to others is much better than having a lot of money. The rich brother lost everything because he was bad and wanted more than he needed. That lesson remained important for every person who lived in the village. Even today, parents share this tale to teach young children about life. The sea continued to move above the machine at the bottom of the ocean. Fish swam around the metal parts while the handle turned day and night. No one could reach the bottom where the machine worked without stopping. The salt spread through the water and changed the nature of the sea completely. Some people wondered if the machine would ever stop making salt. Others believed that it would run forever until the end of time.

Whatever the truth was, the sea remained full of salt through all seasons. Visitors came from far away to see the blue water and hear the local story. They walked along the beach and looked out toward the far edge with wonder. Many years went by, and new generations of children grew up in the village. They heard the same old tale from their mothers and fathers during long evenings. The magical machine stayed hidden deep below the surface where no human eye could see. People often thought about the rich brother and how his greed destroyed him completely. They understood that money was not the most important thing in this world. Kindness and love remained the greatest treasures that anyone could ever hope to find. Every person who listened to the story felt a deep sense of peace. The blue ocean waves rolled gently against the sandy shore under the bright sun. Life in the village went on quietly as the generations changed over time.

### C1
In a secluded coastal village where the gray mist clung persistently to the cliffs, two brothers lived starkly contrasting lives. One possessed boundless wealth, accumulating lands and gold through shrewdness and fortunate investments, while the other endured relentless poverty. Despite having virtually nothing to call his own, the poor brother retained a remarkably generous spirit and shared whatever meager scraps he found with those even more destitute. During a particularly merciless winter week, when frost hardened the earth like iron, the poor man exhausted his last provisions and faced starvation. Driven by desperation, he wrapped his thin coat tightly around his shivering frame and walked to the grand estate of his wealthy sibling. He knocked timidly on the heavy oak door, hoping that familial ties might evoke a flicker of compassion in his brother's heart. When the wealthy man opened the door, his face twisted into an expression of cold annoyance at the sight of such an impoverished petitioner. Instead of offering the requested piece of bread, the rich man muttered a harsh insult and hurled a small, peculiar object directly at his face. The poor man caught the strange gift instinctively, bowing his head in quiet defeat before turning away into the deepening shadows of the dark forest. As he trudged deeper among the towering pines, a sudden shimmering in the twilight air signaled an inexplicable shift in the atmosphere. A group of diminutive, elven figures materialized around him, radiating an otherworldly energy that made the hairs on his arms stand on end. They cast eager glances toward the peculiar object he clutched in his trembling hands, desperately wanting the device back. Sensing an opportunity born of magic, the poor man traded the curious artifact in exchange for a massive abundance of warm food and vital provisions for his starving family. To his astonishment, he soon discovered that the traded item was far more than a mere trinket or a clever diversion. When he turned the small metal handle protruding from its side, the device began to produce gleaming gold coins and lavish feasts of the finest food. Day after day, the miraculous machine provided endlessly for his household, lifting them out of the heavy burden of poverty forever. Because the utility of the device was so profound, their lives transformed into a state of comfortable security that they had never dared to imagine. Word of this sudden and inexplicable prosperity inevitably traveled across the district until it reached the ears of the wealthy brother. Consumed by bitter jealousy and burning curiosity, the rich man journeyed to the modest cottage to witness the miracle with his own eyes. When he saw the magical machine in action, a dark resolve hardened in his mind, and he determined to steal it while the household slept. Under the cover of a moonless night, he crept into the humble dwelling, seized the precious artifact, and hurried down to his private boat. With the stolen treasure safely stowed beneath the wooden deck, he pushed off from the shore and sailed far out across the dark, swelling sea. Once he was completely isolated from the coastline and shrouded in thick ocean fog, the rich man decided he wanted the machine to generate a vast cargo of salt. Eager to profit from this new commodity, he grasped the small handle and began to turn it with greedy enthusiasm. However, in his haste, he had neglected to learn the specific mechanism required to halt the process, leaving him completely helpless as the machine whirred to life. Instantly, pristine white salt began to pour out in a thick, continuous stream, covering the deck and filling the hold with blinding speed. Panic gripped the wealthy merchant as he frantically twisted the handle backward and forward, but the relentless device ignored his desperate commands. The salt accumulated endlessly, growing heavier by the minute until the wooden boat lost its buoyancy and began to list dangerously to one side. Within moments, the overloaded vessel slipped beneath the dark waves, dragging the greedy man and his stolen prize down into the freezing abyss. The magical machine remained resting firmly at the bottom of the ocean, turning its hidden handle forever in the silent darkness. Because it never ceases its relentless production, the vast waters of the sea remain permanently saturated with salt to this very day. This is the reason why villagers still gather by the shore to recount the legend of the miraculous device and its fateful journey. Long years passed after the tragic sinking of the boat, yet the memory of the poor brother and his enduring kindness never faded from the community's heart. The villagers understood deeply that the world could be a remarkably harsh place for those who lacked material wealth or influence. Yet, the story of the magical machine offered a persistent glimmer of hope that honesty and virtue might ultimately prevail. Children sat in wide-eyed silence, listening intently as their parents repeated the ancient tale through the long, howling evenings of winter. They absorbed the fundamental lesson that possessing a compassionate heart far outweighed the hollow value of accumulating excessive riches. Meanwhile, far beneath the churning surface, the sea continued its timeless dance above the hidden machinery at the ocean floor. Schools of silver fish glided effortlessly around the metallic parts while the handle turned ceaselessly through day and night, untouched by human hands. No mortal swimmer could ever descend to that crushing depth to witness the tireless labor of the submerged artifact. The salt diffused steadily through the vast marine currents, altering the very chemistry of the deep ocean in a permanent, silent transformation. Generations came and went like the rolling tide, but the blue waters retained their saline taste through every shifting season of the world. Visitors from distant lands frequently walked along the sandy beaches, gazing out toward the horizon while local guides shared the timeless narrative. They pondered the fate of the wealthy brother, recognizing how unchecked avarice had paved the path to his ultimate destruction. Kindness and love remained the most precious treasures any human being could hope to cultivate during their brief existence. As the golden sun dipped below the horizon, gentle waves washed rhythmically against the shore, whispering secrets of the deep to the attentive land. Life in the village flowed onward in a tranquil rhythm, untouched by the passage of decades and the unending turning of the hidden handle. Every person who heard the tale felt a profound sense of quiet reassurance settling deep within their souls. The enduring legend stood as a timeless reminder that true wealth resides not in gold, but in the enduring grace of a generous spirit.

## st-0205 — The Firebird
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
A man lives in a big house. He has three children. He has a great area. A tree grows food. The food has a color. The color is like the day. Someone takes the food every night. The man asks his children for help. The big children try first. They fall down every night. They stay down for a long time. Then Prens goes into the area. He looks at the tree.

A light thing appears at night. It is a small animal with a light. The light has a color. The color is like red. Prens tries to take it. He only keeps one little part. He keeps one hand part. The man is very glad. He wants the whole animal. Prens starts a long way. He meets another little animal. The animal helps him. Prens gets to the place.

He follows every rule. He does every job. He wins every day. He brings the animal home. He finds his good future. The man is good with Prens. The area is now good. Everyone lives in very good peace.

### A2
Once upon a time, a man lived in a great house. The man had three sons. The man owned a large garden. A tree grew nice food there. However, someone kept taking the good food every night. The man asked his sons to find the person. The older brothers tried first. They grew less able and fell down quickly. Then the young son, Prens, went into the garden.

During the night, a light animal appeared near the tree. It was a small animal. Its back part looked like a red fire. Prens tried to catch it. He only managed to keep one shining part. The man was happy with the part. However, he wanted the whole animal. Prens started a long trip across the country. He met a different animal along the way. This animal helped him through many problems.

Prens finally reached the place. He followed all the rules. He completed every task with care. In the end, Prens won success. He brought the animal home and found his future. The man was very happy with his young son. The garden was fine once more. Everyone lived in close peace.

### B1
Once upon a time, a king lived in a great house with his three sons. The king had a wonderful garden where a tree grew fruit of yellow color. However, someone kept taking the fruit every single night. The king asked his sons to find the bad person and protect the tree. The older brothers tried first, but they grew tired and fell down quickly. Then the youngest son, Prens, went into the garden to watch the tree. During the middle of the night, a bright animal appeared near the tree. It was an amazing bird whose body gave light like a warm sun. Prens tried to catch the bird, but he only kept one part of it. He held a bright piece from the animal in his hand. The king was happy with this item, but he wanted the whole bird. Prens started a long trip across the country to find the bird. He has walked for many days to reach this place. He has seen many things on his long journey across the land.

Along the way, he met a strange animal that helped him through many difficulties. With the help of his new friend, Prens finally reached the place where the bird lived. He followed all the rules and completed every task with care. In the end, Prens won success, brought the bird home, and found his happy future. The king was extremely proud of his youngest son. The garden was safe once more, and everyone lived in peace. If Prens finds the bird, the king will be happy. The strange animal has stayed by his side through every single moment. They have crossed many fields and hills together. The king has waited for his son to return home safely. If the bird stays in the garden, peace will remain there always. The people of the country have thanked Prens for his great work. He has learned much about the world during this time. His family has supported him through every single day of the trip.

Many people live in this large country. They work hard every day to build a good life. The king rules the land with care and peace. His sons live in the great house near the garden. The tree grows yellow fruit in the warm sun. Birds fly across the blue sky during the day. Animals live in the green fields and hills. People walk along the roads to reach different places. Children play games near the houses in the afternoon. Everyone feels safe when the sun goes down. Night comes quickly over the hills and fields. The bright moon gives light to the dark world. Prens remembers his long journey across the land. He knows that his friend helped him win success. The story of Prens lives on in the hearts of the people.

### B2
Once upon a time, a king lived in a great castle with his three sons. The king possessed a wonderful garden where a tree grew golden fruit. However, someone kept stealing the golden fruit every single night. The king asked his sons to find the person who took the fruit and protect the tree. The older brothers tried first, but they grew tired and closed their eyes quickly. Then the youngest boy, Prens, went into the garden to watch the tree through the night. During the middle of the night, a bright creature appeared near the golden tree. It was an amazing bird whose body shone like heat and light. Prens tried to catch the bird, but he only managed to keep one shining piece of it. The king was happy with that piece, but he wanted the whole bird. Prens started a long journey across the country to find the bright creature. Along the way, he met a strange animal that helped him through many difficulties. With the help of his new friend, Prens finally reached the place where the bird lived. He followed all the rules and completed every task with care. In the end, Prens won success, brought the bright bird home, and found his happy future. The king was extremely proud of his youngest son. The garden was safe once more, and everyone lived in quiet happiness. Many days passed after Prens returned home with the bright bird. The people of the country were very glad that the garden remained safe. The king called all his family together to celebrate the great success. Prens looked back on the long journey and remembered the strange animal. He knew that without that help, he would never have reached the secret place.

The older brothers also learned an important lesson about responsibility and care. They understood that closing their eyes during the night was a big mistake. From that day forward, the castle was always full of life and joy. The golden fruit continued to grow on the tree without any further problem. Nobody ever tried to steal the valuable fruit again. The king gave more power and respect to his youngest son. Prens lived a long and quiet life inside the great castle. The story of his adventure was told to every new generation in the country. Children heard the tale and dreamed about traveling across strange lands. The bright bird stayed in the garden, bringing light and happiness to everyone. Thus, the land remained strong and secure for many years to come. Quiet and peace ruled the land from the highest mountain down to the sea. Sometimes people wonder how Prens managed to find the exact location of the creature. The answer lies in the deep friendship between the boy and the strange animal. They walked together through forests and across rivers until they reached the goal. Every single challenge was met with courage and strong determination. The animal gave good advice whenever a difficult situation appeared. Prens listened carefully and followed every instruction without any question. Because they worked as a team, nothing could stop them from winning success. The journey taught Prens that friendship is more valuable than gold or power. When they finally arrived, the bright bird was waiting for them.

Prens showed respect and care, so the bird allowed him to bring it home. That moment changed the life of the young boy forever. He became a wise man who always protected the people in his charge. The king looked at his son with deep pride and happiness. Everything was in order, and the land entered a wonderful new period of history. The memory of those events lived on in the hearts of all who heard the tale. And so, the wonderful garden remained a symbol of hope and success for all time. Many seasons came and went across the land, bringing peace to the people. The castle walls stood strong against any wind or trouble from the outside. Prens often walked through the garden to look at the golden fruit on the tree. He remembered the long days and nights spent traveling far away from home. The strange animal often stayed near him, sharing quiet moments in the shade. Every person in the country knew the story of the brave young prince. Parents told the tale to their children before they went to sleep at night. The bright bird sang sweet songs that echoed through the royal halls. Nothing could ever break the strong bond between the king and his family. The kingdom grew rich and powerful because everyone worked together in peace. Travelers from distant places came just to see the wonderful garden and the bird. The king smiled as he watched his people live in such harmony. The legacy of Prens remained bright for all future generations to come. And the magical garden stayed forever as a wonderful home for the sacred creature.

### C1
In an era when ancient walls guarded the borders of a vast kingdom, a powerful monarch resided within a magnificent stone fortress alongside his three sons. At the heart of the royal courtyard flourished an extraordinary botanical sanctuary, boasting a legendary tree that bore fruit of pure, unblemished gold. Yet, a persistent shadow cast itself over this nocturnal beauty as an unseen thief plundered the radiant harvest night after night. Disturbed by the continuous loss, the sovereign summoned his heirs to defend the precious asset and apprehend the audacious culprit. The elder brothers stepped forward first, eager to prove their worth, but weariness quickly claimed their vigilance as the midnight hour deepened.

Neither sibling possessed the endurance required for the silent vigil, their eyelids surrendering to sleep before the first watch had even concluded. Then came the turn of Prens, the youngest of the three princes, who volunteered to patrol the fragrant paths beneath the canopy of stars. He settled near the trunk of the golden tree, forcing his senses to remain razor-sharp against the heavy, cool breeze of the midnight air. Scarcely had the clock struck the midnight hour when a sudden illumination pierced the gloom of the secluded garden. A magnificent creature descended from the velvet sky, its form radiating a brilliant heat and a blinding, magical light that painted the leaves in amber.

Heart pounding against his ribs, Prens lunged forward with desperate speed to capture the dazzling apparition before it could escape into the clouds. The bird proved too swift for his clumsy grasp, slipping away into the dark expanse like a falling star. Only a single, glowing feather remained in the prince's trembling palm, burning softly with the warmth of a thousand hearths. When dawn broke, the king marvelled at the radiant relic brought to him by his youngest son, feeling a profound surge of pride. Despite his satisfaction with the glowing prize, the monarch confessed that he desired the living creature itself to complete his royal treasury.

Driven by a sense of duty and a longing for adventure, Prens embarked on a perilous quest across uncharted mountains and dense, whispering forests. The journey tested his physical strength and his mental resolve as the miles stretched endlessly before his tired feet. Midway through his travels, an unusual creature crossed his path, offering companionship and unexpected wisdom when the road grew treacherous. This mysterious animal guided the young prince through winding labyrinths of briars and across roaring rivers that threatened to sweep them away. Working in perfect harmony, the companions advanced ever closer to the legendary realm where the luminous bird made its hidden nest.

Every obstacle they encountered was met with unwavering courage and careful attention to the wise advice of the loyal animal companion. Prens learned to listen more than he spoke, discovering that patience and humility were far more potent than any sword or shield. At last, after weeks of relentless travel, they arrived at the secret enclosure where the magnificent bird resided in quiet splendour. Mindful of his guide's strict instructions, the prince approached the glowing sanctuary with deliberate caution and absolute respect. He followed every required rule to the letter, avoiding the traps of haste and arrogance that so often ruined ambitious travellers.

Touched by the youth's sincere reverence, the radiant bird offered no resistance as Prens gently gathered it into his protective custody. That pivotal moment altered the trajectory of the prince's life forever, transforming him from a hesitant boy into a remarkably wise young man. With his precious charge secured, he retraced his steps alongside his faithful animal friend, eager to return to the kingdom of his birth. The journey home felt lighter under the glow of the captive creature, whose gentle song seemed to clear the very air around them. When they finally passed through the grand gates of the castle, a wave of jubilation swept through the stone corridors and royal courtyards.

The king welcomed his youngest son with open arms, gazing at the magnificent bird with tears of absolute wonder in his weary eyes. No longer threatened by midnight thieves, the golden tree flourished more abundantly than ever before under the watchful care of the kingdom. The older brothers observed these events with a quiet realization, finally grasping the true meaning of responsibility and steadfast devotion. They understood that their own failure was rooted in carelessness, and they resolved to support Prens in all his future endeavours. From that memorable day forward, the castle resonated with laughter, music, and the unbreakable unity of a truly reconciled family.

Life within the kingdom entered a peaceful era, untouched by the turbulence and strife that plagued less fortunate neighbouring lands. Prens often strolled through the blossoming garden, admiring the heavy golden fruit that hung undisturbed from the sacred branches. His loyal animal companion remained close by his side, sharing quiet afternoons in the dappled shade of the ancient trees. The story of their grand adventure became a cherished legend, recounted by parents to their children as the evening lamps were lit. Generations grew up hearing the tale of the brave prince who valued friendship and duty far above material wealth or royal privilege.

Travellers from distant provinces arrived daily just to catch a fleeting glimpse of the wondrous garden and the miraculous bird within. The radiant creature filled the royal halls with sweet, harmonious melodies that seemed to chase away every trace of sorrow or doubt. Under the wise guidance of the king and his capable son, the realm prospered economically, socially, and culturally beyond all expectation. Every citizen felt secure within the fortified walls, knowing that justice and harmony governed their daily lives from the mountains to the sea. The profound bond between Prens and his family remained as unshakeable as the ancient stones that formed the foundation of their home.

Time flowed onward like a gentle river, carrying the kingdom through peaceful seasons of abundant harvests and untroubled celebrations. Whenever Prens looked back upon his long and arduous journey, he knew that his success stemmed entirely from collaboration and mutual respect. The strange animal that had aided him so selflessly was honoured above all other creatures in the royal domain. Together, the prince and his companions ensured that the light of the bird would never fade from the heart of their homeland. Thus, the legendary garden endured as an eternal symbol of hope, proving that true courage is always rewarded by destiny.

## st-0206 — Vasilisa the Beautiful
Kaynak: PD-uyarlama

### A1
Vasilisa lives in a small house. She is very kind. Her mother dies. Her new family is bad. They make her work all day. Vasilisa has a special little doll. Her mother gives it to her. The present helps her do work. One night, the family puts out lights. They force Vasilisa to go out.

She goes into the forest. She looks for light from Baba Yaga. Baba Yaga is a woman. Vasilisa is not well. The little present helps her. She walks to a big place. Baba Yaga lives in a moving house. The house stands on the floor. The woman sees Vasilisa. She is very cross.

She says Vasilisa must work. The woman gives her hard work. She must make things right. Vasilisa begins to water eyes. The work is too hard. The little present speaks to her. The doll tells her to stop. The doll does everything. Morning comes. All work is done.

The house is clear. The woman gives her more work. The woman leaves. The doll helps again. Every job is done before night. Baba Yaga is very cross. She cannot change the girl. She gives her a head light. She tells her to leave. Vasilisa takes the head.

She goes back through the forest. She comes home. The head looks at the family. Light burns the bad people. They go away. Vasilisa lives a good life.

### A2
Vasilisa lived in a small house with her family. She was very beautiful and kind. Her mother died. Then her new mother and sisters treated her badly. They made her work all day long. Vasilisa had a special little present. Her mother gave it to her before she passed away. Each time the mother gave her a task, the present helped her.

One dark night, the bad family put out all the fires. They wanted to force Vasilisa to go out. They sent her into the deep forest for fire. She needed to find Baba Yaga. Baba Yaga was a hard person. Vasilisa felt small fear. Her little present told her not to worry. She walked through the dark trees. She reached a different place made of human parts.

Baba Yaga lived in a house on high animal feet. The house moved around the forest. The old woman returned and was not happy. She saw a human child near her door. Baba Yaga said Vasilisa must work or she would be eaten. The bad woman gave her huge lines of food to clean. Vasilisa began to feel bad because the work was too hard.

Then her special little present spoke. It told her to sleep while it did everything. When the morning light came up, all the work was finished. The house was clean. Baba Yaga was surprised, but she let her try another test. The woman gave her more work and went away. Again, the little present helped Vasilisa complete every task.

Baba Yaga became very bad. She could not find any reason to cause bad things to the girl. The woman gave her a head with hot eyes. She told her to leave. Vasilisa took the special head and ran home. Her present helped her through the dark forest. She arrived home.

The light eyes of the head looked at the bad family. The fire from the head killed the bad family. They were gone completely. Vasilisa lived a happy life after that.

### B1
Vasilisa lived in a small house with her family. She was very beautiful and kind. After her mother died, her new family treated her badly. They made her work all day long. However, Vasilisa had a special little present. Her mother gave it to her before she passed away. If the family gave her a difficult task, the present helped her finish it quickly. One dark night, the bad family put out all the fires in the house. They wanted to force Vasilisa to go out. They sent her into the deep forest to find fire from Baba Yaga. Baba Yaga was an old lady living in the woods. Vasilisa was afraid, but her little present told her not to worry. The present said that it would protect her. She walked through the dark trees until she reached a strange bar made of human bone.

Baba Yaga lived in a house that stood on foot legs. The house moved around the forest. When the old lady returned, she was angry to see a child standing near her door. Baba Yaga said that Vasilisa must work for her. The bad lady gave her massive numbers of food to clean. She also gave her terrible rooms to clean before morning. Vasilisa began to cry because the work was too hard for one person to finish. Then her special little present spoke to her. It told her to sleep while it did everything. When the morning sun came up, all the work was completely finished. The house was clean. Baba Yaga was surprised, but she still had to let the girl try another difficult test.

The lady gave her even more work to do. Baba Yaga went away on her flying metal. Again, the little present helped Vasilisa complete every single task before the sun went down. Baba Yaga became very sad. She could not find any reason to hurt the bright girl. The lady finally gave her a head bone with burning eyes to use as a light. She told her to leave. Vasilisa took the terrible head bone and ran back through the dark forest. Her present helped her on the way. When she arrived home, the clear eyes of the bone looked right at the bad family. The bright fire from the bone burned the bad family. They burned until they disappeared completely from the house. After that, Vasilisa lived a glad life. She never had to worry about bad people again.

### B2
Vasilisa lived in a small house with her family. She was very beautiful and kind. After her mother died, her new family members treated her badly. They made her work all day long. However, Vasilisa had a special little toy which her mother gave her before she passed away. Whenever the mother gave her a difficult task, the toy would help her finish it quickly. Vasilisa always kept the toy close to her hand. It brought her comfort during hard times. She loved the little toy because it was a wonderful gift from her dear mother. The days were long and hard, but she stayed strong.

One dark night, the bad family put out all the fires in the house. They wanted to force Vasilisa to go out into the deep forest. They sent her to find fire from Baba Yaga, who was an old woman. Vasilisa was afraid, but her little toy told her not to worry about anything. The toy promised that it would protect her through the dark trees. She walked through the dark trees until she reached a strange fence made of human bones. The forest was very strange, but she did not stop. Her heart beat fast as she moved forward. The little toy whispered words of peace to keep her safe. She knew that she must complete the journey to save herself.

Baba Yaga lived in a house that stood on chicken legs and moved around the forest. When the old woman returned, she was angry to see a human child standing near her door. Baba Yaga said that Vasilisa must work for her or else she would be eaten. The bad woman gave her massive piles of grain to clean. She also gave her terrible rooms to sweep before morning. Baba Yaga thought that the girl could never finish all those difficult jobs in one night. The bad woman laughed loudly and went to sleep. Vasilisa sat alone in the dark room. She looked at the huge piles of work and felt very sad. The night was cold, and the wind blew hard against the walls.

Vasilisa began to cry because the work was too hard for one person to finish. Then her special little toy spoke to her. It told her to sleep while it did everything. When the morning sun came up, all the work was completely finished. The house was clean and quiet. Baba Yaga was surprised, but she still had to let the girl try another difficult test. The old woman watched the child carefully to see if she would fail the next task. She did not believe that the girl could do it again. Vasilisa felt much better after a good night of rest. She knew her little toy was the best friend she could ever have.

The woman gave her even more work to do while she went away on her flying pot. Again, the little toy helped Vasilisa complete every single task before the sun went down. Baba Yaga became very angry because she could not find any reason to hurt the bright girl. The woman finally gave her a head bone with burning eyes to use as a light. She told her to leave the dark house. Baba Yaga wanted the light to test the girl. She wanted to see what would happen when she returned home. Vasilisa took the strange object and prepared to walk back. The forest was still full of shadows, but she was ready. Her power grew with every step she took.

Vasilisa took the strange head bone and hurried back through the dark forest with the help of her toy. When she arrived home, the bright eyes of the head bone looked right at the bad family members. The bright fire from the head bone burned the bad family until they disappeared completely from the house. After that, Vasilisa lived a happy life. She never had to worry about bad people again. She stayed in the small house and shared her days with friends who loved her very much. The toy stayed by her side for many years. Everyone in the local area knew about her kind heart. Peace and joy returned to her home forever. She finally found the happiness that she deserved.

### C1
Vasilisa inhabited a modest, timber-framed cottage alongside her family, her existence defined by an inherent grace and a gentle spirit that endeared her to all who truly knew her. Following the untimely demise of her mother, however, the domestic atmosphere shifted dramatically toward cruelty. Her new stepfamily members treated her with bitter disdain, imposing exhausting chores that occupied every waking hour from dawn until dusk. Yet, amidst the unrelenting toil, Vasilisa possessed a singular solace: a small wooden doll bequeathed to her by her dying mother as a talisman of enduring affection. Whenever her stepmother assigned an impossible task, Vasilisa would quietly feed the doll a morsel of bread, and the miniature figure would animate to finish the labor swiftly.

She kept the small effigy perpetually hidden close to her heart, drawing quiet comfort from its presence during the bleakest moments of her youth. The days stretched endlessly before her, heavy with fatigue and sorrow, but she managed to preserve her inner resilience. She understood that maintaining her composure was her only shield against the constant hostility within her own home. Every gesture of kindness she offered was met with cold indifference, reinforcing her isolation. Despite the harshness of her reality, the memory of her mother sustained her fragile hope through the lengthening shadows.

One particularly moonless night, the hostile household conspired to extinguish every hearth fire within the dwelling, plunging the rooms into absolute darkness. Their malicious intent was to force Vasilisa out into the terrifying expanse of the deep, untamed forest under the pretext of securing embers. They commanded her to seek out Baba Yaga, the notorious and unpredictable old woman whose reputation struck fear into the hearts of villagers. Terror gripped Vasilisa, but her small wooden companion whispered words of reassurance, urging her not to yield to panic. The doll promised to guide her safely through the tangled underbrush and protect her from the unseen perils of the nocturnal woods.

With trembling steps, she ventured into the dense thicket until she encountered a bizarre and menacing fence constructed entirely of human bones. The forest hummed with unnatural vitality, yet she refused to let paralysis overcome her resolve. Her heart hammered against her ribs as she pressed forward, driven by the sheer necessity of survival. The little toy hummed a faint, steadying melody, keeping her nerves intact as the shadows twisted around her path. She knew with absolute certainty that completing this harrowing journey was the only way to preserve her life.

Deep within the labyrinthine woods stood the infamous domicile of Baba Yaga, a peculiar structure perched precariously upon giant chicken legs that shifted autonomously. When the formidable old crone finally returned to her domain, she flared with instant rage upon discovering a human child lingering near her threshold. Baba Yaga declared in a harsh voice that Vasilisa must submit to rigorous servitude or face immediate consumption. The wicked woman assigned towering mounds of mixed grain to be sorted by morning, alongside impossibly filthy chambers demanding thorough sanitation. Convinced that no mortal girl could accomplish such monumental chores in a single night, the crone laughed aloud and retired to her chamber.

Vasilisa remained entirely alone in the cavernous, shadowy room, staring despairingly at the overwhelming mountains of work. The nocturnal air grew intensely cold, and the wind lashed furiously against the exterior walls like an impatient beast. Overwhelmed by the sheer impossibility of the demands, the exhausted girl began to weep softly into her hands. Instantly, her faithful little toy stirred, advising her to rest while it performed the labor on her behalf. Obeying the comforting guidance, Vasilisa drifted into a profound and dreamless sleep.

When the pale light of dawn crept across the wooden floorboards, every single task had been executed with miraculous precision. The entire dwelling was immaculately clean, and a profound silence hung in the air. Baba Yaga emerged with a look of intense astonishment, though she was bound by custom to grant the girl a brief reprieve. The suspicious old woman observed the child with hawk-like eyes, determined to uncover any sign of weakness or deceit. Vasilisa felt remarkably restored after a night of unbroken rest, her confidence bolstered by the unwavering loyalty of her tiny protector.

Before departing once more upon her supernatural flying mortar, the mistress of the hut piled on an even greater burden of labor. True to its nature, the little toy intervened once again, ensuring every assignment was completed long before the sun dipped below the horizon. Baba Yaga returned to find the work flawlessly done, her frustration mounting as she failed to find any legitimate pretext to harm the clever girl. Reluctantly conceding defeat for the moment, the crone finally handed Vasilisa a glowing human skull mounted on a wooden staff to serve as a torch. She commanded the girl to leave immediately, hoping that the terrifying light would test her resilience on the return journey.

Vasilisa grasped the macabre lantern firmly and hurried back through the nocturnal forest, guided once more by the subtle instincts of her toy. As she emerged from the treeline and approached her former home, the luminous eye sockets of the skull flared with an intense, penetrating glare. The brilliant beams swept across the malicious stepfamily members, burning with a fierce and concentrated heat until they vanished completely. Silence reclaimed the small cottage, leaving Vasilisa standing alone amidst the lingering echoes of her turbulent past. She recognized that her trials had finally concluded, and a profound sense of liberation settled over her spirit.

In the aftermath of her victory, Vasilisa established a peaceful existence within the familiar walls of her childhood home. She never had to endure the cruelty of malicious guardians again, choosing instead to surround herself with compassionate and genuine companions. The magical little doll remained faithfully by her side through the passing years, a quiet reminder of maternal love. Word of her remarkable grace and steadfast resilience spread throughout the surrounding region, earning the deep respect of all her neighbors. Tranquility and enduring joy enveloped her household, rewarding her at last with the happiness she so richly deserved.

## st-0207 — The Fool of the World and the Flying Ship

### A1

The man give hard work. The man want them to eat food. The man eat all food. The man want water. The good man bring water.

The man test them again. They stay in a bad room. They stay good all night. The man can no longer say no. The family take the young man. A big party start now. They live good together. They are very glad.

### B1

Finally, the king tested them by telling them to survive inside a hot metal house. The group used their good skills to stay safe and comfortable all night long. After all these trials, the king could no longer say no.

The family finally accepted the young man, and a big party began. The good young man and his true friends lived happily ever after. They have shared many great adventures together since that day. If anyone asks about the story, they will remember the flying ship. Life has become much better for the entire family now. The young man has proven that a good heart can win any battle.
